Result: 在三个基于GQA的VLM上，CHAIRs相对降低4.0%，随机模式控制实验显示几乎无变化。消融实验表明幻觉信号特异于主导QK模式，且主要定位在对称互注意通道中。

Conclusion: QK Product Steering提供了一种简单的替代解码时缓解方法的方案，无需额外数据、微调或推理开销，同时基本保留多模态能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) often generate fluent but visually unsupported descriptions, especially by mentioning objects absent from the image. We propose QK Product Steering, a data-free, training-free, and zero-inference-cost weight edit for reducing object hallucination. The method directly edits the per-head query-key product, the operator that produces pre-softmax attention logits, by suppressing a small number of dominant singular modes in selected middle layers. The edited product is then mapped back to the query weights through a closed-form query-only update while keeping shared key weights fixed, making the edit compatible with grouped-query attention. We further decompose the QK product into symmetric and antisymmetric components to distinguish mutual content-similarity patterns from directional attention patterns. Across three GQA-based VLMs, QK Product Steering achieves an average relative CHAIR$_s$ reduction of $4.0\%$, while matched random-mode controls show negligible change. Interpretability ablations show that the hallucination signal is specific to dominant QK modes and is primarily localized to the symmetric mutual-attention channel. Overall, QK Product Steering offers a simple alternative to decoding-time mitigation, requiring no additional data, fine-tuning, or inference-time overhead while largely preserving general multimodal capability.

</details>


### [118] [CSWinUNETR: Segmentation of Thin Anatomical Structures in Medical Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19824)
*Junho Moon,Haejun Chung,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CSWinUNETR是一个通用的2D/3D薄结构分割骨干网络，通过十字形条带自注意力、循环移位、细节增强多尺度自注意力和稀疏控制动态蛇形卷积，在视网膜血管、脑脉管系统和面部皱纹等基准上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卷积和Transformer模型在分割低对比度、易断裂、类别严重不平衡的薄结构（如视网膜血管、脑脉管、面部皱纹）时，常产生碎片化预测且无法恢复细小的分支结构。

Method: 1) 十字形条带自注意力(Cross-shaped stripe self-attention)建模长程主轴上下文；2) 循环移位(Cyclic shifts)增强条带间的信息交换；3) 细节增强多尺度自注意力模块(Detail-enhanced multi-scale self-attention)聚合多分辨率上下文特征；4) 稀疏控制动态蛇形卷积(Sparse-control dynamic snake convolution)从稀疏预测控制点重建密集曲线核以贴合弯曲几何结构。

Result: 在眼科、神经血管成像和皮肤科四个基准数据集上，CSWinUNETR无需任务特定的后处理或拓扑感知损失函数，持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: CSWinUNETR作为通用骨干网络，有效解决了薄结构分割中的断裂和细分支缺失问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of thin, tortuous anatomical structures, such as retinal vessels, cerebral vasculature, and facial wrinkles, remains challenging due to low contrast, frequent discontinuities, and severe class imbalance. Although recent convolutional and Transformer-based models have improved performance, they often yield fragmented predictions and fail to recover fine branches. We propose CSWinUNETR, a general-purpose backbone for 2D and 3D thin-structure segmentation. It employs cross-shaped stripe self-attention to model long-range principal-axis context and incorporates cyclic shifts to enhance information exchange across stripes. To better preserve fine-grained details, we further introduce a detail-enhanced multi-scale self-attention module that aggregates contextual features from multi-resolution representations. In addition, we propose sparse-control dynamic snake convolution, which reconstructs reliable dense curvilinear kernels from sparsely predicted control points to better follow tortuous geometry. Extensive experiments on four benchmarks across ophthalmology, neurovascular imaging, and dermatology demonstrate that CSWinUNETR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods without task-specific post-processing or topology-aware losses. The code is available at https://github.com/labhai/CSWinUNETR.

</details>


### [119] [InfantFace: Detecting infant faces in neonatal clinical environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20449)
*Abdullah Bin-Obaid,Maria M. Cobo,Rebeccah Slater,Lionel Tarassenko,Mauricio Villarroel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于YOLOv11m的单阶段新生儿面部检测模型，在多个公开数据集预训练后在新生儿临床数据集上微调，AP50从0.87提升至0.96，显著优于现有通用人脸检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿临床环境中面部检测面临背景杂乱、光照变化、面部遮挡等挑战，而准确的面部定位是基于视频的非接触式评估（如疼痛表情分析、心肺信号提取等）的首要步骤。

Method: 采用YOLOv11m单阶段检测器，先在VGGFace2、CelebA、FDDB、WIDER FACE等公开数据集上训练，再在包含228个视频、114次录制、113名独立婴儿的新生儿研究数据集上进行领域微调。

Result: 预训练模型在新生儿数据集上AP50达0.87，优于三种最先进通用人脸检测器；经临床领域微调后AP50提升至0.96。

Conclusion: 针对新生儿临床环境定制的面部检测模型效果显著，但缺乏公开新生儿数据集仍是领域进展的主要瓶颈，需在隐私保护和伦理标准下优先构建此类数据集。

Abstract: Reliable localisation of the neonatal face is the first step for several video-camera based non-contact assessments such as pain and distress related facial expression analysis, pain scoring, cardiorespiratory signal extraction and cessation of breathing alerts. However, major challenges persist in neonatal clinical environments. Cluttered backgrounds, illumination changes and poor lighting conditions can reduce the accuracy of face detection models. Clinical interventions, monitoring equipment and, in some cases, medical devices can obstruct the face, making visual assessment difficult. We propose a one-stage YOLOv11m-based model tailored for face detection of infants in neonatal clinical environments. We combined multiple publicly available datasets (VGGFace2, CelebA, FDDB, WIDER FACE) to train and evaluate our proposed model. We then fine-tuned our model on a neonatal research dataset involving 228 videos from 114 recording sessions of 113 independent infants. Before fine-tuning, our model achieved an AP50 of 0.87, surpassing the performance of three state-of-the-art general face detectors. Performance improved further to an AP50 of 0.96 after clinical-domain adaptation. Evaluating face detection performance across different datasets remains a challenge due to the lack of publicly available neonatal datasets. Prioritising the creation of such datasets, while upholding appropriate privacy safeguards and ethical standards in their creation and use, would greatly support further progress in this field.

</details>


### [120] [PCFootprint: A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark for Vectorized Building Footprint Extraction from Aerial LiDAR Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20455)
*Haoyuan Shen,Kuihao Wang,Ruisheng Wang,Yujun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了PCFootprint，首个大规模机载激光扫描点云建筑物足迹提取公开数据集，包含33,000个图块及3,000个跨域测试集，并建立了主流方法的基准评估，揭示了复杂地理环境下的高类内方差、数据不平衡和噪声等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于光学影像的建筑物足迹提取方法易受遮挡、透视畸变和残余地形位移影响，导致提取不完整或错位，且缺乏显式高程信息限制了其在细节层次（LOD）建筑建模中的直接应用。因此，亟需一个基于激光扫描点云的大规模公开数据集来推动该领域研究。

Method: PCFootprint数据集包含33,000个128m×128m的图块，源自爱沙尼亚土地与空间发展委员会数据，覆盖城乡多样化景观，并包含3,000个跨域测试集以评估泛化能力。数据集提供了与点云系统对齐的矢量化足迹，并建立了主流方法的基准评测。

Result: 实验结果表明，现有主流方法在PCFootprint上面临显著挑战，包括高类内方差、数据不平衡以及复杂地理环境下的噪声问题。

Conclusion: PCFootprint数据集将推动建筑物建模、城市场景理解和地理空间分析等领域的未来研究，并已公开提供。

Abstract: Building footprint extraction is a fundamental task in photogrammetry, remote sensing, and computer vision. Recent image-based methods have achieved remarkable progress in extracting vectorized footprints from high-resolution optical imagery. However, optical imagery inherently susceptible to occlusions, perspective distortions, and residual relief displacement, yielding incomplete or misaligned footprint extraction. Furthermore, the lack of explicit elevation information limits its direct applicability to Level of Detail building modeling. In this paper, we present PCFootprint, the first large-scale public dataset for footprint extraction from airborne laser scanning point clouds. PCFootprint comprises \num{33000} tiles derived from the Estonian Land and Spatial Development Board, covering diverse urban and rural landscapes. Each tile spans \qtyproduct{128 x 128}{\m} with systematically aligned vectorized footprints aligned to point clouds. The dataset includes a \num{3000} tiles cross-domain test set for evaluating generalization across geographic regions. We establish comprehensive benchmarks by evaluating mainstream methods. Experimental results reveal significant challenges including high intra-class variance, data imbalance, and noise across complex geospatial environments. We believe PCFootprint will advance future research in building modeling, urban scene understanding, and geospatial analysis. The PCFootprint dataset is publicly available at \url{https://huggingface.co/datasets/Haoyuan-Shen/PCFootprint}.

</details>


### [121] [Scalable Training of Spatially Grounded 2D Vision-Language Models for Radiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20477)
*Yusuf Salcan,Simon Ging,Robin Schirrmeister,Philipp Arnold,Elmar Kotter,Behzad Bozorgtabar,Thomas Brox*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了大规模双语放射学视觉语言数据集RefRad2D（120万CT/MR图像-文本对），并提出联合模型RadGrounder，可同时完成报告生成、视觉问答和空间定位（边界框检测/分割），且添加定位监督不影响语言质量，在外部VQA基准上取得竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学视觉语言模型缺乏空间定位能力，且训练数据需要昂贵的手工标注。本文旨在利用临床实践中自动生成的数据，训练能同时执行多种任务（报告生成、VQA、空间定位）的VLM，无需人工空间标注。

Method: 1) 构建RefRad2D数据集：从临床实践中获取120万CT/MR图像-文本对（德/英双语），通过LLM自动策展和自动分割生成任务特定的VQA和空间接地子集。2) 训练RadGrounder模型：联合执行报告生成、视觉问答和空间接地（边界框检测或分割）。

Result: 1) 在Slake和VQA-RAD外部VQA基准上取得与专用医学VLM竞争的结果。2) 添加临床数据到训练混合中比仅在下游数据集上微调提升了开放VQA性能，证明了数据集的迁移性。3) 添加接地监督不会降低语言质量，可在不影响VQA性能的情况下实现空间可验证输出。

Conclusion: 本文证明了大规模自动生成的放射学VLM数据无需手工空间标注即可有效训练多任务模型，且空间定位与语言质量可兼得，为临床可信赖的放射学AI提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We study how to train visually grounded vision-language models (VLMs) for radiology without manual spatial annotations. We introduce RefRad2D, a large-scale bilingual (German/English) dataset of 1.2M CT and MR image-text pairs derived from clinical practice, with task-specific VQA and spatial grounding subsets generated automatically via LLM-based curation and automated segmentation. Trained on this data, our model RadGrounder jointly performs report generation, visual question answering, and spatial grounding via bounding-box detection or segmentation. On external VQA benchmarks (Slake, VQA-RAD), RadGrounder achieves competitive results with specialized medical VLMs. Adding our clinical data to the training mixture improves open-ended VQA over fine-tuning on the downstream datasets alone, showing the transferability of our dataset. Crucially, adding grounding supervision does not degrade language quality, enabling spatially verifiable outputs at no cost to VQA performance.

</details>


### [122] [How Fragile Are Training-Free AI-Generated Image Detectors? A Controlled Audit of Score Direction, Preprocessing, and Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20488)
*Jingwen Zhou,Mingzhe Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对两种免训练的AI生成图像检测方法（AEROBLADE风格的重建分数和RIGID风格的噪声扰动特征相似度分数）进行了系统审计，在包含7种生成器和JPEG压缩的1500图像基准上发现三个警示性发现：实现细节伪装成方法差异、分数方向取决于超参数而非方法本身、数据集格式偏差夸大了鲁棒性声明。审计还发现不同分数有互补的失败集，但简单的z-score融合无法超越最佳单一分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练AI生成图像检测器声称无需分类器训练且可跨生成器部署，但其报告的性能指标缺乏统一可控协议的对比，导致不同方法之间的实际表现差异被实现细节和评估偏差所混淆。因此需要在一个标准化协议下对代表性方法进行严格审计。

Method: 在统一的1500图像GenImage衍生基准上，对两种免训练分数（AEROBLADE风格的自编码器重建分数和RIGID风格的噪声扰动特征相似度分数）以及一个朴素的特征kNN控制方法进行审计。基准涵盖7种生成器和JPEG压缩（质量70和50）。系统性地比较了不同实现选择（如LPIPS骨干网络、预处理方式）、超参数（噪声水平sigma）以及数据集格式偏差对AUROC指标的影响。

Result: (i) 实现细节影响巨大：LPIPS骨干从AlexNet换为VGG-16使AUROC提升0.085，预处理方式（resize-to-512 vs. 原始分辨率）导致每个生成器结论翻转达0.38 AUROC。(ii) 分数方向由超参数决定：RIGID风格分数在sigma=0.05时对SD1.5和Wukong生成器倒置（AUROC<0.5），sigma=0.01时恢复>0.5，sigma=0.3时崩溃至0.15。(iii) 数据集格式偏差：未经统一重编码时JPEG-50下的AUROC甚至超过干净条件，偏差校正后异常仅局限于BigGAN。不同分数的失败集互补，但简单z-score融合未超越最佳单一分数。

Conclusion: 免训练检测器的性能评估高度依赖于实现细节、超参数选择和数据集预处理，现有报告的数字不可直接比较。方法之间具有互补性但需要方向感知的融合策略才能有效利用。建议社区建立统一的评估协议和偏差校正标准。

Abstract: Training-free detectors of AI-generated images promise generator-agnostic deployment without classifier training, yet their reported numbers are rarely compared under a single controlled protocol. We audit two representative training-free scores -- an autoencoder-reconstruction score (AEROBLADE-style) and a noise-perturbation feature-similarity score (RIGID-style) -- plus a naive feature-kNN control, on a common 1,500-image GenImage-derived benchmark spanning seven generators and JPEG compression at quality 70 and 50. The audit yields three cautionary findings. (i) Implementation details masquerade as method differences: replacing the LPIPS backbone (AlexNet -> VGG-16) changes overall AUROC by +0.085, and switching between resize-to-512 and native-resolution preprocessing flips per-generator conclusions by up to 0.38 AUROC. (ii) Score direction is not a property of the method but of its hyperparameters: the RIGID-style score is inverted (AUROC < 0.5) on SD1.5 and Wukong at noise level sigma=0.05, recovers to >0.5 for every generator at sigma=0.01, and collapses to 0.15 at sigma=0.3. (iii) Dataset format bias inflates robustness claims: without unified re-encoding, AUROC under JPEG-50 exceeds the clean condition for the AlexNet-backbone reconstruction score; after bias correction the residual anomaly localizes to a single generator (BigGAN). The audited scores have complementary per-generator failure sets, but naive z-score fusion does not beat the best single score, indicating that exploiting complementarity requires direction-aware combination.

</details>


### [123] [FreeStyle: Free Control of Style-Content Dual-Reference Generation from Community LoRA Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20506)
*Jinghong Lan,Wei Cheng,Yunuo Chen,Ziqi Ye,Peng Xing,Yixiao Fang,Rui Wang,Yufeng Yang,Xuanyang Zhang,Xianfang Zeng,Difan Zou,Gang Yu,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FreeStyle，一种基于社区LoRA挖掘的可扩展双参考图像生成框架。通过构建大规模风格-内容三元组数据，并采用两阶段课程学习（注意力级富集约束+频率感知RoPE调制）解决内容泄漏问题，在风格对齐、内容保持和泄漏抑制上取得强平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风格-内容双参考生成面临三个挑战：1) 模型需平衡内容保真度、风格对齐和指令遵循，同时避免风格参考的语义泄漏；2) 缺乏大规模、内容-风格分离清晰且覆盖长尾风格的三元组数据。

Method: 1) 将社区LoRA视为风格和内容的组合锚点，设计生成-过滤流水线构建大规模三元组；2) 两阶段课程学习：风格迁移阶段采用注意力级富集约束抑制风格参考泄漏；双参考阶段采用频率感知RoPE调制策略处理基于位置对应的泄漏；3) 提出新基准，包括风格不变内容对齐分数(CAS)和基于VLM的校准拒绝分数。

Result: 实验表明模型在风格对齐、内容保持和泄漏抑制之间取得了强平衡。

Conclusion: FreeStyle通过社区LoRA挖掘和两阶段解耦机制有效解决了双参考生成中的内容泄漏问题，实现了可扩展的高质量风格-内容合成。

Abstract: Style-content dual-reference generation aims to synthesize an image that preserves the structure and semantics of a content reference while adopting the style of a separate style reference.Despite recent progress, this setting remains challenging because models must balance content fidelity, style alignment, and instruction following avoiding semantic leakage from the style reference.A key bottleneck is the lack of large-scale triplet data with clean content-style separation and broad long-tail style coverage.In this work, we propose FreeStyle, a scalable dual-reference generation framework based on community LoRA mining.We treat community LoRAs as compositional anchors for style and content, and design a rigorous generation and filtering pipeline to construct large-scale Style-Reference and Content-Reference triplets across multiple base models.To address content leakage, we adopt a two-stage curriculum with stage-specific disentanglement mechanisms: an attention-level enrichment constraint that suppresses style-reference leakage in the style-transfer stage, and a frequency-aware RoPE modulation strategy that targets positional-correspondence-based leakage in the harder dual-reference stage.We also introduce a benchmark covering both style-reference and dual-reference generation, with evaluations on style similarity, content preservation, aesthetics, instruction following, and leakage rejection. The benchmark incorporates a style-invariant Content Alignment Score (CAS) and introduces a calibrated VLM-based Rejection Score for evaluating generation reliability and leakage suppression.Extensive experiments show that our model achieves a strong balance among style alignment, content preservation, and leakage suppression.

</details>


### [124] [S-Agent: Spatial Tool-Use Elicits Reasoning for Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20515)
*Yalun Dai,Hao Li,Shulin Tian,Runmao Yao,Yuhao Dong,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Fangfu Liu,Baoliang Tian,Dingwen Zhang,Tao Wang,Kim-Hui Yap,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出S-Agent，一种空间工具使用智能体范式，通过将空间推理建模为时空证据积累过程，利用VLM作为语义规划器，配合层次化空间工具和时序记忆机制，实现对连续多视角图像和视频的空间理解与推理。实验表明该方法无需训练即可提升各类VLM性能，通过SFT构建的S-Agent-8B在空间推理任务上显著超越同规模基线，媲美GPT-5.4等闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM和工具增强智能体主要处理静态、无状态的单帧视觉观察，缺乏对连续演化的3D世界进行空间推理的能力，无法实现真正的空间智能。

Method: 提出S-Agent范式：VLM作为语义规划器决策所需证据；层次化空间工具（2D目标检测→3D几何证据提升→高层空间知识聚合）实现空间理解；时序记忆机制（场景记忆+智能体记忆）实现跨帧和跨推理步骤的证据整合。

Result: 在多视角和视频空间推理基准上，S-Agent以无需训练的方式一致提升各类VLM性能；基于S-Agent生成的空间轨迹数据S-300K进行SFT得到的S-Agent-8B显著超越同规模基线，性能媲美GPT-5.4和Gemini 3等闭源模型。

Conclusion: 将空间推理从孤立帧级预测重塑为场景级时空证据积累范式，能够有效提升VLM的空间智能，且小模型可通过蒸馏获得接近闭源大模型的性能。

Abstract: Real-world spatial intelligence requires reasoning over a continuous and evolving 3D world, yet existing VLMs and tool-augmented agents largely remain tied to static, stateless inference from isolated visual observations. We introduce \textbf{\textsc{S-Agent}}, a spatial tool-use agentic paradigm for understanding and reasoning over continuous multi-view images and videos. By formulating spatial reasoning as spatio-temporal evidence accumulation rather than isolated frame-level prediction, \textsc{S-Agent} reshapes spatial perception into scene-centric understanding beyond frame-centric recognition. Specifically, \textsc{S-Agent} casts the VLM as a semantic planner that decides what evidence is needed, while a hierarchy of spatial tools and experts grounds objects in 2D, lifts them into 3D geometric evidence, and aggregates this evidence into high-level spatial knowledge (\textit{e.g.}, counting, measurement, orientation, and relative position). Additionally, a temporal memory mechanism, including Scene Memory for maintaining the evolving scene state and Agent Memory for accumulating reasoning context, enables evidence integration across frames and reasoning steps. Comprehensive experiments on multi-view and video spatial reasoning benchmarks show that \textsc{S-Agent} consistently improves both open-source and closed-source VLMs in a training-free manner. Beyond inference-time augmentation, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on \textsc{S-Agent}-generated spatial trajectories \textsc{S-300K} yields \textsc{S-Agent-8B}, a compact spatial agent that significantly surpasses similar-scale baselines (e.g., Qwen3-VL-8B) and performs comparably to advanced closed-source models (e.g., GPT-5.4 and Gemini 3).

</details>


### [125] [HumanScale: Egocentric Human Video Can Outperform Real-Robot Data for Embodied Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20521)
*Juncheng Ma,Jianxin Bi,Yufan Deng,Xuanran Zhai,Kewei Zhang,Ye Huang,Bo Liang,Shukai Gong,Jiankai Tu,Xiaotian Tang,Jiaxin Li,Kaiqi Chen,Duomin Wang,Yuqi Wang,Bingyi Kang,Eric Huang,Zhiyang Dou,Zhen Dong,Enze Xie,Wojciech Matusik,Tat-Seng Chua,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了以自我为中心的人类视频和远程操作的真实机器人轨迹作为具身基础模型预训练数据源的效果，发现经过精心设计的过滤和标注流程后，自我中心数据不仅可行，而且性能更优：在真实机器人动作预测的验证损失降低24%，分布内和分布外任务执行成功率分别提升52.5%和90%。这验证了一种可扩展的具身基础模型范式：先用自我中心人类视频预训练学习多样化世界表征，再用少量标注真实机器人数据适配动作空间对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程操作的真实机器人轨迹虽因精确动作监督和具身对齐成为主要预训练数据源，但其采集成本高、获取困难、行为和环境多样性低，限制了数据规模扩展。相比之下，自我中心人类视频具有可扩展、成本低、多样性高的优势，但其有效性尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 采用系统对比研究，在固定的后训练和验证协议下，比较自我中心人类视频与远程操作真实机器人轨迹作为预训练数据源的效果。对自我中心数据设计了专门的过滤和标注流程，在相同数据量下进行预训练，评估真实机器人动作预测的验证损失以及分布内/分布外任务执行成功率。

Result: 相同预训练数据量下，基于自我中心数据预训练的模型在真实机器人动作预测验证损失降低24%，分布内任务成功率提升52.5%，分布外任务成功率提升90%。

Conclusion: 验证了一种可扩展的具身基础模型范式：首先使用自我中心人类视频进行预训练以学习多样化的世界表征，然后使用少量标注的真实机器人数据进行适配以实现动作空间对齐。该研究鼓励更广泛地探索自我中心数据，并在昂贵的机器人数据采集之前提供数据质量评估指导。

Abstract: Embodied foundation models are expected to benefit from data scaling like large language models, but face a much tighter data bottleneck. Teleoperated real-robot trajectories remain the dominant pretraining source due to their precise action supervision and embodiment alignment, yet their scalability is limited by high collection cost, acquisition difficulty, and low behavioral and environmental diversity. These limitations have sparked interest in egocentric human video as a scalable, substantially lower-cost, and more diverse alternative for embodied model pretraining. However, its effectiveness compared to teleoperated real-robot data remains underexplored. To address this question, we conduct a systematic study comparing egocentric human video and teleoperated real-robot trajectories as pretraining data sources for embodied foundation models, under fixed post-training and validation protocols. Surprisingly, we find that egocentric data, when processed through a carefully designed filtering and labeling pipeline, is not merely a viable substitute for model pretraining but can lead to superior performance. With the same amount of pretraining data, models pretrained on egocentric data achieve a 24% lower validation loss on real-robot action prediction, as well as 52.5% and 90% higher success rates on in-distribution and out-of-distribution real-robot task execution, respectively. This finding verifies a scalable paradigm for embodied foundation models: pretrain on egocentric human video to learn diverse world representations, then adapt with a small amount of labeled real-robot data for action-space alignment. We hope this study encourages broader exploration of egocentric data and offers guidance for data quality assessment before costly robot data collection.

</details>


### [126] [SARLO-80: Worldwide Slant SAR Language Optic Dataset 80cm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20523)
*Solène Debuysère,Nicolas Trouvé,Nathan Letheule,Elise Colin,Georgia Channing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了一个名为SARLO-80的大规模VHR SAR-光学-文本三模态数据集，基于Umbra卫星SICD格式数据构建，包含119,566个三元组（复值/幅度SAR斜距图像、对齐的光学图像、自然语言描述），覆盖72个国家257个地点，并公开了预处理代码和基准测试方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAR-光学数据集多基于低分辨率、仅强度的GRD产品，未能保留复值SAR测量和原生获取几何信息，限制了物理上有依据的多模态学习。同时，结合VHR SAR SLC、对齐光学图像和自然语言描述的大规模公开数据集仍然缺乏。

Method: 从约2,500景Umbra聚束模式SICD数据出发，通过带限FFT重采样将所有SAR数据标准化到80cm斜距网格，分块为1024×1024切片；为每块SAR切片检索高分辨率光学图像，利用局部坐标对应关系进行像素级对齐；生成三种长度（短/中/长）的描述文本，形成119,566个三模态三元组。

Result: 构建了包含119,566个SAR-光学-文本三元组的VHR数据集（20cm-2m原生分辨率），覆盖72个国家257个地点，包含多种地类和基础设施类型，并提供了固定的训练/验证/测试划分。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了VHR SAR SLC与光学、文本对齐的大规模公开数据集的空白，能够支持原生SAR几何下的跨模态检索和条件生成等多模态对齐任务的可重复基准研究。

Abstract: Multimodal foundation models have advanced rapidly thanks to large optical benchmarks, but comparable resources for synthetic aperture radar (SAR) remain limited. Existing SAR--optical datasets largely rely on low-resolution, intensity-only Ground Range Detected~(GRD) products and do not preserve complex-valued SAR measurements or native acquisition geometry, which restricts physically grounded multimodal learning. In particular, large-scale public datasets combining very-high-resolution (VHR) SAR SLC, aligned optical imagery, and natural-language descriptions are still lacking. We present a VHR SAR--optical--text dataset built from open-access Umbra spotlight acquisitions distributed as Sensor Independent Complex Data (SICD). From around 2,500 worldwide scenes (VV/HH, 20cm--2m native resolution), we standardize all SAR data to an 80cm slant-range grid via band-limited FFT resampling and tile the imagery into 1024 by 1024 patches. For each SAR patch, we retrieve a high-resolution optical tile and warp it into the SAR grid using local coordinate correspondences for local pixel-level alignment. We further generate three caption variants (SHORT/MID/LONG) per sample to support vision--language training and evaluation. Our dataset contains 119,566 triplets (complex and amplitude slant-range SAR patch, aligned optical patch, natural-language description) covering 257 locations across 72 countries and a broad range of land types and infrastructures. We release fixed train/validation/test splits and the full preprocessing and baseline code to enable reproducible benchmarks for multimodal alignment on cross-modal retrieval and conditional generation in native SAR geometry. The dataset is publicly available on the Hugging Face Hub at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ONERA/SARLO-80.

</details>


### [127] [VisDom: Sparse Novel View Synthesis with Visible Domain Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20531)
*Mariia Gladkova*,Tarun Yenamandra*,Edmond Boyer,Robert Maier,Tony Tung,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisDom是一种无需学习的几何约束，通过多视图可见性阈值对经典视觉壳重建进行增强，在稀疏视角新视图合成中显著减少伪影并提升几何一致性，可无缝集成到NeRF和3DGS管线中，仅需4张输入图像即可实现高质量重建，且不引入任何可学习参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于NeRF和3DGS的方法在密集视角下表现良好，但在稀疏视角下容易过拟合，产生漂浮伪影和不一致的几何结构。虽然轮廓一致性被用作正则化项，但轮廓一致区域可能超出真实物体几何边界，约束仍然不足。因此需要一种更强、更简单的几何先验来缓解稀疏视角下的3D歧义性问题。

Method: 定义可见域（visible domain）为被至少K个视角观察到的3D空间子集，将其作为额外的滤波准则叠加在标准轮廓重建之上。在隐式（NeRF）和显式（3DGS）管线中，分别通过限制体积采样和引导高斯点放置来集成该约束。该方法无需学习，仅依赖二值轮廓图。

Result: 在三个挑战性数据集上，稀疏视角NVS取得一致改进，仅需4张输入图像即可实现高质量物体级重建。在Omni3D和MipNeRF360数据集上，VisDom叠加于GaussianObject之上进一步提升了性能，且在22倍更低的训练成本下达到或超越了基线水平。

Conclusion: VisDom是一种简单、领域无关、无需学习的几何约束，可作为现有稀疏视角NVS方法的有效补充，显著提升几何质量并降低训练开销。

Abstract: Sparse novel view synthesis (NVS) remains challenging due to the ambiguity of recovering 3D geometry from few input views. While NeRF- and Gaussian Splatting (GS)-based methods perform well with dense supervision, they often overfit in sparse settings, producing floating artifacts and inconsistent geometry. Silhouette consistency is commonly used as a regularizer, but it remains insufficient, as silhouette-consistent regions can extend beyond the true object geometry. We introduce VisDom, a learning-free geometric constraint that augments classical carving-based visual hull reconstruction by enforcing a minimum multi-view visibility requirement. Specifically, we define a visible domain as the subset of 3D space observed by at least $K$ views and use it as an additional filtering criterion on top of standard silhouette-based reconstruction. This provides a stronger spatial prior in sparse-view settings. We integrate VisDom into both implicit (NeRF) and explicit (GS) pipelines by restricting volumetric sampling and guiding Gaussian placement during optimization. Experiments on three challenging datasets show consistent improvements in sparse-view NVS, enabling high-quality object-centric reconstruction from as few as four input images. Our method is domain-agnostic, requires only silhouettes, and introduces no learned parameters, making it a simple complement to existing approaches. Applying VisDom on top of GaussianObject further improves performance on Omni3D and MipNeRF360, while matching or surpassing it at 22 $\times$ lower training cost.

</details>


### [128] [The FID Lottery: Quantifying Hidden Randomness in Generative-Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20536)
*Nicolas Dufour,Alexei A. Efros,Patrick Pérez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了FID作为随机变量的可重复性，发现在ImageNet 256x256上训练SiT网络时，重新训练导致的FID波动（3.2倍）远大于重新采样，且增大计算量或模型规模几乎不缩减变异系数（1-2%）。建议采用每单元最优引导、报告多个训练种子的误差棒，并将低于~1.3% CoV的FID差距视为不确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FID是图像生成的黄金标准，但大多数论文仅报告单个模型单次采样的单一数值，其可重复性未得到系统量化。

Method: 将FID视为训练种子和生成种子两轴上的随机变量，在数百个SiT网络（class-conditional ImageNet 256x256）上直接测量其方差，分析重训练与重采样的差异及其驱动因素。

Result: (a) 重训练导致的FID变化是重采样的3.2倍；(b) 该差距由随机初始化、数据顺序和流匹配损失中的每步高斯噪声驱动；(c) 增大计算量或模型规模几乎不改变1-2%的CoV；(d) 每单元CFG调优减半方差但打乱最优种子排序，幸运训练种子可用2x更少计算达到相同FID。

Conclusion: 建议新的FID评估协议：在每单元最优引导下评估，将低于~1.3% CoV的FID差距视为不确定，报告多个训练种子的误差棒而非单一FID数值。

Abstract: The Frechet Inception Distance (FID) is the de facto arbiter of image generation, yet most papers report just a single number from a single trained model using a single sampling seed. How reproducible is that number if we retrain the model, or merely resample from it? In this paper, we treat FID as a random variable on a two-axis panel of training and generation seeds, and measure its variance directly on several hundred SiT networks trained on class-conditional ImageNet 256x256. We report surprising findings: (a) Retraining the model using the same recipe with a different seed moves FID 3.2x more (in Inception feature space) than redrawing samples from a fixed network. (b) That gap is driven by three factors: random initialisation, data ordering, and the per-step Gaussian noise of the flow-matching loss. (c) Increasing compute or model size barely tightens the spread, holding the FID coefficient of variation (CoV) inside a 1-2% band. (d) Per-cell classifier-free-guidance tuning halves the spread but reshuffles which seeds work best, and a lucky training seed reaches the same FID with up to 2x less compute than an unlucky one. Based on these findings, we recommend a new FID evaluation protocol: evaluate under per-cell optimal guidance, treat any FID gap below the empirically measured ~1.3% CoV as inconclusive, and report an error bar over several training seeds rather than a single FID number.

</details>


### [129] [ROSE: Benchmarking the Perception-to-Action Gap in Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19965)
*Yihao Wang,Zijian He,Jie Ren,Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ROSE基准测试，通过固定视觉场景但改变区域约束和符号输出要求，评估多模态大语言模型能否将相同的视觉证据转化为不同任务上下文所需的特定动作。实验发现，9个MLLMs从计数任务到区域条件动作的性能平均下降高达44.5个百分点，而人类准确率达98.8%，揭示了模型在上下文依赖的动作推理中存在显著瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在处理视觉信息时，面对同一场景下不同任务上下文可能要求不同动作的情况，模型能否可靠地将相同的视觉证据转化为当前上下文所需的动作尚不清楚，因此需要系统性地评估这一问题。

Method: 提出ROSE基准，包含耦合计数和坐标动作任务，固定视觉场景不变，通过变化区域约束和符号输出要求，测试模型能否推断隐式多数参考并在变化上下文中基于细粒度视觉证据执行动作。采用配对场景和区域控制、全局点击和匹配局部对照等分析手段。

Result: 在9个近期MLLMs上，从计数导向任务到区域条件动作任务的性能下降高达44.5个百分点，而人类表现为98.8%。即使在模型正确计数的配对场景和区域上，该差距仍然存在。坐标定位只能解释部分性能损失，揭示了将共享视觉证据转化为上下文特定动作中存在的、依赖于模型的独特瓶颈。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs在上下文感知的动作推理方面存在显著缺陷，无法可靠地将相同的视觉证据转化为不同任务上下文所需的特定动作，这构成了一个区别于基础视觉定位能力的独特瓶颈。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly expected to act on visual information, yet the same scene may require different actions under different task contexts. How reliably can a model turn the same visual evidence into the action required by the current context? To answer this question, we introduce \textsc{ROSE} (\textbf{R}eference-conditioned \textbf{O}ddity and \textbf{S}ymbolic \textbf{E}xecution), a controlled benchmark that holds the visual scene fixed while varying region constraints and required symbolic outputs. Through coupled counting and coordinate-action tasks, \textsc{ROSE} tests whether models can infer an implicit majority reference and act on the resulting fine-grained visual evidence under changing contexts. Across nine recent MLLMs, performance drops by as much as 44.5 percentage points from counting-oriented tasks to region-conditioned action, despite 98.8\% human performance. The gap persists on paired scenes and regions for which the same model returns the correct count, while global-click and matched local controls show that coordinate grounding explains only part of the loss, revealing a distinct, model-dependent bottleneck in turning shared visual evidence into context-specific actions.

</details>


### [130] [CalTennis: Large Multi-View Tennis Video Dataset and Benchmark of Monocular-to-3D Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20542)
*Ilona Demler,Xinran Xie,Blake Werner,Anna Szczuka,Pietro Perona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CalTennis是一个包含1100万帧（51小时）网球运动视频的大型基准数据集，用于评估野外单目到3D姿态估计。它比现有数据集大10倍，提供同步多视角记录，并提出了新指标来揭示深度估计和脚部接触等失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有野外人体运动视频数据集规模有限，缺乏大规模、多视角同步记录的专业运动数据，难以有效评估和推动单目到3D姿态估计算法的发展。

Method: 收集40名网球运动员的2-6个同步摄像头60Hz录制视频（超1100万帧），采用标准化协议实现无需专业设备的自动化标定与同步，并提出footwork和stability两个新评价指标。

Result: 现有最先进方法在3D关节角度恢复上表现准确，但在深度估计和足部接触一致性上普遍存在困难；新指标暴露了此前未被充分探索的失败模式。

Conclusion: CalTennis为评估和提升野外姿态估计提供了大规模基准，指出了深度估计和足部接触等具体改进方向。

Abstract: The Caltech Tennis Dataset (CalTennis) is a large-scale video benchmark for evaluating monocular-to-3D pose estimation in the wild. CalTennis comprises over 11 million frames (51 hours) of tennis practice and match play from 40 players, captured with 2-6 synchronized cameras at 60 Hz. It is 10 times larger than existing in-the-wild human motion video datasets and 3 times larger than existing MOCAP-ground-truthed datasets, and it is the first large-scale benchmark to provide synchronized multi-view recordings of expert athletic motion. The multi-view setup enables inexpensive, label-free evaluation of monocular-to-3D pose estimation algorithms. We describe a simple, standardized protocol that enables data collection without specialized equipment or expertise, along with fully automated video calibration and synchronization. Benchmarking state-of-the-art monocular-to-3D pose methods on CalTennis, we find that while 3D joint angle recovery is now quite accurate, all models struggle to estimate depth and foot contact consistently. We further propose two novel performance metrics, footwork and stability, as well as qualitatively study body shape inconsistency. These metrics expose previously underexplored failure modes and point to concrete opportunities for improvement in pose estimation and action analysis.

</details>


### [131] [PU-UNet: Stable Multiplicative Interactions for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20035)
*Ziyuan Li,Osamah Sufyan,Uwe Jaekel,Babette Dellen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PU-UNet通过在U-Net的低分辨率阶段引入稳定的乘积单元残差块，实现了显式乘法特征交互，在三个医学图像分割数据集上取得了优于基线Residual U-Net的性能，且参数量和计算开销几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密集预测网络主要依赖加法特征变换，仅隐式建模高阶特征交互。乘积单元虽能提供显式乘法建模，但其对数-指数形式存在数值不稳定问题，限制了其在深度密集预测网络中的应用。

Method: 提出PU-UNet，在残差U-Net的低分辨率阶段集成稳定的乘积单元残差块。采用平滑正性映射结合对数域裁剪策略，实现稳定的乘法特征学习，且计算开销可忽略不计。

Result: 在ISIC 2018、Kvasir-SEG和BUSI上Dice分别达到0.942、0.959和0.925；相比匹配的Residual U-Net基线，在参数、FLOPs和推理延迟几乎不变的情况下持续提升Dice和IoU；正常BUSI病例的图像级假阳性率从0.077降至零。

Conclusion: 稳定的乘积单元残差学习是增强U-Net风格分割网络显式乘法交互的有效方式，增益与乘积单元交互相关，在低分辨率放置时最强，且受益于所提出的稳定化设计。

Abstract: Many dense prediction networks rely on additive feature transformations and model higher-order feature interactions only implicitly. Product units provide an explicit mechanism for multiplicative feature modeling, but their logarithmic--exponential formulation can cause numerical instability, which has limited their use in deep dense prediction networks. In this work, we propose Product-Unit U-Net (PU-UNet), a residual U-Net that integrates stable product-unit residual blocks into rich low-resolution stages for medical image segmentation. The proposed formulation combines smooth positivity mapping with log-domain clipping, enabling stable multiplicative feature learning with negligible computational overhead. On ISIC 2018, Kvasir-SEG, and BUSI, PU-UNet achieves Dice scores of 0.942, 0.959, and up to 0.925, respectively. Compared with a matched Residual U-Net baseline, PU-UNet consistently improves Dice and IoU while keeping parameters, FLOPs, and inference latency nearly unchanged, and reduces the image-level false-positive rate on normal BUSI cases from 0.077 to zero. Ablation studies suggest that the gains are associated with product-unit interactions, are strongest under low-resolution placement, and benefit from the proposed stabilization design. These results suggest that stable product-unit residual learning can be an effective way to enhance U-Net-style segmentation networks with explicit multiplicative interactions.

</details>


### [132] [SSD: Spatially Speculative Decoding Accelerates Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20543)
*Shilong Xiang,Zirui Zhang,Lijun Yu,Chengzhi Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出空间推测解码（SSD）框架，通过利用图像2D空间局部性同时预测相邻水平与垂直方向token，将自回归图像生成加速最高13.3倍，同时保持高保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视觉模型将图像展平为1D序列，丢弃了2D空间局部性，导致推理时存在严重计算瓶颈（内存墙问题）。

Method: 空间推测解码（SSD）：模型不再仅预测1D序列中的下一个token，而是同时预测水平相邻token和正下方token，利用2D空间相关性实现推测解码。

Result: 在DPG-Bench和GenEval上，自回归图像生成加速最高达13.3倍，同时保持高保真度。

Conclusion: 尊重视觉信号的内在几何结构可解锁大规模计算效率，为实时、高分辨率自回归生成模型铺平道路。

Abstract: Autoregressive models excel in visual generation by treating images as 1D sequences of discrete tokens, mirroring language modeling. However, this flattening discards the intrinsic 2D spatial locality of visual signals, creating severe computational bottlenecks during inference. We introduce Spatially Speculative Decoding (SSD), a framework that aligns the predictive objective with the natural geometry of images. Rather than predicting only the immediate next token in a 1D sequence, our model simultaneously predicts the adjacent horizontal token and the token directly below it. By capitalizing on this 2D spatial correlation, spatially speculative decoding overcomes the memory wall in visual inference. Our approach accelerates autoregressive image generation by up to 13.3x while maintaining high fidelity on DPG-Bench and GenEval. Our results suggest that respecting the underlying geometry of vision unlocks massive computational efficiencies, paving the way for real-time, high-resolution autoregressive generative models.

</details>


### [133] [Current World Models Lack a Persistent State Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20545)
*Jinpeng Lu,Dexu Zhu,Haoyuan Shi,Linghan Cai,Guo Tang,Yinda Chen,Jie Cao,Duyu Tang,Yi Zhang,Yong Dai,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WRBench基准，系统性测试世界模型在视角干预（如相机移动）下内部世界状态的持续演化能力。测试发现：即使最先进的模型也无法在物体被遮挡后保持其状态演进，而是恢复为被遗忘前的状态，说明当前世界模型本质上是跟踪拍摄，而非真正模拟物理世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型基准仅评估生成帧的表面质量（保真度、运动、相机可控性），从未检验模型在观察中断后世界状态是否持续演化。然而物理世界的关键特征是物体和事件在无人观察时仍按规律运行，这一能力是实现通用人工智能的必要条件，却未被任何基准覆盖。

Method: 设计WRBench诊断基准，将相机运动视为对可观察性的干预，构建人机校准的评估链：(1) 相机是否执行了指定交互；(2) 场景在视野内是否连续可识别；(3) 返回目标是否与之前启动的事件状态一致。在9600个视频、23个模型、4种控制范式上进行系统评估。

Result: 所有模型均表现出系统性失败：当相机移开后再返回时，场景恢复到被遗忘前的状态，而非继续演进。这一缺陷跨控制范式、模型家族和规模增长持续存在，表明更清晰的图像、更精确的控制、更丰富的几何先验或更大的参数量都无法解决世界状态持续演化问题。

Conclusion: 物理状态核的稳定性和视角干预下世界线的一致性应成为世界模型设计的首要目标。世界模型应捕获"世界将如何展开"，而非"下一帧看起来如何"。

Abstract: World models are increasingly regarded as a decisive step toward artificial general intelligence, yet modeling the physical world demands more than rendering convincing frames on demand: it requires an internal world state that keeps evolving over time, decoupled from observation, so that objects endure and events run to their conclusions whether or not a camera is watching, much as the moon holds to its orbit when no one is looking. This requirement is a blind spot of existing benchmarks, which reward surface properties such as fidelity, motion, and camera controllability while never asking whether a generated world keeps evolving once it is unobserved. We introduce \textbf{WRBench}, the first systematic diagnostic benchmark that treats camera motion as an intervention on observability and resolves evaluation into a human-calibrated chain that asks whether the camera executes the requested interaction, whether the scene stays continuous and identifiable while in view, and whether a returning target remains consistent with the event that was set in motion. Across 9{,}600 videos from 23 models spanning four control paradigms, one finding proves stubborn: current systems maintain the observed world as a tracking shot, resuming a returning target in the state at which it was abandoned rather than advancing the event while it went unseen. Because this failure recurs across control paradigms, model families, and increments of scale, robust world-state evolution does not follow from cleaner imagery, tighter control, richer geometric priors, or sheer parameter count We therefore argue that the stability of the physical state kernel and the consistency of worldlines under viewpoint intervention should become first-class objectives of world-model design, so that a world model captures how the world will unfold rather than how the next frame appears.

</details>


### [134] [Thinking in Boxes: 3D Editing in Real Images Made Easy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20556)
*Pradhaan S Bhat,Naveen Chandra R,Rishubh Parihar,Vaibhav Vavilala,R. Venkatesh Babu,D. A. Forsyth,Anand Bhattad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"Thinking in Boxes"方法，通过3D盒子作为结构化规范进行图像编辑，用户提供输入和输出盒子，将编辑转化为几何问题，实现了对真实图像中物体平移、旋转、缩放和视角变化的精确控制，在大型3D编辑任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本和2D条件接口对图像编辑中的空间变换（尤其是大物体运动和相机变化）控制能力弱且模糊；先前使用3D基元（如盒子）仅作为松散条件信号指示大致位置，未能精确指定变换。

Method: 1) 使用3D盒子作为结构化规范，用户提供输入和输出盒子，将编辑视为良定义的几何问题；2) 每个盒子面采用颜色编码以传达3D朝向；3) 引入深度对齐的平面地板作为全局参考框架，并添加深度感知提示进行着色；4) 采用两阶段训练：先在合成多物体场景上训练，再在Objectron的真实世界视频上微调。

Result: 该方法直接操作真实照片，在大型3D编辑任务上显著优于近期最先进方法，能够恢复之前不可见的物体区域，同时保持场景和物体身份不变。

Conclusion: "Thinking in Boxes"接口通过3D盒子精确控制真实图像中的平移、旋转、缩放和视角变化，为图像编辑提供了一种更精确、更结构化的3D控制范式。

Abstract: Text and 2D-conditioning interfaces provide weak, ambiguous control over spatial transformations in image editing -- particularly under large object motions and camera changes. Prior work has used 3D primitives such as boxes, but only as loose conditioning signals indicating approximate object location rather than specifying the transformation. We instead use 3D boxes as structured specifications: the user provides the input and output boxes of the edit, casting editing as a well-posed geometry problem. This ``thinking in boxes'' interface, where each box face is color-coded to convey 3D orientation, gives precise control over translation, rotation, scaling, and viewpoint changes in real images while preserving scene and object identity, and recovering previously unseen object regions. To ground transformations in scene appearance, we introduce a depth-aligned planar floor as a global reference frame, shaded with depth-aware cues. Conditioned on this structure, an image generator produces consistent results under large transformations. Trained in two stages -- on synthetic multi-object scenes and a small set of real-world videos from Objectron -- the system generalizes to complex, in-the-wild real images. Our method operates directly on real photographs and substantially outperforms recent state-of-the-art methods on large 3D edits.

</details>


### [135] [See-and-Reach: Precise Vision-Language Navigation for UAVs within the Field of View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20045)
*Fanfu Xue,En Yu,Yantian Shen,Zhikun Hu,Hongjun Wang,Yang Yang,Xindi Wang,Jiande Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了UAV-VLN-FOV任务，用于单独评估无人机在目标可见条件下的精准到达能力，并设计了3DG-VLN框架，通过动态3D方向引导的视觉-语言航点预测，在仿真和真实环境中显著提升了目标到达成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV-VLN将远距离目标发现与最终目标接近联合优化和评估，难以单独衡量无人机在目标进入视野后能否准确地将视觉-语言证据转化为精确的3D运动，即终端的"看见并到达"能力。

Method: 提出3DG-VLN框架：1) 自适应处理高分辨率前视和下视观测以保留细粒度视觉和几何细节；2) 在闭环导航过程中在线更新目标相对方向，减少累积方向漂移；3) 基于动态3D方向线索进行视觉-语言航点预测。

Result: 在构建的高分辨率基准上，3DG-VLN相比现有UAV-VLN基线方法，成功率提升了13.82%，并在真实世界实验中验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: 通过将目标可见导航阶段独立出来评估，并结合动态3D方向引导的航点预测，可以有效增强无人机在视觉-语言导航中的精准目标到达能力。

Abstract: UAV Vision-Language Navigation (UAV-VLN) is typically formulated as a holistic search-and-reach problem, where long-range target discovery and final target approach are optimized and evaluated jointly. This formulation makes it difficult to assess a critical capability of aerial embodied agents, namely whether a UAV can accurately ground a visible target and translate vision-language evidence into precise 3D motion once the target enters its field of view. To address this limitation, we introduce UAV-VLN-FOV, a target-visible navigation task that isolates the see-and-reach stage and enables a more diagnostic evaluation of terminal reaching ability. We further propose 3DG-VLN, a vision-language waypoint prediction framework guided by dynamic 3D direction cues to enhance fine-grained visual grounding and spatial direction alignment for precise target reaching. Specifically, 3DG-VLN adaptively processes high-resolution front-view and downward-view observations to preserve fine-grained visual and geometric details for target grounding. It also updates the target-relative direction online during closed-loop navigation, allowing the agent to maintain spatial alignment with the target and reduce accumulated direction drift. To support this task, we construct a dedicated high-resolution benchmark which contains 2,717 trajectories with target-oriented high-level instructions, high-resolution front-view and downward-view egocentric observations, and continuous 3D waypoint annotations. Experiments show that 3DG-VLN outperforms competitive UAV-VLN baselines, achieving a 13.82\% improvement in success rate. Real-world trials further demonstrate the potential of 3DG-VLN for practical see-and-reach navigation. The source code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/xuefanfu/3DG-VLN.

</details>


### [136] [UNIEGO: Proxies as Mediators for Unified Egocentric Video Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20559)
*Wenhao Chi,Arkaprava Sinha,Dominick Reilly,Hieu Le,Srijan Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UNIEGO，一种层级式多教师蒸馏框架，通过代理模型和选择性代理蒸馏策略，将来自不同视角、模态和基础模型的知识统一到单一第一人称编码器中，在三个自我中心视频理解任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心视频理解受限于可穿戴摄像头的狭窄视角，单一视角、单一模态、单一模型无法充分捕捉人类行为的丰富性。需要一种能够融合跨视角、跨模态和跨基础模型知识的统一自我中心表示，同时保证仅从自我中心视频部署。

Method: 提出层级式多教师蒸馏框架：（1）引入代理模型层，将异构教师（涵盖自我-外部视角、RGB/深度/骨架模态、四个基础模型）的知识转化为同构的自我中心空间表示；（2）选择性代理蒸馏（SPD）自适应地为每个训练样本选择正确且置信的代理子集进行蒸馏；（3）通过将UNIEGO初始化为代理参数的凸组合，使模型在蒸馏前处于损失景观的良好条件区域。

Result: 在三个自我中心视频理解任务（动作识别、视频检索、动作分割）和三个挑战性基准数据集上达到最先进性能，显著优于朴素的多教师蒸馏基线方法。

Conclusion: 结构化的、代理介导的知识迁移能够产生更丰富、更具判别力的自我中心表示，验证了多源异构知识统一蒸馏的有效性与可行性。

Abstract: Egocentric video understanding is inherently limited by the narrow perspective of wearable cameras: a single viewpoint, a single modality, a single model cannot capture the full richness of human action. We argue that a truly expressive egocentric representation must subsume complementary knowledge across viewpoints, modalities, and foundation model representations, yet remain deployable from egocentric video alone. To this end, we introduce a hierarchical multi-teacher distillation framework that produces UNIEGO, a unified egocentric encoder trained with nine teachers spanning ego-exo viewpoints, RGB, depth, and skeleton modalities, and four foundation models. Rather than distilling directly from heterogeneous teachers whose incompatible architectures and feature geometries induce conflicting gradients, our framework interposes a layer of representation-specific Proxy models that translate diverse teacher knowledge into a homogeneous egocentric space. A second distillation stage, Selective Proxy Distillation (SPD), then adaptively selects, for each training sample, the subset of proxies that are both correct and confident, distilling exclusively from reliable supervision and suppressing erroneous signals. SPD is further stabilized by initializing UNIEGO as a learned convex combination of proxy parameters, placing the unified model in a well-conditioned region of the loss landscape before distillation begins. UNIEGO achieves state-of-the-art performance across three egocentric video understanding tasks - action recognition, video retrieval, and action segmentation on three challenging ego-exo benchmarks, outperforming naive multi-teacher distillation baselines and demonstrating that structured, proxy-mediated knowledge transfer yields richer and more discriminative egocentric representations.

</details>


### [137] [Variable-Length Tokenization via Learnable Global Merging for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20076)
*Dong Hoon Lee,Seunghoon Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于合并机制的可变长度分词器（LGM），通过将相似token合并而非截断来调节序列长度，解决了传统可变长度分词器中跨长度表征不对齐的问题，使单个扩散模型能在不同长度下有效运行，在ImageNet 256×256生成任务上实现了更优的gFID-计算量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在扩散模型的质量-计算量权衡受限于分词器的固定压缩率；可变长度分词器虽能自适应调节token数量以灵活平衡质量与计算量，但传统方法通过截断有序token序列来调节长度，导致token语义依赖位置、跨长度表征不对齐，使得单一可变长度扩散模型难以有效运行。

Method: 提出基于合并的可变长度分词器：1) 通过合并相似token来调节序列长度，实现跨长度表征的直接对齐；2) 引入可学习的全局合并（learnable global merging），使其数据无关、在生成阶段可访问，从而兼容扩散Transformer。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256生成任务上，该合并式可变长度分词器结合扩散Transformer，相比先前的可变长度分词器方法实现了更优的gFID-计算量权衡。

Conclusion: 基于合并的可变长度分词器有效解决了跨长度表征偏移问题，使单个扩散模型能在不同token数量下灵活运行，为扩散模型的质量-计算量自适应权衡提供了新思路。

Abstract: Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) have become dominant in visual synthesis, but their quality-compute trade-off is largely constrained by the tokenizer's fixed compression ratio. Variable-length tokenizers (VLTs) promise adaptive compression by varying token counts, allowing diffusion models to flexibly balance quality and compute. However, conventional VLTs modulate length by truncating ordered token sequences, which makes token semantics depend on token position and breaks representational alignment across lengths. This leads to a cross-length shift in the latent distribution that hinders a single variable-length diffusion model from operating effectively. To address this, we propose a novel variable-length tokenizer that modulates length by merging tokens. We show that encouraging similar tokens to merge enables direct cross-length representation alignment when the diffusion transformer operates according to the merging pattern. Since conventional merging methods are data-dependent, making the merging pattern inaccessible during generation, we introduce learnable global merging, which is data-independent, to ensure compatibility with diffusion transformers. On ImageNet 256$\times$256 generation, our merging-based variable-length tokenizer integrated with a diffusion transformer achieves a superior gFID-compute trade-off compared to prior VLT methods. Code is available at [this https URL](https://github.com/movinghoon/lgm)

</details>


### [138] [TimeProVe: Propose, then Verify for Efficient Long Video Temporal Reasoning in Activities of Daily Living](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20561)
*Arkaprava Sinha,Dominick Reilly,Siddharth Krishnan,Hieu Le,Srijan Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TimeProVe是一个面向长视频问答的高效混合框架，通过先使用轻量模块生成基于动作的证据假设，再调用昂贵VLM进行验证，在OpenTSUBench上比最强基线提升7.3%，同时减少75%的VLM调用和93%的推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频问答（LVQA）需要在数小时长的未裁剪视频中定位稀疏的、与查询相关的证据。现有方法要么密集处理视频导致计算成本过高，要么依赖稀疏字幕推理而丢失时间定位和运动中心证据，亟需一种成本高效的时序推理方案。

Method: 提出TimeProVe混合框架：首先使用轻量模块生成基于动作的答案-证据假设（ACE模块将时间定位的动作转化为查询条件化的候选答案和支持证据窗口），然后仅对候选进行昂贵的VLM验证。同时构建OpenTSUBench（OTB）基准数据集用于评估真实日常活动场景中的时序推理。

Result: 在OTB上TimeProVe超越最强基线7.3%，VLM调用减少75%，推理成本降低93%；在Charades-STA上无需时序训练即达竞争性能，结合时序VLM后达到SOTA。

Conclusion: TimeProVe通过"轻量生成+昂贵验证"的混合范式有效平衡了长视频问答的性能与计算效率，ACE模块成功捕获时序动作证据，OTB为时序推理评估提供了更具挑战性的基准。

Abstract: Long Video Question Answering (LVQA) requires identifying sparse, query-relevant evidence within hours-long untrimmed videos. Existing approaches either process videos densely with large vision-language models (VLMs), incurring prohibitive computational cost, or rely on sparse caption-based reasoning, which often misses temporally localized and motion-centric evidence. We introduce TimeProVe, a cost-efficient hybrid framework for temporally grounded reasoning in long videos. TimeProVe first employs lightweight modules to generate action-grounded answer--evidence hypotheses and subsequently invokes an expensive VLM only for targeted verification. The core of our framework lies in the Action-based Candidate Evidence (ACE) module, which converts temporally localized actions into query-conditioned candidate answers and supporting evidence windows through lightweight LLM reasoning. We further introduce OpenTSUBench (OTB), an open-ended benchmark designed to evaluate temporally grounded reasoning in real-world Activities of Daily Living (ADL) scenarios. Experiments show that TimeProVe outperforms the strongest baseline on OTB by 7.3%, while reducing VLM calls by 75% and inference cost by 93%. Furthermore, without explicit temporal grounding training, TimeProVe achieves competitive performance on Charades-STA, and reaches state-of-the-art results when enhanced with grounding VLMs.

</details>


### [139] [JanusMesh: Fast and Zero-Shot 3D Visual Illusion Generation via Cross-Space Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20563)
*Siang-Ling Zhang,Huai-Hsun Cheng,Tsung-Ju Yang,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种快速、无需训练的文本驱动3D视觉错觉生成框架JanusMesh，通过跨空间双分支去噪和视图条件纹理合成，在3-5分钟内生成具有几何完整性和双重语义的3D错觉，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D视觉错觉生成方法存在两大问题：基于优化的方法速度慢且颜色过饱和；朴素拼接方法会产生几何不连贯、可见接缝和语义泄露。因此需要一种快速、几何一致且语义清晰的3D错觉生成方法。

Method: 提出两阶段生成框架：第一阶段为跨空间双分支去噪过程，动态解码3D潜变量到体素空间，进行CLIP引导的视角对齐和符号距离场融合；第二阶段为视图条件纹理合成模块，将视图特定的2D扩散先验投影并聚合到融合后的几何体上。

Result: 该方法在3-5分钟内生成高度逼真的双语义3D错觉，在几何完整性、语义可识别性和效率方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种快速、无需训练的3D视觉错觉生成方案，有效解决了几何不连贯和语义泄露问题，在效率和生成质量上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Creating 3D visual illusions, a single 3D mesh that reveals entirely different semantics from various viewing angles, is a fascinating but tough challenge. Existing optimization-based methods are slow and can produce oversaturated colors. In contrast, naive stitching approaches fail to produce geometrically coherent objects. This results in visible unnatural seams and semantic leaks. In this paper, we present a fast and training-free framework for generating text-driven 3D visual illusions. Our approach decouples the generation into two stages. First, we propose a cross-space dual-branch denoising process. This process dynamically decodes 3D latents into voxel space for CLIP-guided orientation alignment and Signed Distance Field (SDF) blending, which ensures seamless geometric fusion. Second, we introduce a view-conditioned texture synthesis module that projects and aggregates view-specific 2D diffusion priors onto the fused geometry. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method generates highly realistic, dual-semantic 3D illusions in just 3-5 minutes. It significantly outperforms existing methods in geometric integrity, semantic recognizability, and efficiency. Project page: https://siang1105.github.io/JanusMesh.github.io/

</details>


### [140] [Evaluating and Enhancing Negation Comprehension in Remote Sensing MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20177)
*Haochen Han,Jue Wang,Alex Jinpeng Wang,Fangming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RS-Neg基准评估遥感多模态大模型的否定理解能力，并设计NeFo测试时学习方法，仅用约5%无标注测试样本即可显著提升模型对否定语义的理解和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感多模态大模型在理解否定语义（如"非洪水区域"）方面能力不足，限制了其在需要明确识别"不存在什么"的真实场景（如应急疏散路线规划）中的应用。

Method: (1) 构建RS-Neg基准：利用LLM自动化生成多样化否定查询，并引入动态视觉焦点模块进行验证；(2) 提出NeFo：一种测试时学习方法，将否定的逻辑角色显式融入模型优化过程，仅需约5%无标注测试样本。

Result: 现有先进遥感MLLM在处理否定语义时出现幻觉和显著性能下降；NeFo方法能显著提升模型的否定理解能力，并展现出对未见任务的强泛化性。

Conclusion: 遥感多模态大模型在否定理解上存在根本性局限，而NeFo作为一种轻量级测试时学习方法可有效弥补这一差距。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable success in various Remote Sensing (RS) tasks. However, their ability to comprehend negation remains underexplored, limiting deployment in real-world applications where models must explicitly identify what is false or absent, e.g., emergency responders need to locate non-flooded routes for evacuation. To comprehensively study this limitation, we introduce RS-Neg, the first benchmark to evaluate negation understanding across region-level to scene-level tasks. Specifically, we design an automated data generation pipeline for RS imagery, using LLMs to synthesize diverse negation queries, and introduce a dynamic visual focus module for verification. Our evaluation reveals that advanced RS MLLMs struggle with negation, exhibiting hallucinations and substantial performance degradation. To close this gap, we propose NeFo, a novel test-time learning method that explicitly incorporates the logical role of negation into the model optimization. Remarkably, using about 5\% unlabeled test samples, NeFo significantly improves the negation understanding of models and shows strong generalization to unseen tasks. Code and data will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [141] [HilDA: Hierarchical Distillation with Diffusion for Advancing Self-Supervised LiDAR Pre-trainin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20189)
*Maciej Wozniak,Jesper Ericsson,Hariprasath Govindarajan,Truls Nyberg,Thomas Gustafsson,Patric Jensfelt,Olov Andersson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HilDA提出了一种自监督预训练框架，通过层级蒸馏（多层蒸馏+全局上下文蒸馏）和时序占用扩散目标，更好地利用视觉基础模型的层级语义结构和全局上下文以及LiDAR序列的时空信息，在3D目标检测、场景流和语义占用预测等任务上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将视觉基础模型视为黑盒教师，仅依赖逐帧特征相似性，未能充分利用教师的层级语义结构、全局上下文以及LiDAR序列中丰富的时空信息。

Method: HilDA结合了层级蒸馏（多层蒸馏实现渐进语义对齐+全局上下文蒸馏实现场景级语义）和时序占用扩散目标（促进时空一致性）。

Result: 在跨模态蒸馏基准上达到最优，在3D目标检测、场景流和语义占用预测任务上超越先前蒸馏方法训练出的模型。

Conclusion: HilDA通过更好地捕捉驾驶任务所需的语义“什么”和几何“哪里”信息，实现了更有效的LiDAR骨干自监督预训练。

Abstract: Leveraging Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) for camera-to-LiDAR knowledge distillation offers a promising solution to the scarcity of annotated data needed to represent the immense geometric and kinematic diversity of real-world autonomous driving (AD). However, current approaches typically treat VFMs as black-box teachers, relying exclusively on frame-wise feature similarity. Consequently, they do not fully exploit the teacher's layer-wise semantic structure and global context, as well as the rich spatiotemporal information inherent in LiDAR sequences. We propose HilDA, a self-supervised pretraining framework for LiDAR backbones that better captures the semantic what and geometric where needed for driving tasks. HilDA combines hierarchical distillation comprising multi-layer distillation for progressive semantic alignment and global context distillation for scene-level semantics, with a temporal occupancy diffusion objective promoting spatiotemporal consistency. Models pre-trained with HilDA achieve state-of-the-art results on cross-modal distillation benchmarks and outperform models trained via prior distillation approaches on 3D object detection, scene flow, and semantic occupancy prediction. Code available at: https://maxiuw.github.io/hilda.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Secure Coding Drift in LLM-Assisted Post-Quantum Cryptography Development: A Gamified Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19474)
*R. D. N. Shakya,C. P. Wijesiriwardana,S. M. Vidanagamachchi,Nalin A. G. Arachchilage*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出“后量子密码学安全编码漂移”这一新型社会技术漏洞模型，描述因持续依赖大语言模型生成代码而导致安全编码实践逐渐退化的现象，并提出一种游戏化、LLM增强的安全编码框架，将LLM从被动助手转变为主动安全副驾驶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向PQC的过渡带来了常量时间执行、侧信道抵抗等严格实现要求，而LLM在密码工程中广泛使用却经常生成不安全或次优代码。现有工作仅关注静态漏洞，忽略了人机交互中长期依赖导致的渐进式安全退化。因此需要研究这种社会技术性风险并设计缓解方案。

Method: 提出Secure Coding Drift in PQC（PQC安全编码漂移）概念模型，将安全风险视为纵向行为现象。设计一个游戏化、LLM增强的安全编码框架，整合对抗性评估、行为反馈和安全评分机制，嵌入开发工作流中。

Result: 提出了一个将LLM从被动代码生成工具转变为主动安全协作者的概念框架，能够通过持续反馈和对抗性评估来检测和缓解安全编码漂移现象。

Conclusion: 该研究首次从社会技术角度系统化了LLM在PQC实现中引发的安全编码退化问题，提出的框架为在AI介导环境下实现更安全的PQC部署提供了新方向。

Abstract: The transition to Post Quantum Cryptography (PQC) introduces considerable implementation complexity, requiring strict adherence to constant-time execution, side channel resistance, and precise parametrisation. Simultaneously, large language models (LLMs) are heavily embedded in software development workflows, including cryptographic engineering. While LLMs improve productivity, evidence shows that they frequently generate insecure or suboptimal code, particularly in security critical domains. This paper introduces Secure Coding Drift in PQC, a novel socio technical vulnerability model capturing the gradual degradation of secure coding practices due to sustained reliance on LLM-generated code. Unlike prior work that focuses on static vulnerabilities, we conceptualise security risk as a longitudinal behavioural phenomenon rising from human AI interaction. To mitigate this, we propose a gamified, LLM augmented secure coding framework that embeds adversarial evaluation, behavioural feedback, and security scoring into development workflows. Our approach reframes LLMs from passive assistants into active security co-pilots, contributing toward safer PQC implementation in AI mediated environments.

</details>


### [143] [FloatDoor: Platform-Triggered Backdoors in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19535)
*Nils Loose,Jonas Sander,Felix Mächtle,Thomas Eisenbarth*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FloatDoor，首个输入无关、平台触发的后门攻击方法，利用LLM在不同部署平台（GPU/TPU/CPU）上的浮点运算数值差异，通过两个轻量LoRA适配器放大平台间数值分歧并将平台签名绑定到恶意下游任务，实现模型在目标平台上表现出攻击者指定的恶意行为（如生成可利用代码漏洞），而在其他平台表现正常。该攻击利用模型审计与部署之间的TOCTOU差距，在Qwen3-4B及多种硬件平台上成功验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在敏感场景（如软件工程）中部署，但同一模型在不同平台上因非结合性浮点运算和不同的内核实现会产生可测量的输出差异。作者试图研究这种平台依赖的可变性的安全影响，发现了一种新的攻击面。

Method: FloatDoor包含两个轻量LoRA适配器：第一个适配器放大跨平台数值分歧（amplifies inter-platform numerical divergence），第二个适配器将生成的平台签名绑定到恶意下游任务（binds the resulting platform signature to a malicious downstream task）。攻击利用模型审计与模型服务之间的TOCTOU（time-of-check, time-of-use）差距。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B模型及多种部署目标（NVIDIA GPU、Google TPU、AWS Graviton、Alibaba Yitian-710）上成功验证。案例研究表明FloatDoor能够可靠地在目标平台上诱发可利用的代码漏洞。

Conclusion: FloatDoor建立了一类新的LLM部署攻击，强调了在LLM驱动的敏感应用中建立可信模型供应链的紧迫性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in sensitive settings such as software engineering, where their outputs directly shape downstream artifacts. Recent work has shown that an identical model can produce measurably different outputs depending on the deployment platform, a consequence of non-associative floating-point arithmetic and divergent kernel implementations. We study the security implications of this platform-dependent variability and uncover a novel attack surface on LLM deployments. We introduce FloatDoor, the first input-independent, platform-triggered backdoor attack against generative LLMs. The compromised model exhibits adversary-chosen behavior when served on a target platform and is otherwise benign. FloatDoor is realized through two lightweight LoRA adapters, one that amplifies inter-platform numerical divergence and one that binds the resulting platform signature to a malicious downstream task, while leaving aggregate model utility largely intact. FloatDoor exploits a pronounced time-of-check, time-of-use gap between model auditing and serving. We demonstrate FloatDoor on Qwen3-4B across a broad range of deployment targets, including NVIDIA GPUs, Google TPUs, AWS Graviton, and Alibaba Yitian-710. As a final case study, we show that FloatDoor reliably induces exploitable code vulnerabilities on a chosen target platform. Our results establish a new class of attacks on LLM deployments and underscore the pressing need for trusted model supply chains in sensitive, LLM-powered applications.

</details>


### [144] [G-Lox: Group-Adaptive, Privacy-Preserving Bridge Distribution with Two-Party Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19620)
*Baigang Chen,Nicholas Hopper*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: G-Lox是一个保持Lox式分发器盲化特性的群自适应桥接分发系统，通过双服务器隐私墙、DPF/FSS协议和两方安全计算实现隐藏的、有状态的群级自适应，评估显示低客户端开销且比Lox和rBridge等基线具有更好的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现有桥接分发系统（如Lox）中，分发器虽对具体桥接分配保持盲化，但缺乏群级自适应能力，无法根据群组状态（如封锁报告、传输协议适配）动态调整分配策略，导致鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出G-Lox系统，将自适应分配逻辑置于双服务器隐私墙之后，确保单一服务器无法获知群标识符或群-桥接分配；采用两服务器DPF/FSS协议和安全两方计算实现私密状态访问与状态相关更新，支持封锁报告、传输感知重分配和隐私保护的群拆分。

Result: C++/EMP实现表明：私密状态访问的客户端可见开销低，状态规模达2^16时通信量仅为低KiB级别；M=1024时，客户端发送1968字节、接收1280字节，单次迭代约0.25秒。群特定封锁和女巫枚举模拟显示，在保持广泛发行的系统中，G-Lox比Lox和rBridge类基线具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: G-Lox成功在保持分发器盲化特性的前提下引入隐藏的、有状态的群级自适应，以较低开销显著提升了系统鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present G-Lox (group-adaptive Lox), a bridge-distribution system that preserves Lox-style distributor blindness while enabling hidden, stateful group-level adaptation. G-Lox places adaptive assignment logic behind a two-server privacy wall, so no single server learns group identifiers or group-to-bridge assignments. Private state access and state-dependent updates use two-server DPF/FSS protocols and secure two-party computation, supporting blockage reporting, transport-aware reassignment, and privacy-preserving group splitting.
  We evaluate G-Lox through system measurements and policy simulation. In our C++/EMP implementation over real TCP sockets, private state access has low client-visible overhead: across state sizes up to 2^16, communication remains in the low-KiB range per iteration. At M=1024, the client sends 1,968 bytes, receives 1,280 bytes, and completes an iteration in about 0.25 s. Simulations with group-specific blocking and Sybil enumeration show that G-Lox improves robustness over Lox- and rBridge-like baselines among systems that maintain broad issuance.

</details>


### [145] [PUFFERDOS: Efficient and Effective Attack String Generation for Regular Expression Denial of Service Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19654)
*Shangzhi Xu,Ziqi Ding,Xiao Cheng,Yuekang Li,Nan Sun,Benjamin Turnbull,Shuangxiang Kan,Siqi Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PUFFERDOS，一种能生成在实际长度预算内且经程序级验证的有效ReDoS攻击字符串的生成器，解决了现有攻击字符串不实用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ReDoS攻击字符串生成方法存在两个主要缺陷：一是生成的攻击字符串假设不切实际的输入长度预算，二是不在程序层面验证攻击的有效性和效率，导致许多字符串在实际程序中无法触发漏洞，实用性有限。

Method: 首先基于观察和形式化验证定义三种漏洞模式；然后根据这些模式进行综合技术生成攻击字符串；最后通过ReDoS专用的组合符号执行对字符串进行精炼和验证，确保实际可利用性。

Result: PUFFERDOS能够生成在现实长度预算内可行且经程序级验证的攻击字符串，成功利用真实世界程序中的ReDoS漏洞。

Conclusion: 通过定义漏洞模式、综合生成与组合符号执行验证相结合的方法，PUFFERDOS有效解决了现有攻击字符串不实用的问题，提升了ReDoS漏洞检测的实用价值。

Abstract: ReDoS attacks constitute a critical class of resource-exhaustion vulnerabilities. In such attacks, adversaries exploit the pathological worst-case execution behavior of regular expression (regex) engines to induce highly asymmetric computational workloads, ultimately exhausting system resources and degrading service availability. To protect systems against ReDoS attacks, numerous detection techniques have been proposed that simulate the attack process by generating attack strings to proactively exploit ReDoS vulnerabilities at the early development stage and facilitate remediation. Existing techniques broadly fall into two classes: static analyses that search for pathological regex structures, and dynamic exploration methods that synthesize candidate attack strings. However, the generated attack strings are often impractical for real-world exploitation because they usually assume unrealistic input-length budgets and do not validate the effectiveness and efficiency of the attack at the program level. Therefore, many generated strings fail to trigger vulnerable regexes when applied to real-world programs, further limiting the practical utility. To address these shortcomings, we introduce an effective and efficient attack string generator, PUFFERDOS, designed to synthesize attack inputs that are both feasible within realistic length budgets and validated at the program level, enabling effective exploitation of ReDoS vulnerabilities in real-world programs. Specifically, we first define three vulnerable patterns based on our observation and formal verification. According to the patterns, PUFFERDOS conducts a synthesis technique to generate attack strings, and then refines and validates the strings with ReDoS-specific compositional concolic execution to guarantee real-world exploitability.

</details>


### [146] [A Layered Security Framework Against Prompt Injection in RAG-Based Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19660)
*Gulshan Saleem,Nisar Ahmed,Muhammad Imran Zaman,Ali Hassan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三层提示注入防御框架，在推理管线的用户输入、上下文组装和模型输出三个阶段分别拦截直接与间接提示注入，将攻击成功率从71.4%降至11.3%，误报率4.8%，中位延迟开销61.2ms，且优于现有单层方案和已发表的护栏系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御仅在管线孤立阶段运作且不完整：输入过滤器无法检查检索文档，输出监控无法阻止恶意载荷到达模型。RAG聊天机器人因此易受间接注入攻击，一个被投毒的知识库文档可危害所有检索到它的用户。

Method: 三层框架：第1层用规则模式库和微调语义异常分类器筛查用户输入；第2层在上下文组装时实施基于来源的指令层级，防止检索内容覆盖操作策略；第3层用策略规则引擎和语义漂移检测器审计模型输出。连续审计循环聚合结构化日志并支持重训练。模型无关，以中间件形式部署。

Result: 在GPT-4o、Llama 3和Mistral 7B上对5,080个样本评估，ASR从71.4%降至11.3%，优于最佳单层基线27.3个百分点，优于已发表的护栏系统23.8个百分点，误报率4.8%，中位延迟开销61.2ms。消融实验证实三层提供互补保护且联合效果超过各自贡献之和。

Conclusion: 该三层框架在无需修改底层LLM的前提下，显著降低了直接和间接提示注入的攻击成功率，为RAG聊天机器人提供了一种实用且可扩展的防御方案。

Abstract: Prompt injection is ranked as the most critical vulnerability in large language model (LLM) deployments by the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications, yet existing defenses operate at isolated pipeline stages and remain incomplete. Input filters cannot inspect retrieved documents, while output monitors cannot prevent malicious payloads from reaching the model. Consequently, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) chatbots remain vulnerable to indirect injection, where a poisoned knowledge-base document compromises every user whose query retrieves it. We present a three-layer framework that intercepts both direct and indirect prompt injection throughout the inference pipeline. Layer 1 screens user input using a rule-based pattern library and a fine-tuned semantic anomaly classifier. Layer 2 enforces a provenance-based instruction hierarchy during context assembly, preventing retrieved content from overriding operator policy. Layer 3 audits model output using a policy rule engine and semantic drift detector before delivery. A continuous audit loop aggregates structured logs and supports retraining to adapt the classifier to emerging attack patterns. The framework is model-agnostic and deploys as middleware without modifying the underlying LLM. Evaluation on 5,080 samples across GPT-4o, Llama 3, and Mistral 7B shows that the framework reduces Attack Success Rate (ASR) from 71.4\% to 11.3\%, outperforming the best single-layer baseline by 27.3 percentage points and a published guardrail system by 23.8 percentage points, while maintaining a 4.8\% false positive rate and a median latency overhead of 61.2 ms. Ablation studies confirm that all three layers provide complementary protection and that their combined effect exceeds the sum of individual contributions.

</details>


### [147] [When Global Gating Is Enough: Admission-Time Hubness Control in Anisotropic Vector Retrieval Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19692)
*Prashant Kumar Pathak,Tarun Kumar Sharma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对RAG系统中向量中心性（hubness）投毒攻击的准入控制方法，通过在文档插入前使用哨兵查询评分并隔离类似hub的文档，实现了接近1.0的召回率、低误报率（1%）和增量维护成本，且仅增加约3.1%的摄入延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中存在向量中心性投毒风险：少量文档会成为许多查询的最近邻，导致单个注入文档可影响无关请求。现有基于周期性反向kNN扫描的防御方案存在暴露窗口期且需重复全语料库计算。

Method: 提出准入时间控制方法：在文档插入前，使用哨兵查询对候选文档评分，将类似hub的文档隔离。设计了全局门控和逐主题门控两种机制，阈值可增量维护，支持HNSW近似索引。

Result: 在两个10万文档语料库、五种编码器、不相交的攻击者和防御者查询集上：全局门控在决定性嵌入空间点召回率1.0（有效范围≥0.92），HotFlip攻击召回率0.91±0.07，通用文档误报率1%。摄入延迟仅增加约3.1%，评分在10^6向量规模下保持平稳，近似索引下仅1.2%的决策翻转且不涉及攻击。

Conclusion: 准入控制可有效防御RAG中的hubness投毒攻击，全局门控优于逐主题门控。增量维护使成本与语料库大小无关，实际开销低。对于自然或紧领域hub，需结合来源追踪作为补充。

Abstract: Vector hubness, where a few points become nearest neighbors of many queries, creates a poisoning risk in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG): one injected document can influence unrelated requests. Existing defenses use periodic reverse-kNN scans, leaving an exposure window and repeated corpus-wide work. We study admission-time control, scoring each candidate against sentinel queries and quarantining hub-like documents before insertion. Across two 100,000-document corpora, five encoders, and disjoint attacker and defender query sets, a global gate achieves recall 1.0 at the decisive embedding-space point (>=0.92 across the effective range) and 0.91 +/- 0.07 on HotFlip attacks, with 1% false positives on general documents. A per-topic gate provides no reliable benefit, consistent with anisotropy coupling local and global visibility. Thresholds are maintained incrementally, with corpus-size-independent insertion cost and amortized deletion cost. On HNSW, admission adds about 3.1% to ingestion latency, scoring remains flat to 10^6 vectors, and 1.2% of decisions flip under approximate indexing, none involving attacks. Provenance complements the gate for natural or tight-domain hubs.

</details>


### [148] [SafeSpec: Fast and Safe LLM via Dynamic Reflective Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19755)
*Haotian Xu,Zeyang Zhang,Linbao Li,Huadi Zheng,Yu Li,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeSpec 是一种将安全风险估计直接集成到推测解码验证过程中的框架，通过轻量级安全头实现安全与效率的联合优化，在保持推理加速的同时显著降低攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全防御方法与推测推理不兼容：要么引入额外计算开销，要么破坏草稿-验证机制，导致加速收益丧失。这揭示了两者之间的根本性不兼容问题。

Method: 在目标模型上附加轻量级潜在安全头，在单次前向传播中联合评估语义有效性和安全性；检测到不安全生成时执行回滚和安全引导的反射式多采样以恢复安全延续；将越狱攻击建模为生成轨迹上的分布偏移，进行风险感知的轨迹恢复。

Result: 在多个模型和对抗性基准测试中，SafeSpec 显著提升了安全-效率权衡。在 Qwen3-32B 上，攻击成功率降低15%，同时在良性工作负载上保持 2.06x 的推理加速。

Conclusion: 推测加速与推理时安全可以联合优化，打破了安全防御与加速机制之间的根本性不兼容。

Abstract: Speculative inference accelerates large language model (LLM) decoding but provides no inherent safety guarantees. Existing safety defenses are largely incompatible with speculative inference: they either introduce additional computation or disrupt the draft-verify mechanism, negating acceleration benefits. This reveals a fundamental incompatibility between current safety methods and speculative decoding. We propose SafeSpec, a safety-aware speculative inference framework that integrates risk estimation directly into the verification process. SafeSpec attaches a lightweight latent safety head to the target model to jointly evaluate semantic validity and safety in a single forward pass. When unsafe generations are detected, SafeSpec applies rollback and safety-guided reflective multi-sampling to recover safe continuations rather than terminating generation. We model jailbreak attacks as distributional shifts over generative trajectories, where adversarial prompts increase the probability of harmful continuations without eliminating safe ones. Under this model, SafeSpec performs risk-aware trajectory recovery within the speculative decoding process. Across multiple models and adversarial benchmarks, SafeSpec achieves a substantially improved safety-efficiency trade-off. On Qwen3-32B, SafeSpec reduces attack success rates by 15% while preserving a 2.06x inference speedup on benign workloads, demonstrating that speculative acceleration and inference-time safety can be jointly optimized.

</details>


### [149] [DISARM: Target Electronic Device Informed Mitigation of Software Runtime Side-Channel Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19807)
*Tasneem Suha,Tanzim Mahfuz,Rima Asmar Awad,Prabuddha Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DISARM是一种联合硬件-软件的运行时侧信道漏洞缓解方法，利用真实嵌入式设备的时序值生成针对性的软件修复，在22个基准测试中优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时侧信道攻击缓解方案在修复过程中未考虑底层硬件或设备特性，导致过度修复、修复不足甚至失败。

Method: 提出DISARM，一种联合硬件-软件方法论，利用真实嵌入式设备的时序值生成针对性软件修复，支持C、C++和Java源码。

Result: 在22个标准基准测试和5种不同嵌入式/边缘设备上验证，DISARM在执行时间开销、代码体积开销和正确性方面均优于PENDULUM和DifFuzzAR等现有方案。

Conclusion: DISARM通过结合硬件时序信息来指导软件修复，有效解决了不考虑硬件特性的缓解方案存在的问题，在多个指标上显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Program runtime or timing attacks exploit variations in a program's execution times to extract sensitive information from the program (e.g. encryption keys, sensitive variable data, intellectual property). State-of-the-art solutions to runtime side-channel attacks attempt to balance the execution time of the sensitive code for different control flow paths to eliminate the timing leakage. However, during the mitigation process, most techniques do not consider the underlying hardware or device on which the target program is supposed to run on. This can lead to over-fixing (unnecessary extra operations), under-fixing (not solving the imbalance properly), and even failures. We propose DISARM, a joint hardware-software methodology (unlike any existing solution) for mitigating runtime side-channel vulnerabilities that utilizes timing values from real embedded devices to generate targeted software fixes. We implement DISARM to support C, C++, and Java source codes and validate it across 22 standard benchmarks. DISARM outperforms state-of-the-art solutions such as PENDULUM and DifFuzzAR in terms of execution time overhead, code size overhead, and correctness on five different embedded or edge devices.

</details>


### [150] [Heterogeneous LLM Debate Under Adversarial Peers: Honest Gains, Replacement Costs, and Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19826)
*Prashanti Nilayam,Kiran Kumar Ramanna,Prashil Tumbade,Sankalp Nayak*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究异构大模型辩论中，异构同伴对诚实智能体修订行为的影响。发现诚实异构同伴能显著降低有害修订率（如Llama-3.1-70B在MATH-hard上从89%降到35%），而对抗性异构同伴则逆转这一效果。当已有同族对抗者时，诚实异构同伴能降低有害修订和正确答案丢失率（翻牌率从31%降至6%），表明异构性既是攻击面也是防御手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构大模型辩论的动机是不同模型能互相纠正错误，但同一交流过程既可能带来纠正，也可能带来对抗性影响。作者旨在量化哪种效应占主导，通过追踪异构同伴如何改变诚实智能体的修订行为。

Method: 比较匹配面板（同质基线、诚实混合、对抗混合）和受污染面板（已存在恶意同族同伴），覆盖四个模型家族和三个推理基准。通过测量智能体改变答案的频率以及改变是纠正性的还是有害的来评估效果。

Result: 诚实异构同伴显著降低有害修订率（Llama-3.1-70B在MATH-hard上从89%降至35%），对抗性异构同伴将其恢复至90%。条件率在弱防御者上隐藏了这种损害，但辩论结束时的翻牌率能暴露它。当已有同族对抗者时，诚实异构同伴将正确答案翻牌率从31%降至6%。

Conclusion: 异构性不仅是攻击面（对抗性异构者能反转有益修订），当对抗者已经存在时，异构性也是一种防御手段。模式在不同模型家族和基准上保持符号一致性，幅度随防御者-基准组合变化。

Abstract: Heterogeneous LLM debate is motivated by the promise that diverse peers correct one another, but the same exchange that carries correction also carries adversarial influence. We measure which dominates by tracking how a heterogeneous peer changes the honest agents' revision behavior: how often they change their answer, and whether the change is corrective or harmful. We compare matched panels (homogeneous baseline, honest-mixed, and adversarial-mixed) and contaminated panels in which a malicious same-family peer is already present, spanning four model families and three reasoning benchmarks. An honest heterogeneous peer sharply lowers harmful revision, and an adversarial one reverses it. For Llama-3.1-70B defenders on MATH-hard, the honest-slot harmful-revision rate falls from 89% in the homogeneous panel to 35% with an honest peer, and an adversarial peer returns it to 90%. The conditional rate hides this damage on weak defenders, but the end-of-debate flip rate exposes it. The pattern keeps its sign across families and benchmarks while its magnitude varies with the defender-benchmark regime. We also measure the effects when an adversarial same-family peer is already present: an honest heterogeneous peer lowers both harmful revision and the rate at which initially-correct answers are lost. On the same Llama-3.1-70B setting, the added honest peer cuts the flip rate on initially-correct items from 31% under a same-family adversary to 6%. Heterogeneity is therefore not only an attack surface but, when an adversary is already present, also a defense.

</details>


### [151] [Low-Cost Multi-Precision Systolic Arrays for Accelerating FHE NTTs on AI ASICs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19866)
*George Alexakis,Dimitrios Schoinianakis,Giorgos Dimitrakopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种在TPU等AI硬件上加速全同态加密（FHE）的方法，通过多精度脉动阵列原生执行高精度数论变换（NTT）重构，解决了低精度矩阵引擎与FHE高精度需求之间的精度不匹配问题，在7nm工艺下实现至少1.33倍加速，硬件开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方案使用矩阵分解和移位累加重构高精度结果，但该重构过程需要卸载到向量处理器，破坏了矩阵乘法的数据流，造成严重的性能瓶颈。因此需要一种不中断矩阵乘法数据流、能在矩阵引擎内原生完成高精度重构的架构。

Method: 提出一种最小化修改的多精度脉动阵列，在阵列内部原生执行全精度输出重构，与低精度矩阵乘法在同一数据流下同步进行。采用7nm工艺使用OpenRoad工具综合实现。

Result: 在128x128矩阵引擎上，对2^12到2^16规模的NTT计算，相比基线实现至少1.33倍加速；硬件开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 该设计成功使标准AI硬件能够高效支持高精度FHE加速，为在AI加速器上部署隐私保护计算提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) ensures robust data privacy but suffers from prohibitive computational overhead. Accelerating FHE on AI hardware like Tensor Processing Units (TPUs) is promising, yet fundamentally limited by a precision mismatch: TPUs are optimized for 8-bit arithmetic, whereas FHE and its critical parts such as the Number Theoretic Transform (NTT), demand high precision. Current approaches bridge this gap using matrix decomposition to execute NTT computations on low-precision matrix engines. However, reconstructing the full-precision results requires shift-and-add accumulation that does not match the dataflow of matrix multiplication. This forces offloading full-precision reconstruction from matrix engines to vector processors that disrupts the matrix multiplication dataflow, creating significant performance bottleneck. To resolve this limitation, we propose a minimally modified multi-precision systolic array that performs full-precision output reconstruction natively within the array in sync with low-precision matrix multiplication under a uniform dataflow. Synthesized at 7nm with OpenRoad, our design incurs negligible hardware overhead.
