Cycle-accurate simulations using SCALE-Sim demonstrate that natively executing NTTs on the proposed architecture achieves at least 1.33x speedup, for transform sizes 2^12 to 2^16 on 128x128 matrix engines, successfully enabling standard AI hardware to support high-precision FHE acceleration.

</details>


### [152] [FFinRED: An Expert-Guided Benchmark Generation and Evaluation Framework for Financial LLM Red-Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19887)
*Chaeyun Kim,Daeyoung Park,Junghwan Kim,Jinyoung Jeong,Eunji Song,Yongtaek Lim,Minwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FinRED是一个由金融专家主导的红队测试框架，用于金融大语言模型安全评估，包含双层分类体系、自动化生成管道和专家验证的评估准则，已在韩国金融安全研究院监管沙盒中部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准针对通用对抗场景，缺乏金融领域特定风险（如监管合规违规、欺诈助长、系统性信任侵蚀）的评估能力，亟需金融大语言模型的专项安全评测框架。

Method: 提出FinRED框架：(1)构建双层分类体系，将全球标准（FATF、EU DORA）映射到金融威胁；(2)设计可扩展管道，将真实金融文档通过专家定义模式转化为上下文丰富的红队行为提示（种子）；(3)开发专家验证的金融专项评估准则，超越简单免责声明检查，将关键假阴性从28降至12。

Result: 专家验证确认了种子的合理性和真实性；新准则比静态统一准则更接近人类专家判断，关键假阴性从28降至12；框架已在韩国金融安全研究院监管沙盒中部署用于生成式AI安全评估。

Conclusion: FinRED与ISO/IEC 27001等国际标准对齐，有效填补了金融LLM安全评估的空白，但需对数据集和管道进行访问控制以防范双重用途风险。

Abstract: Existing safety benchmarks target general adversarial scenarios but miss finance-specific risks. Financial LLMs face regulatory compliance violations, fraud facilitation, and systemic trust erosion that require targeted evaluation. We introduce FinRED, an expert-guided red-teaming framework for financial LLM safety evaluation developed with financial experts. FinRED uses a novel two-level taxonomy mapping global standards (e.g., FATF and EU DORA) to threats ranging from regulatory evasion to complex fraud, integrated with a scalable pipeline that converts real financial documents into context-rich red-teaming Behavioral Prompts (seeds) through an expert-defined schema. Rigorous expert validation confirms seed plausibility and realism for meaningful LLM safety evaluation. We also provide an expert-validated, finance-specific rubric that goes beyond disclaimer checks, aligns more closely with human experts than static one-size-fits-all rubrics, and reduces critical false negatives from 28 to 12. Aligned with internationally adopted risk-management and information-security standards (e.g., ISO/IEC 27001), FinRED is deployed in South Korea's Financial Security Institute (FSI) regulatory sandbox for generative AI security evaluation in real financial services. To mitigate dual-use risks, the dataset, generation pipeline, prompt template, and evaluation framework are gated for qualified researchers at https://github.com/selectstar-ai/FinRED-paper and https://huggingface.co/datasets/datumo/FinRED.

</details>


### [153] [AutoTam: Specifying Secure Protocol Implementations with Tamarin Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19937)
*Johannes Wilson,Mikael Asplund,Niklas Johansson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种语言优先的形式化验证方法，通过专为协议实现设计的领域特定语言（DSL），自动生成Tamarin验证模型，并将验证的通用迹属性转换回具体实现。同时集成符号执行分析内存安全，在Diffie-Hellman签名协议和WireGuard VPN协议上验证了安全性与互操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证对密码协议安全至关重要，但现有工具建模难度大，需要形式化验证背景；且将验证结果迁移到具体实现需要专业知识。因此需要降低使用门槛，并实现验证结果到实现的可信传递。

Method: 采用语言优先方法，设计面向协议实现的领域特定语言（DSL），自动生成Tamarin验证模型；证明已验证的通用迹属性可转换回实现；集成符号执行分析内存安全。

Result: 实现了Diffie-Hellman签名协议和WireGuard VPN协议的模型生成与验证；WireGuard实现通过解释器可与现有实现互操作，性能可接受；通过符号执行与Tamarin模型验证证明了实现的安全性。

Conclusion: 语言优先的方法可降低形式化验证门槛，实现验证结果到实现的可靠传递，兼顾安全性与实用性。

Abstract: Formal verification is a challenging but important task for ensuring the security of cryptographic protocols. While modern protocol verification tools significantly reduce verification effort, modelling remains challenging to practitioners without a background in formal verification. In addition, transferring verification results to a concrete protocol implementation requires expert knowledge.
  In this paper, we present a novel language-first method for verification of trace properties using a domain-specific language for protocol implementations. We target the Tamarin prover for verification, and we prove that verified universal trace properties translate back to the implementation. We additionally integrate symbolic execution in order to analyse the memory safety of protocol implementations. We use our tool to implement and generate accurate models for a signed Diffie-Hellman protocol, and for the WireGuard VPN protocol. Our WireGuard implementation is interoperable with existing implementations when using our interpreter, and achieves acceptable performance. We formally prove our implementations secure using a combination of symbolic execution and verification of the generated Tamarin models.

</details>


### [154] [A Measurement Study of Cryptographic Misuse in Embodied AI Mobile Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19983)
*Junchao Li,Xuelei Wang,Yuhang Huang,Qi Wang,Boyang Ma,Xuelong Dai,Minghui Xu,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对具身AI（EAI）移动生态系统中的密码学误用进行大规模测量研究。通过构建包含507个真实应用的EAIAppZoo基准数据集，并采用自动化语义分析流水线，发现了12,975个密码学误用实例（精确率80.74%）。研究表明，这些密码学缺陷源于EAI特有的工程约束（如延迟敏感控制路径导致的传输保护弱化、离线设备配置和遗留IoT SDK导致的本地硬编码凭据），而非随机开发者错误。移动端密码学漏洞可绕过名义上的网络安全防护，使攻击者拦截命令通道并劫持EAI实体的物理控制，揭示移动应用成为网络物理系统中脆弱且被忽视的密码学信任边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全研究主要关注具身AI设备和云基础设施，而移动控制层作为关键攻击面尚未被充分探索。随着EAI移动应用从辅助用户界面演变为主动控制路径组件，移动端密码学安全直接关系到网络物理信任，因此有必要对这一缺口进行系统性研究。

Method: 构建了EAIAppZoo基准数据集（507个真实应用，涵盖6个EAI领域），并开发了自动化语义感知分析流水线，对五大类密码学失效模式（传输保护弱化、本地硬编码凭据、弱密码算法、证书验证缺失、密钥管理不当）进行测量。

Result: 共发现12,975个密码学误用实例，评估精确率为80.74%。识别出两类结构性安全权衡：延迟敏感控制路径系统性削弱传输保护，以及离线设备配置与遗留IoT SDK依赖加剧本地凭据硬编码。案例研究证明这些移动端密码学漏洞可绕过网络层防护，实现命令通道拦截和物理控制劫持。

Conclusion: 移动应用已成为网络物理系统中脆弱且被忽视的密码学信任边界，EAI特有的工程约束是导致密码学缺陷的结构性根源，而非随机开发者错误。

Abstract: Embodied AI (EAI) mobile applications are evolving from auxiliary user interfaces into active control-path components, directly linking mobile-side cryptographic security to cyber-physical trust. Despite this shift, existing security research predominantly focuses on embodied AI devices and cloud infrastructures, leaving the mobile control layer largely unexplored as a critical attack surface. To bridge this gap, we present the first large-scale measurement study of cryptographic misuse within the EAI mobile ecosystem. We construct EAIAppZoo, a benchmark of 507 real-world applications across six EAI domains, and employ an automated semantic-aware analysis pipeline to measure the prevalence and characteristics of five major cryptographic failure modes. Our measurement yields 12,975 misuse findings (with an evaluated precision of 80.74\%), revealing that these cryptographic failures are driven by EAI-specific engineering constraints rather than random developer errors. We uncover structural security trade-offs: latency-sensitive control paths systematically weaken transport protection, while the heavy reliance on offline device provisioning and legacy IoT SDKs exacerbates the local hardcoding of authentication credentials. Through real-world case studies, we demonstrate how these mobile-side cryptographic flaws bypass nominal network protections, enabling adversaries to intercept command channels and hijack the physical control of EAI entities. Ultimately, our findings highlight that mobile applications have become a fragile, yet overlooked, cryptographic trust boundary in cyber-physical systems.

</details>


### [155] [Accelerating Trust Convergence in IIoT: A ML Approach for Dynamic Network Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20214)
*Aymen Bouferroum,Valeria Loscri,Abderrahim Benslimane*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的信任收敛加速（TCA）方法，通过预测信任收敛所需时间单位并动态调整信任模型中的转移概率，在工业物联网（IIoT）环境中网络质量波动的条件下，实现了高达28.6%的信任收敛时间缩减，并提升了恶意节点场景下的信任评估准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信任模型在IIoT环境中忽略了网络质量波动对信任收敛的影响，导致收敛速度慢、评估不准确，亟需一种能适应动态网络条件的信任管理方案。

Method: 提出TCA方法，集成机器学习模型，基于关键网络指标预测信任收敛所需时间单位，动态调整信任模型中的转移概率以加速收敛；使用基于IEEE 802.11标准的真实Wi-Fi信道条件仿真框架验证效果。

Result: 在恶劣网络条件下信任收敛时间减少28.6%；在恶意节点场景中提升了信任评估的准确性，展现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TCA方法为IIoT系统提供了一种可扩展且自适应的信任框架，能在动态工业环境下保证稳健的性能表现。

Abstract: In Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) environments, trust management plays a vital role in securing systems, especially when dealing with resource-constrained devices. Traditional trust models often overlook the impact of fluctuating network quality, leading to slower trust convergence and inaccurate assessments. In this paper, we propose a dynamic trust management solution, known as the Trust Convergence Acceleration (TCA) approach, which integrates Machine Learning (ML) to accelerate trust convergence under poor network conditions. Our model predicts the number of time units needed for trust convergence based on key network metrics and dynamically adapts transition probabilities in the trust model to enhance convergence speed. Using a simulation framework that incorporates realistic Wi-Fi channel conditions based on the IEEE 802.11 standard, we demonstrate the effectiveness of the TCA-based approach, achieving up to a 28.6% reduction in trust convergence time under challenging conditions. Furthermore, the proposed solution exhibits resilience in scenarios involving malicious nodes, improving trust evaluation accuracy. This work provides a scalable and adaptive trust framework for IIoT systems in dynamic industrial environments, ensuring robust performance under varying network conditions.

</details>


### [156] [GNSS Spoofing Threat for V2X communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20215)
*Adolfo P. Jimenez,Juan Arquero-Gallego,Mario P. Luna,Jose E. Naranjo,Felipe Jimenez Alonso*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用低成本SDR（HackRF One）对GNSS信号进行物理欺骗的方法，并在真实V2X设备（Commsignia OBU/RSU）上成功验证了三种速度场景（90、145、200 km/h），证明V2X通信极易受到GNSS欺骗攻击而无法被检测到，导致服务降级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS信号在V2X领域提供关键的定位、导航和授时服务，但易受欺骗攻击。现有工作缺乏对真实V2X设备（OBU/RSU）进行物理层GNSS欺骗的实际验证，本文旨在填补这一空白，揭示V2X通信的安全性缺陷。

Method: 提出基于SDR的物理欺骗方法：坐标生成管道采用Haversine距离计算、时间离散化（模拟匀速运动）和线性插值，生成高保真GPS基带信号。使用HackRF One在Commsignia OBU和RSU设备上，对三种匀速轨迹（90、145、200 km/h）进行实验验证。

Result: 成功在真实V2X设备上实现了GNSS欺骗攻击，使得被攻击设备获取虚假位置信息，且攻击未被检测到，导致V2X服务降级。

Conclusion: V2X通信在GNSS层面缺乏安全防护，极易受到低成本SDR实现的欺骗攻击，且现有系统无法检测此类攻击。这一安全漏洞对V2X网络的可靠性和车辆安全构成严重威胁。

Abstract: Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS) constitute a core technology for delivering crucial positioning, navigation, and timing (PNT) services in the Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) domain, where they are indispensable for generating Cooperative Awareness Messages (CAM) that uphold network reliability and vehicular safety. Yet, GNSS signals are acutely exposed to spoofing, an advanced attack in which an adversary transmits crafted signals that replicate legitimate satellite characteristics, misleading the receiver into computing a false position. This work presents a methodology for conducting physical spoofing with inexpensive Software Defined Radio (SDR), describing a coordinate generation pipeline that employs Haversine-based distance calculations, temporal discretization to emulate constant velocity, and linear interpolation to produce high-fidelity GPS baseband signals. The proposed attack is experimentally validated on real Commsignia OnBoard Unit (OBU) and RoadSide Unit (RSU) devices using a HackRF One across three scenarios that emulate synthetic trajectories at steady speeds of 90 km/h, 145 km/h, and 200 km/h. The most significant contribution of this paper is the demonstration that V2X communications are not secured, as they are susceptible to GNSS spoofing attacks, which cause service degradation without being detected.

</details>


### [157] [TrustMix: How to Mix Messages in a Mobile Ad-hoc Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20251)
*Yu Shen,Aiswarya Walter,Stefanie Roos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrustMix是一个无需中心可信方的MANET混合网络协议，通过分组转发和可链接环签名实现匿名性与速率限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合网络主要为基础设施网络设计，无法直接应用于移动自组织网络(MANET)；而少数MANET解决方案需要预先知道拓扑结构或依赖可信中心方。

Method: 用户加入分组，消息通过多个分组转发；用户只需找到附近可信的节点转发消息至其分组，该分组内的所有节点共同参与洗牌打乱消息顺序，使原始消息与转发消息无法关联；使用可链接环签名实施速率限制，在不泄露身份的前提下检测超额发送行为。

Result: 在随机预言机模型下证明了协议安全性；通过现有混合网络模拟器评估显示显著提升消息匿名性；在5台Android设备上的概念验证实现达到了可接受的吞吐量。

Conclusion: TrustMix成功实现了无需中心可信方的MANET匿名通信，即使选择的节点是敌对的，也只有在整个分组都被攻陷时才能破坏匿名性。

Abstract: Mix networks are a highly effective way to achieve anonymity, defending against a wide range of traffic-analysis attacks. However, mix networks are usually designed for infrastructure networks and cannot be directly applied in the context of mobile ad hoc networks (MANETs). The few existing solutions for MANETs require advance knowledge of the topology or a trusted central party.
  In this paper, we present TrustMix, a mix protocol for MANETs that operates without any central trusted party. In TrustMix, parties join groups and then messages are forwarded via multiple groups to provide anonymity. With TrustMix, users only need to find a party nearby that they consider trusted. They then forward the message to this party's group, and the party shuffles messages before forwarding to other groups, meaning that the original message and the forwarded message cannot be linked. Furthermore, even if the chosen party is adversarial, they can only break the anonymity if all parties in their group are adversarial as all of them contribute to the shuffling. In addition to anonymity, TrustMix also enforces rate limits on the number of messages through the use of linkable ring signatures, which allows detecting that parties send more messages that allowed without revealing identities.
  We prove the security of our protocol in the random oracle model. We evaluate its anonymity using an existing mix-network simulator and show that TrustMix significantly improves message anonymity. Finally, we present a proof-of-concept Android implementation and show that TrustMix achieves acceptable throughput with 5 mobile devices.

</details>


### [158] [Quantization as a Malicious Task: Removing Quantization-Conditioned Backdoors via Task Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20254)
*Kaihsun Yang,Min-Yan Tsai,Chia-Mu Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出QVec方法，从参数空间视角防御量化条件后门（QCBs），通过量化前后权重差异识别恶意任务向量并进行参数修正，无需重训练、触发样本，仅需一次量化传递和轻量超参数搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对量化条件后门的防御方法通常需要修改量化过程或校正激活统计，引入额外计算开销或依赖特定量化设置，缺乏高效、通用的解决方案。

Method: 将全精度模型与量化模型的权重差异视为结构化行为偏移（恶意任务向量），通过受控的参数修正来抵消该恶意方向，仅需一次量化传递估计参数偏移和轻量超参数搜索。

Result: 在图像分类基准和多个大语言模型攻击场景中，QVec持续抑制后门激活，同时保持干净性能不退化。

Conclusion: QVec是一种无需重训练、无需触发样本、计算开销低的参数空间防御方法，能有效防御量化条件后门攻击。

Abstract: Model quantization is widely adopted to reduce memory usage and inference cost when deploying deep neural networks on resource-constrained devices. However, recent studies have revealed a new security threat known as Quantization-Conditioned Backdoors (QCBs), where a model behaves normally in full precision but activates malicious behavior only after quantization. Existing defenses typically modify quantization procedures or correct activation statistics, often introducing additional computational overhead or relying on specific quantization settings. Here, we present QVec, a parameter-space perspective for defending against QCBs. We observe that the weight difference between a full-precision model and its quantized counterpart encodes a structured behavioral shift, which can be interpreted as a malicious task vector rather than random quantization noise. Based on this insight, QVec counteracts this malicious direction through controlled parameter correction prior to deployment. QVec requires no retraining, no trigger samples, and only a single quantization pass to estimate the parameter shift, together with a lightweight hyperparameter search. Extensive experiments across image classification benchmarks and multiple Large Language Model (LLM) attack scenarios demonstrate that QVec consistently suppresses backdoor activation while preserving clean performance.

</details>


### [159] [LLM agent safety, multi-turn red-teaming, jailbreak benchmarks, adversarial robustness, safety-critical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20408)
*Hanwool Lee,Dasol Choi,Bokyeong Kim,Seung Geun Kim,Haon Park*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出NRT-Bench，一个用于多轮红队攻击LLM智能体（作为安全关键系统操作员）的基准测试平台，基于模拟核电站控制室环境。研究发现，自适应多轮攻击可稳定突破操作团队的安全极限（8.7%-12.1%的攻击会话导致安全功能丧失），且不同模型的脆弱性几乎不重叠，防御措施的效果高度依赖模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体正被越来越多地提议作为安全关键系统的监督组件，但其在面对持续、自适应对抗压力时的鲁棒性尚未得到充分表征。

Method: 构建NRT-Bench基准测试，包含：1）模拟核电站控制室环境，设有5个角色操作团队（每个由可配置LLM驱动）和6个关键安全功能（CSFs）；2）攻击者通过4个信道在多轮会话中注入消息，每轮有反馈；3）以CSF丧失作为客观危害信号（非LLM评判文本）；4）采用固定攻击配对回放协议评估4种前沿操作模型。

Result: 1）自适应多轮攻击可靠地将操作团队推过安全极限：4种模型下8.7%-12.1%的攻击会话导致CSF丧失；2）四种模型的失败样例几乎不重叠：149次攻击中无一次同时击败全部模型，但三分之一击败至少一个模型；3）防御效果高度依赖模型：同一防护栏或安全顾问智能体对一个模型降低攻击成功率，对另一个模型反而提升。

Conclusion: LLM智能体作为安全关键系统操作员的鲁棒性存在显著的模型特异性脆弱性，通用防御措施可能产生反效果，亟需更精细的评估框架和防御策略。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly proposed as supervisory components for safety-critical systems, yet their robustness under sustained, adaptive adversarial pressure remains poorly characterized. We present NRT-Bench, a benchmark for multi-turn red-teaming of LLM agents acting as operators of a safety-critical system, instantiated in a simulated nuclear power plant control room. A five-role operator team, each backed by a configurable LLM, runs a plant governed by six critical safety functions (CSFs), while adversaries inject messages over four channels in bounded multi-turn sessions with per-turn feedback. Harm is an objective signal rather than LLM-judged text: a run terminates the moment any CSF is lost, attributed to the causing message. Evaluating four frontier operator models under a fixed-attack paired-replay protocol, we find that adaptive multi-turn attacks reliably push the operator team past a safety limit: across the four models, between 8.7% and 12.1% of attack sessions end with the plant losing a critical safety function. Although the four models look almost equally robust by this aggregate rate, their failures barely overlap: of $149$ sessions, none defeat all four models while a third defeat at least one, so vulnerabilities are nearly disjoint across models rather than nested. The effect of added defences is strongly model-dependent: the same guardrail stack or safety-advisor agent that lowers attack success for one model can raise it for another. We release the simulation venue, attack dataset, and replay tooling for reproducible safety evaluation of LLM agents.

</details>


### [160] [Analyzing Defensive Misdirection Against Model-Guided Automated Attacks on Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20470)
*Reza Soosahabi,Vivek Namsani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过概率建模分析攻击-防御场景，指出传统的检测-拦截防御在足够查询预算下攻击成功率趋近于1，而检测-误导策略通过向攻击者法官注入假阳性错误，可显著降低并有界化攻击成功率。提出的CMPE方法在越狱基准测试中将攻击成功率上限降低两个数量级，几乎消除PAIR和GPTFuzz攻击的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Agentic AI系统依赖语言模型组件来解释指令、处理外部数据、调用工具和协调其他智能体，提示注入和越狱攻击的后果愈发严重。攻击者开始利用模型引导的自动化来扩展探测、提示优化和响应评估，迫切需要更有效的防御策略。

Method: 通过概率模型分析目标系统、防御机制和攻击者自动评判器之间的博弈关系。提出检测-误导策略，即在检测到恶意交互后返回受控的非操作性响应，诱导攻击者评判器产生假阳性错误。具体实现为CMPE（Contextual Misdirection via Progressive Engagement），一种轻量级对话误导方法，用安全但策略性误导的响应替代可预测的拒绝文本。

Result: 在越狱基准测试中，CMPE将攻击成功率上限降低最多两个数量级，并几乎完全消除了端到端PAIR和GPTFuzz攻击中的验证成功。

Conclusion: 检测-误导策略能够有效应对自动化越狱攻击，突破了传统检测-拦截防御在有限查询预算下逐渐失效的瓶颈，为Agentic AI系统提供了一种更鲁棒的安全防御方向。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems increasingly rely on language-model components to interpret instructions, process external data, invoke tools, and coordinate with other agents. These capabilities make prompt-injection and jailbreak attacks more consequential, especially as attackers adopt model-guided automation to scale probing, prompt refinement, and response evaluation. This work analyzes the resulting attack-defense setting through a probabilistic model of a target system, its defense mechanism, and the attacker's automated judge. Our analysis shows that conventional detect-and-block defenses can allow attacker success rate (ASR) to approach one as the query budget grows, since predictable refusals provide useful feedback to automated search. We then examine detect-and-misdirect, where detected malicious interactions receive controlled, non-operational responses designed to induce false-positive errors in the attacker's judge. This strategy reduces the positive predictive value of attacker-selected candidates and yields a bounded asymptotic ASR. We evaluate a proof-of-concept realization of this strategy through Contextual Misdirection via Progressive Engagement (CMPE), a lightweight conversational misdirection method designed to replace predictable refusal text with safe but strategically misleading responses in automated jailbreak settings. On jailbreak benchmarks, CMPE reduces estimated ASR upper bounds by up to two orders of magnitude and nearly eliminates verified attack success in end-to-end PAIR and GPTFuzz attack runs.

</details>


### [161] [A-COMPASS: Formal Foundations for Anonymity Analysis in Microdata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20492)
*Tamara Tagliavia,Silvia Ghilezan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出A-COMPASS语言，将原有的COMPASS语言从操作预处理后的分组表扩展到操作微数据表（一条记录对应一个人），并新增匿名化动作执行功能，同时提供了语法、语义及关键性质证明，支持k-匿名和l-多样性等隐私模型的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据隐私模型的正式验证仍不充分，COMPASS语言虽能指定匿名条件但仅适用于预处理后的分组表（一条记录对应一组人），无法直接处理更常见的微数据表形式（一条记录对应一个人），且缺乏匿名化动作执行能力。

Method: 对COMPASS语言进行修改，使其能够操作标准微数据表；定义A-COMPASS的语法和形式化语义；证明语义的关键性质（如确定性、组合性）；提供验证k-匿名和l-多样性等匿名属性的机制。

Result: 成功构建了A-COMPASS语言，能够处理微数据表并执行匿名化动作，证明了语义的确定性和组合性，实现了对k-匿名和l-多样性的形式化验证。

Conclusion: A-COMPASS扩展了COMPASS的适用范围，增强了隐私验证的表达能力和实用性，为微数据表上的隐私合规性验证提供了更直接的形式化工具。

Abstract: In the information age, one of the leading problems is how to ensure individual's privacy. Depending on the context in which privacy is considered, various data privacy models have emerged. However, the domain of formal verification of these models is still not sufficiently explored even when it comes to the most basic models. An attempt to verify privacy requirements is the Compliance Assertion Language (COMPASS). In COMPASS, one can specify an anonymity condition that a table needs to satisfy, and an action that will modify the table if the condition is not satisfied. It is designed to operate on preprocessed tables in a form one record - one group of people. In this paper, we modify the COMPASS language in order to operate on microdata tables in their usual form of one record - one person. The modified language is called A-COMPASS. Along with checking of previously applied anonymity conditions, A-COMPASS enables the execution of anonymization actions as a new feature. We further provide the syntax and the semantics for the A-COMPASS language. We also prove the most important properties of the introduced semantics like determinism and compositionality. Finally, we provide a mechanism to verify anonymity properties, such as k-anonymity and l-diversity.

</details>


### [162] [Calibration Without Comprehension: Diagnosing the Limits of Fine-Tuning LLMs for Vulnerability Detection in Systems Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20502)
*Arastoo Zibaeirad,Marco Vieira*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CWE-Trace框架，通过严格时间分割的数据集和两个诊断指标（DFI和HDD），系统评估了LLM在漏洞检测中的表现。研究发现：数据污染未带来可衡量的优势（84%的污染样本无可用的记忆信号），模型在历史数据和截止后数据上表现出稳定的系统性失败模式，微调仅改变输出阈值而未改变决策策略，最佳检测准确率仅52.1%，CWE精确排名Top-1低于1.3%，表明当前LLM缺乏可靠的系统软件安全推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对LLM在漏洞检测中是否真正具备安全推理能力存在争议，高分可能源于数据污染而非真实理解。作者旨在通过严格的实验设计（时间分割、诊断指标）来区分表现提升的来源是语义理解还是记忆效应。

Method: 构建CWE-Trace框架，包含834个手工整理的内核样本（覆盖74个CWE），实施严格的时间分割（历史集/泄露-free集），引入Directional Failure Index (DFI)和Hierarchical Distance and Direction (HDD)两个诊断指标，对8个基础LLM和15个LoRA微调变体进行非目标检测、目标检测和CWE分类评估。

Result: 1) 数据污染无优势：84%的污染样本无可用记忆信号，~31%的污染样本存在CWE错误分类；2) 骨干模型的方向先验主导微调效果：DFI在-85.5到+94.8个百分点范围内，模型表现出从历史数据到截止后数据一致的系统性失败模式；3) 微调仅调整输出阈值而不改变决策策略，是"无理解的校准"；4) 最佳检测准确率仅52.1%，CWE Top-1准确率低于1.3%。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在系统软件漏洞检测中缺乏可靠的安全推理能力，无论采用何种微调策略。检测与理解是解耦的能力，检测能力最弱的模型在粗粒度CWE分类上反而提升最大，进一步印证了能力解耦的观点。

Abstract: Whether LLMs scoring well on vulnerability benchmarks genuinely reason about security or merely pattern-match on contaminated data remains unresolved. We present CWE-Trace, a framework for LLM vulnerability detection built from 834 manually curated Linux kernel samples spanning 74 CWEs. The framework enforces a strict temporal split (pre-2025 historical set / post-cutoff leakage-free set), preserves context-aware vulnerable--patched pairs, and introduces two diagnostic metrics: the Directional Failure Index (DFI) and Hierarchical Distance and Direction (HDD). We evaluate eight vanilla LLMs and 15 LoRA fine-tuned variants across non-targeted detection, targeted detection, and CWE classification. Our analysis yields two key results. First, data contamination provides no measurable advantage. Function-level analysis shows that 84% of nominally contaminated samples carry no usable memorization signal: vulnerable functions are absent or cross-mapped across datasets, and ~31% of contaminated samples carry CWE misclassification. Second, backbone directional priors dominate fine-tuning. Models exhibit stable, systematic failure modes (DFI ranging from -85.5 to +94.8 pp) that persist from historical to post-cutoff data and resist correction. Fine-tuning shifts the output threshold without changing the decision policy. This is calibration without comprehension: output distributions adapt to training data while the underlying security reasoning remains absent. The weakest backbone at binary detection (DeepSeek-R1) gains the most in coarse CWE classification, revealing that detection and understanding are decoupled capabilities. The best detection score reaches only 52.1% (+2.1 pp above chance); exact CWE ranking remains below 1.3% Top-1 accuracy, confirming that current LLMs lack reliable security reasoning for systems software, regardless of fine-tuning strategy.

</details>


### [163] [Efficient and Sound Probabilistic Verification for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20510)
*Alaia Solko-Breslin,Pramod Kaushik Mudrakarta,Mihai Christodorescu,Somesh Jha,Krishnamurthy Dj Dvijotham*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对AI智能体在复杂数字环境中的安全需求，本文提出了一种基于分布鲁棒优化的运行时监控框架，能够在存在概率谓词和状态转移（如PII检测器有失败概率）且不依赖独立性假设的情况下，计算策略违反概率的严格上界，并在终端和工具调用智能体基准测试中优于现有方法，改善了安全-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Datalog的策略监控方法仅限于确定性策略，而实际AI智能体应用中常面临不确定性（如PII检测器有失败概率），且难以满足概率推理所需的独立性假设，因此需要一种能处理模糊性且不依赖独立性假设的鲁棒验证方法。

Method: 引入分布鲁棒优化（Distributionally Robust Optimization）框架，计算策略违反概率的严格上界（sound upper bounds），无论谓词之间可能存在何种相关性，都能保证概率上界的可靠性。

Result: 在标准终端和工具调用智能体基准测试上，该方法优于先前的工作，改善了安全性与效用之间的权衡，同时确保策略违反概率的严格界限。

Conclusion: 本文提出的分布鲁棒优化方法能够有效处理AI智能体安全监控中的概率不确定性，无需独立性假设，为复杂数字环境下的智能体安全验证提供了更实用且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Securing AI agents that operate in complex digital environments has become a critical need, and runtime monitoring approaches that formulate and enforce policies expressed in a formal language like Datalog offer a promising solution. However, existing approaches are restricted to deterministic policies. In many practical applications of AI agents, there is a need to enforce security policies in the face of ambiguity, leading to probabilistic predicates or state transitions (for example, a declassifier or Personally Identifiable Information (PII) detector that has some failure probability on each invocation). Furthermore, in many such applications, one cannot easily make the independence assumptions necessary to invoke prior work on probabilistic inference in Datalog. We address this by introducing a sound and efficient framework for such verification based on distributionally robust optimization, computing sound upper bounds on the probability of policy violation regardless of possible correlations between predicates. On standard benchmarks for terminal and tool calling agents, we demonstrate that our approach outperforms prior art and improves the security-utility trade-off while ensuring rigorous bounds on the probability of policy violation.

</details>


### [164] [Sovereign Execution Brokers: Enforcing Certificate-Bound Authority in Agentic Control Planes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20520)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sovereign Execution Broker (SEB)，一种运行时强制边界，将生产环境的变更权限从非确定性推理过程中分离出来，通过证书绑定的执行合约实现短生命周期、可撤销、可审计的运行时能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有访问控制机制授权身份，保证层认证提议动作，但两者均未在变更执行时刻提供强制性的认证权限执行点。生产环境中的变更权限不应驻留在非确定性推理过程中。

Method: SEB 作为运行时强制边界，消费 Sovereign Assurance Boundary (SAB) 签发的证书，验证请求变更与认证执行合约是否匹配，检查时间窗口、策略周期、撤销周期和实时状态漂移，生成作用域执行身份，调用基础设施 API，并记录签名的决策和结果记录。通过分离提议、准入和执行三个环节实现。

Result: 在 AWS 和 Kubernetes 集群上进行了原型评估，测量了延迟开销、撤销传播、漂移检测以及在故障注入下的安全性。

Conclusion: SEB 将认证权限转化为短生命周期、可撤销、可审计的运行时能力，前提是生产环境变更 API 拒绝非代理身份。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly connected to cloud, deployment, and data-control workflows, but production mutation authority should not reside inside non-deterministic reasoning processes. Existing access-control mechanisms authorize identities, while assurance layers certify proposed actions; neither alone provides a mandatory enforcement point for certified authority at the moment of mutation. This paper introduces the Sovereign Execution Broker (SEB), a runtime enforcement boundary for certificate-bound agentic infrastructure. SEB consumes certificates issued by the Sovereign Assurance Boundary (SAB), verifies that the requested mutation matches the certified execution contract, checks validity windows, policy epochs, revocation epochs, and live-state drift, mints scoped execution identity, invokes infrastructure APIs, and records signed decision and outcome records. By separating proposal, admission, and execution, SEB turns certified authority into a short-lived, revocable, auditable runtime capability, provided that production mutation APIs reject non-broker identities. We present the SEB execution model, certificate and replay-verification predicates, scoped identity semantics, bypass-prevention deployment patterns, failure behavior, and a concrete prototype implementation. We evaluate the prototype on AWS and Kubernetes clusters, measuring latency overheads, revocation propagation, drift detection, and security under fault injection.

</details>


### [165] [From Efficiency to Leakage -- Privacy Backdoor in Federated Language Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20553)
*Shanghao Shi,Chaoyu Zhang,Heng Jin,Yang Xiao,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik,William Yeoh,Ning Zhang,Y. Thomas Hou,Wenjing Lou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种名为NeuroImprint的隐私攻击方法，恶意参数服务器可以在联邦学习的参数高效微调(PEFT)过程中，将适配器隐秘地转化为隐私后门，通过为每个训练样本分配专用记忆神经元并限制其单步更新，实现客户端训练样本的高保真重建（59%-79%），且不降低模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习与参数高效微调（PEFT）结合的场景中，现有研究主要关注模型效用和通信效率，但恶意参数服务器是否能在不降低模型性能的前提下，从PEFT适配器中提取客户端隐私数据这一问题尚未被充分探索。作者旨在揭示这种隐蔽的隐私泄露风险。

Method: NeuroImprint攻击方法包含三个关键设计：(1) 为每个训练样本分配专用的记忆神经元；(2) 限制每个神经元在局部微调轨迹中最多更新一次，以避免跨样本碰撞和大批量、状态优化器（如Adam/AdamW）引入的跨步混合；(3) 微调后通过闭式解析方法逆变换隔离的每样本更新，恢复文本嵌入并确定性地映射回token序列。

Result: 在BERT、GPT-2、Qwen2和Llama3.2等多个语言模型以及四个不同领域数据集上的实验表明，NeuroImprint能够重建59%到79%的微调样本，且重建结果具有高语义保真度。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了联邦学习场景下PEFT适配器存在严重隐私安全隐患，恶意参数服务器可以在不降低模型效用的情况下实施隐私攻击，对当前联邦学习隐私保护机制提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables multiple parties to collaboratively fine-tune language models for domain-specific tasks without sharing raw data. Since full model fine-tuning is often prohibitively expensive for FL clients, parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has become the de facto approach in practice, freezing the base model and training only a small set of adapters. In this paper, we show that a malicious parameter server can stealthily corrupt a PEFT adapter into a privacy backdoor that implicitly memorizes the client's training samples as isolated per-sample parameter updates stored in separate neurons, without degrading model utility. Concretely, our attack, NeuroImprint, assigns a dedicated memorization neuron to each training sample and constrains that each neuron is updated at most once along the local fine-tuning trajectory. This design mitigates both cross-sample collisions and cross-step mixing introduced by large local batches and stateful optimizers (e.g., Adam/AdamW) in language-model fine-tuning. After fine-tuning, the resulting isolated per-sample updates can be analytically inverted in closed form to recover text embeddings, which are then deterministically mapped back to token sequences. To understand the generality of our method, we implemented NeuroImprint on multiple language models (BERT, GPT-2, Qwen2, and Llama3.2) and evaluated it across four fine-tuning datasets spanning diverse domains. The results demonstrate that our attack can reconstruct 59% to 79% of all finetuning samples with high semantic fidelity.

</details>


### [166] [Multi-View Decompilation for LLM-Based Malware Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20436)
*Bercan Turkmen,Vyas Raina*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出多反编译器提示方法，通过同时使用Ghidra和RetDec两种反编译器的伪C视图，提高了LLM在恶意软件二分类中的F1分数（主要提升召回率），实验表明不同反编译器提供互补证据，该方法无需训练即可提升实际场景中的恶意软件分类效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的反编译代码分类方法通常依赖单一反编译器视图，但反编译器是有损启发式工具，不同反编译器可能暴露同一二进制文件的不同伪影，单一视图的假设存在脆弱性。

Method: 构建包含良性工具和恶意程序的基准数据集，每个样本分别用Ghidra和RetDec反编译得到匹配的伪C视图，随后在多个主流LLM家族上比较单视图与多视图（两视图联合）的恶意软件二分类性能。

Result: 提供两种反编译器视图相比单一视图显著提升恶意类别的F1分数，主要提升体现在恶意样本的召回率上；一致性分析表明Ghidra和RetDec存在部分不同的错误模式，验证了反编译器输出提供互补信息。

Conclusion: 多反编译器提示是一种简单、无需训练的方法，可以有效改善LLM在实际恶意软件分类中的应用效果。

Abstract: Malware analysts often inspect compiled binaries through decompiled pseudo-C, when source code is unavailable. Recent work suggests that large language models (LLMs) can assist this process by classifying decompiled code as benign or malicious, but existing pipelines typically rely on a single decompiler view. We argue that this assumption is fragile: decompilers are lossy heuristic tools, and different decompilers can expose different artefacts of the same binary. We curate a benchmark of benign utilities and malicious programs spanning a range of threat behaviors. Each sample is compiled and decompiled with both Ghidra and RetDec, yielding matched pseudo-C views. Across a range of LLMs from major model families, we find that providing both decompiler views improves malicious-class F1, mainly by increasing recall on malicious samples. Agreement analyses further show that Ghidra and RetDec make partially different errors, supporting the view that decompiler outputs provide complementary evidence. Our results suggest that multi-decompiler prompting is a simple, training-free way to improve LLM-based malware triage in practical settings.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Variational Consensus Monte Carlo for Bayesian Mixture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19643)
*Julie Fendler,Francesca L. Crowe,Tom Marshall,Sylvia Richardson,Paul D. W. Kirk*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个在联邦学习框架下对贝叶斯混合模型进行推断的完整流程，采用共识蒙特卡洛方法，允许数据分布在多个节点上而无需共享原始数据，并扩展了变分CMC以处理过拟合贝叶斯混合模型，同时设计了适用于跨数据孤岛的聚类匹配算法和多种聚合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 健康数据具有隐私性、敏感性和共享限制，传统方法需要将数据集中处理，无法满足联邦学习场景下数据不共享的需求。因此，需要一种能够在数据分散存储时仍能有效推断贝叶斯混合模型的方法。

Method: (i) 将变分CMC扩展到过拟合贝叶斯混合模型，无需共轭先验即可推断聚类数量及所有模型参数；(ii) 提出适用于跨数据孤岛的新聚类匹配算法，处理局部数据集中未出现所有聚类的情况；(iii) 针对不同联邦学习约束设计了多种聚合策略；(iv) 提供实践选择指南。

Result: 综合仿真研究表明，当局部数据集的构成反映底层聚类结构时，该方法在恢复小聚类方面比标准MCMC应用于汇总数据更准确；并在大规模电子健康记录数据上成功识别了英国老年人群的多发病模式。

Conclusion: 所提出的联邦贝叶斯混合模型推断框架在保护数据隐私的同时，能够有效恢复聚类结构，尤其在小聚类发现方面优于传统集中式方法，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Motivated by the privacy, sensitivity and sharing limitations of health data, we present a comprehensive pipeline for inference of Bayesian mixture models within a federated learning setting, i.e. when data cannot be fully shared or pooled across compute nodes. We adopt a Consensus Monte Carlo (CMC) approach, in which an MCMC algorithm is run independently within each data silo to estimate local posterior distributions, which are then aggregated to approximate the posterior over the full data. The variational CMC approach of Rabinovich, Angelino and Jordan (2015) [1] frames the aggregation step as a variational inference problem, but their application to mixtures assumes the number of clusters and key mixture parameters to be known. Our main methodological contributions are: (i) an extension of variational CMC to over-fitted Bayesian mixture models that infer the number of clusters and all model parameters, without requiring conjugacy; (ii) novel cluster-matching algorithms suitable for cross-silo settings in which not every cluster appears in each local dataset; (iii) a number of inference strategies for the aggregation step, matched to different federated learning constraints; and (iv) guidelines for choosing among these in practice. A comprehensive simulation study validates the framework and allows us to compare to state-of-the-art federated learning alternatives. Notably, we show that when the composition of local datasets reflects the underlying clustering structure in the data, our approach can recover small clusters with greater accuracy than standard MCMC applied to the pooled data. We illustrate the framework on large-scale electronic health record data, identifying multi-morbidity patterns in a British geriatric population.

</details>


### [168] [AURA: Adaptive Uncertainty-aware Refinement for LLM-as-a-Judge Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19714)
*Zilong Zhang,Yi-Ting Hung,Weiyi He,Junxi Zhang,Lei Ding,Chi-Kuang Yeh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: AURA是一个自适应不确定性感知的改进框架，用于在有限人工验证下审计LLM作为评判者的成对比较决策，通过迭代学习人类一致性信号、传播可靠证据并优先处理不确定的比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审计流程通常假设有可靠的初始标注子集或干净的监督信号（如人工标注、启发式过滤或强评判者输出），但在LLM评估中这一假设脆弱：初始划分可能继承评判者偏差，而人工验证通常过于稀缺无法大规模定义稳定分组。

Method: AURA迭代地学习人类一致性信号，传播可靠证据，并优先选择不确定的比较进行人工审查。核心思想是将对评判者的信任度视为一个潜在变量，随着证据积累逐步精炼。包含紧凑的公式化描述、稳定的精炼过程。

Result: 在合成数据和真实成对LLM回答数据上进行了全面评估。

Conclusion: AURA能够在有限人工验证下有效改进LLM评判者的审计质量，通过自适应不确定性感知的精炼框架弥补人类监督的稀缺性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as judges for open-ended generation, as large-scale human evaluation is often expensive and difficult to scale, yet their preferences remain imperfect proxies for human judgment. Existing auditing pipelines often assume that a reliable subset of examples or clean supervision signals are available beforehand, for example from human annotation, heuristic filtering, or the outputs of strong judges. In LLM evaluation, this assumption is fragile: the initial split may inherit judge bias, while human verification is typically too scarce to define stable groups at scale. We propose AURA, an adaptive uncertainty--aware refinement framework for auditing pairwise LLM--as--a--judge decisions under selected human verification. AURA iteratively learns a human-consistency signal, propagates reliable evidence, and prioritizes uncertain comparisons for human review. The key idea is to treat trust in a judge as a latent quantity that is progressively refined as evidence accumulates. We provide a compact formulation, a stable refinement procedure, and a comprehensive evaluation on both synthetic and real pairwise LLM-answer data.

</details>


### [169] [Stochastic Linear Contextual Bandits with Bounded Noise: A Set-Membership Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20022)
*Haonan Xu,Yingying Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对随机线性上下文赌博机问题，在奖励噪声有界（而非传统次高斯假设）的条件下，提出SME-OFU算法，利用集员估计和乐观原则，实现了$O(\log T)$的遗憾界，并通过仿真验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLCB工作通常假设次高斯奖励噪声和有界期望奖励，得到$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$的最优遗憾界。但在许多实际应用中，观测到的奖励自然有界，即有界噪声比次高斯条件包含更多信息，却未被现有工作显式利用。

Method: 提出SME-OFU算法，采用集员估计（SME）进行不确定性量化，并应用乐观面对不确定性（OFU）原则来设计算法。

Result: 在奖励噪声有界的条件下，SME-OFU算法的遗憾界为$O(\log T)$，优于次高斯假设下的$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$。仿真结果表明SME-OFU在噪声有界时优于为次高斯噪声设计的基准算法。

Conclusion: 有界噪声假设比次高斯假设更强，因此$O(\log T)$的遗憾界并不与已有的$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$下界矛盾。将实际问题中天然存在的有界性显式纳入算法设计，可以显著提升理论保证和实际性能。

Abstract: This paper considers stochastic linear contextual bandits (SLCB) with bounded reward noise. Existing works typically assume sub-Gaussian reward noise and bounded expected rewards, under which the optimal regret bound scales as $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ in terms of horizon $T$. However, in many applications, realized/observed rewards are also naturally bounded, implying bounded reward noise. Bounded noise is more informative than the sub-Gaussian condition but has not been leveraged explicitly in the SLCB literature. In this paper, we propose a novel algorithm SME-OFU by utilizing an uncertainty quantification method called set-membership estimation (SME) and applying the principle of optimism in the face of uncertainty (OFU). Our algorithm enjoys an improved regret bound $O(\log T)$. Notice that this does not contradict the existing optimal bound $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ for sub-Gaussian noise because bounded noise is a stronger condition. Finally, simulations show empirical improvements of SME-OFU over a benchmark algorithm designed for sub-Gaussian noise when the reward noise is bounded.

</details>


### [170] [AK-MCS-C2 : Active Kriging Monte Carlo Simulation method with conformal certification for failure probability estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20191)
*Edgar Jaber,Vincent Chabridon,Mathilde Mougeot*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合主动克里金蒙特卡洛模拟与共形预测的主动学习框架，用于结构可靠性分析中的失效概率估计。采用自适应交叉共形策略和J+GP共形估计器，在少量样本下提供分布无关的预测误差保证，提高极限状态面附近样本分类的可靠性，尤其适用于稀有事件场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AK-MCS方法缺乏对预测误差的分布无关保证，导致在少量样本和稀有事件场景下失效概率估计的可靠性和鲁棒性不足。

Method: 将共形预测集成到主动克里金蒙特卡洛模拟框架中，采用自适应交叉共形策略（针对小样本和克里金代理模型），并使用J+GP共形估计器提供分布无关的预测误差保证。

Result: 在标准基准测试上的可复现数值结果表明，该方法在失效概率估计的准确性和鲁棒性上优于经典方法，尤其适用于稀有事件场景。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架通过共形预测提供了分布无关的误差保证，显著提升了主动学习在结构可靠性分析中的可靠性和效率。

Abstract: We introduce a novel active-learning framework for failure probability estimation in structural reliability analysis that integrates Active Kriging Monte Carlo simulation with conformal prediction. The proposed approach employs an adaptive cross-conformal strategy specifically designed for small-sample settings and kriging surrogate models using the J+GP conformal estimator. Unlike standard AK-MCS methods, the proposed framework provides distribution-free guarantees on prediction errors, leading to more reliable classification of samples near the limit-state surface. This improved uncertainty quantification enhances both the accuracy and robustness of failure probability estimates, especially for rare-event regimes where such efficiency is crucial. Reproducible numerical results illustrate the effectiveness of the method and also compare it to classical approaches on well-established benchmarks.

</details>


### [171] [Off-Policy Evaluation for Missingness-Aware Policies in MDPs with Rewards Missing Not at Random](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20206)
*Ziheng Wei,Annie Qu,Rui Miao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了离线强化学习中奖励因缺失非随机（MNAR）导致的评估偏差问题，提出利用未来状态作为影子变量识别条件均值奖励，并通过桥函数和极小极大估计方法恢复奖励，进而构建FQE风格的离线策略评估器，理论证明了一致性和有限样本误差界，实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离线强化学习中，日志批数据中的即时奖励常因记录稀疏、不规则或截断而缺失（MNAR），导致不可忽略性和选择偏差，传统方法无法直接应用。本文旨在解决MNAR下离线策略评估（OPE）的奖励恢复与无偏估计问题。

Method: 1) 形式化奖励依赖的倾向模型，利用未来状态作为影子变量识别全数据条件均值奖励；2) 引入桥函数恢复条件均值奖励，无需显式建模MNAR机制；3) 采用极小极大估计方法避免双重采样；4) 基于Fitted-Q-Evaluation框架传播恢复的奖励，允许目标策略依赖历史缺失指示符。

Result: 理论层面建立了OPE估计量的一致性及有限样本误差界；实验层面在模拟数据和MIMIC-III脓毒症数据集上，所提方法显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文为MNAR下离线策略评估提供了理论框架与实用方法，填补了奖励缺失非随机情况下OPE研究的空白，在医疗、营销等实际场景具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: In offline Reinforcement Learning, immediate rewards in logged batch data are often unobserved due to sparse or irregular record-keeping, or censored beyond certain reward values. This issue arises in practical settings, including health care and marketing. We investigate off-policy evaluation (OPE) in finite-horizon Markov decision processes when rewards are missing not at random (MNAR), which breaks ignorability and induces selection bias even after conditioning on states and actions. To address this, we formalize a reward-dependent propensity model and use future states as shadow variables to identify the full-data conditional mean reward. We further introduce a bridge function that recovers the conditional mean reward without explicitly modeling the MNAR mechanism, and estimate it via a min-max procedure to avoid double sampling. Building upon these identification results, we propose an Fitted-Q-Evaluation-style estimator that propagates the recovered rewards while allowing target policies to depend on past missingness indicators. Finally, we establish consistency and finite-sample error bounds for our OPE estimator, and show through experiments the strong performance of our method compared to existing methods on simulated and MIMIC-III Sepsis data.

</details>


### [172] [Statistical Properties of Training & Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20299)
*Itay Lavie,Noam Levi,Yonatan Kahn*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从物理学视角审视深度学习，探讨其与传统统计学的差异、神经缩放定律，以及物理问题中机器学习的约束与归纳偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习的成功挑战了许多经典统计学的直觉，作者希望从物理学视角系统理解深度学习的关键特征与令人惊讶的现象，并阐明模型构建中诸多选择的合理性。

Method: 采用物理学启发的视角，通过分析神经缩放定律等现象，讨论深度学习模型构建中的各种选择及其背后依据，特别关注物理应用场景下的约束与归纳偏差。

Result: 揭示了深度学习在性能上超越经典统计预期的关键特征；阐释了神经缩放定律与物理问题中归纳偏差之间的相互作用关系。

Conclusion: 从物理学角度为深度学习模型的构建提供了系统性的理解框架，有助于指导将机器学习应用于物理问题时的模型设计选择。

Abstract: Deep learning has managed to evade numerous intuitions from classical statistics to achieve unprecedented performance on a number of real-world tasks. In this article, we investigate the key features and surprises of deep learning from a physics-informed perspective, taking care to point out and justify where possible the many choices inherent in constructing a deep learning model. In particular, we review the phenomenon of neural scaling laws and discuss their interplay with the constraints and inductive biases which may be present when applying machine learning to problems in physics.

</details>


### [173] [SSH-Net: A Deep Neural Network for Predicting Failure Time Distribution Functions under Competing Risks with Application to GPU Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20451)
*Jie Min,Yueyao Wang,Mengkun Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构化的分段风险深度神经网络（SSH-Net），用于在存在竞争风险的情况下进行故障时间预测。该方法将神经网络结构与数据层级结构关联，通过独立的子网络处理不同协变量组，并基于竞争风险模型输出特定原因的风险函数，仿真和GPU数据验证了其预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂工程系统中存在竞争风险，深度神经网络虽然灵活但存在两方面问题：一是超参数调优困难；二是当系统具有多层层级结构时，将所有结构层次作为单一输入组可能丢失关键信息。

Method: 提出SSH-Net，将神经网络的子网络结构与数据的层级结构对应，不同协变量组通过独立子网络影响预测；基于特定原因竞争风险模型构建网络，输出特定原因风险函数；采用惩罚对数似然作为损失函数。

Result: 通过仿真研究（Brier分数、AUC、RMSE指标）验证预测精度，并在Titan GPU故障时间数据上展示了模型预测故障时间分布函数的能力。

Conclusion: SSH-Net有效解决了竞争风险下复杂分层结构系统的故障预测问题，在精度和结构化建模方面均表现良好。

Abstract: Competing risks are commonly observed in engineering fields and can bring challenges to time-to-event data modeling when the application scenarios are complicated. Recently, deep neural networks have received great attention for prediction with competing risks, due to their flexibility and high learning capability. However, the complexity of neural network structure brings extra difficulty in hyperparameter tuning based on different data inputs. Additionally, when an engineered system has complex physical structures with multiple hierarchical levels, treating all structural levels as a single group of inputs may fail to capture critical information. To address the issues, we propose a Structured Segmented Hazard Deep Neural Network (SSH-Net) for failure time prediction under cause-specific competing risks framework. Our approach associates neural network structure with data structures, and allows different covariate groups to impact the failure prediction through separate sub-networks. The neural network is constructed based on a cause-specific competing risks model. The SSH-Net outputs cause-specific hazard functions, and utilizes the penalized log-likelihood as the loss function. The prediction accuracy of SSH-Net is validated through simulation studies by evaluating the Brier score, the area under receiver operating characteristic curves (AUC), and the root mean square error (RMSE) of the predicted cause-specific cumulative incident function. We further demonstrate the model's ability to predict failure time distribution functions using the Titan GPU failure time data.

</details>


### [174] [The Representational Limit of Scalar Interactions: An Interventional Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19410)
*Potito Aghilar,Sabino Roccotelli,Stanislao Fidanza,Vito Walter Anelli,Sebastiano Stramaglia,Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明成对交互分数本质上是唯一性、冗余性和协同性的混杂，并提出Stochastic Hi-Fi——一种无需重新训练、基于干预掩码推断的预测可解释性分解方法，能够估计每个特征在唯一性、冗余性和协同性上的分布，在理论上有精确干预语义、有限样本蒙特卡洛界、耦合菱形采样方差缩减和均匀有限词汇收敛等保证，实验上在表格SCM、GPT-2 IOI电路和NIH ChestX-ray14上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成对交互评分方法（如Shapley-Taylor、Shapley Interaction）将三阶效应分散到成对标量中，无法区分唯一性、冗余性和协同性三种机制，导致解释混淆。

Method: 提出Stochastic Hi-Fi，一种后验且无需重新训练的预测可解释性分解方法，通过干预掩码推理为每个特征估计U/R/S分布。方法具有精确干预语义、有限样本蒙特卡洛界、耦合菱形采样实现严格方差缩减，以及均匀有限词汇收敛保证。

Result: 在表格SCM上，恢复结构的能力比标量基线高出最多411倍；在GPT-2 IOI电路中，成功分离出冗余头和协同头；在NIH ChestX-ray14上，Stochastic Hi-Fi在Pointing Game上匹配GradCAM，在Deletion AUC上大幅提升。

Conclusion: 成对交互分数本质上混淆了U/R/S三种机制，而Stochastic Hi-Fi能够有效解耦这些机制，在多种任务上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Signed pairwise interaction scores fundamentally conflate uniqueness (U), redundancy (R), and synergy (S). We prove this on a minimal 3-way XOR structural causal model: faithful indices such as Shapley-Taylor return zero per pair, whereas projective indices such as Shapley Interaction spread the third-order effect into pair scalars that conflate the three mechanisms. We introduce Stochastic Hi-Fi, a post-hoc, retraining-free predictability decomposition that estimates per-feature U/R/S profiles by interventional masked inference. The estimator provides exact interventional semantics, finite-sample Monte Carlo bounds, strict variance reduction from coupled diamond sampling, and uniform finite-vocabulary convergence. Across tabular SCMs, Stochastic Hi-Fi recovers structure missed by scalar baselines (up to 411x larger interaction-magnitude recovery ratios). It also separates redundant and synergistic heads in the GPT-2 IOI circuit. On NIH ChestX-ray14, Stochastic Hi-Fi matches GradCAM on Pointing Game and improves substantially on Deletion AUC.

</details>


### [175] [A Solver-Free Training Method for Predict-then-Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19587)
*Beichen Wan,Mo Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于测度变换原理的可扩展决策聚焦学习方法，在训练过程中完全不需要调用优化求解器，显著降低了计算成本，同时保持了与现有方法相当的决策质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在predict-then-optimize范式中，直接最小化经验决策遗憾是不可行的，因为决策映射是分段常数函数，梯度几乎处处为零。现有方法通过平滑微分过程来解决此问题，但每个梯度评估都需要调用计算昂贵的求解器，存在可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出基于测度变换原理的决策聚焦学习流水线，构建了一个在训练期间完全不需要优化求解器的新的替代损失函数。

Result: 该方法在决策质量上与最先进方法相当，同时将训练时间减少了数个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过理论保证（Fisher一致性、超额风险界）和实验验证，证明了所提方法在不牺牲决策质量的前提下，大幅提升了训练效率，解决了现有方法的可扩展性瓶颈。

Abstract: We propose a scalable method for training prediction (machine learning) models in the predict-then-optimize paradigm, where model outputs serve as coefficients for a subsequent linear optimization task. Directly minimizing the empirical decision regret is intractable for linear programming and combinatorial optimization since the decision mapping is piecewise constant, and the gradients are zero almost everywhere. While existing methods address this by smoothing the differentiation process, they suffer from scalability issues, since a computationally expensive solver call is required for every gradient evaluation. To address this, we propose a decision-focused learning pipeline based on a measure transformation principle, which yields a new surrogate loss that is completely optimization-solver-free during training. We establish theoretical guarantees, including Fisher consistency and excess risk bounds. Empirically, our method achieves decision quality competitive with state-of-the-art methods while reducing training time by orders of magnitude.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [A Laplace equation approach to the Behrens--Fisher problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19726)
*Nagananda K G,Jong Sung Kim*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出Behrens-Fisher问题的偏微分方程形式，通过正交分解将学生化均值差转化为球面楔概率，并导出基于Beta函数的精确有限样本分布表示，可在Excel等商业软件中直接实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决两独立正态样本方差未知且不等时均值比较的精确推断问题，克服传统近似方法（如Welch t检验）在有限样本下的精度局限。

Method: 采用偏微分方程框架，通过正交分解分离均值与残差分量，将学生化均值差的分布转化为球面楔概率的调和测度与Laplace-Dirichlet边值问题，进而导出Beta函数形式的闭式解。

Result: 获得了CDF和PDF的精确有限样本表示（仅依赖于样本量与方差比的Beta函数形式），建立了Gegenbauer分离变量展开（系数为Beta-Gamma闭式），并导出了带显式领头常数和高阶修正的尖尾展开。

Conclusion: 该框架将Behrens-Fisher分布纳入标准特殊函数形式，可在Excel等商业软件中直接计算，显著提升了分布评估与分位数计算的易用性。

Abstract: We develop a partial differential equation formulation of the Behrens-Fisher problem for two independent normal samples with unknown and unequal variances. An orthogonal decomposition separates mean and residual components (corresponding to the centered within-sample variation left after removal of the mean directions) and recasts the studentized difference of sample means as a scale-invariant geometric constraint. This reduction transforms the distributional problem into the evaluation of spherical wedge probabilities, which are identified with harmonic measure and with the value at the origin of a Laplace-Dirichlet boundary value problem. From this framework, we derive exact finite-sample representations for the cumulative distribution function and the probability density function in terms of beta functions, with dependence only on the sample sizes and the variance ratio. These representations place the Behrens-Fisher law in a standard special-function form that is directly accessible in widely available commercial software -- including Microsoft Excel -- thereby facilitating distributional evaluation and quantile computation. We also obtain a Gegenbauer separation-of-variables expansion for the associated harmonic extension and its threshold derivative, with coefficients in closed Beta-Gamma form, and derive sharp tail expansions with explicit leading constants and higher-order corrections.

</details>


### [177] [Private Rate-Double-Robust Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20427)
*Máté Kormos,Aad van der Vaart*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文通过局部隐私机制注入噪声保护个体隐私，同时实现目标参数的率双稳健推断。研究表明，合适的隐私机制能将敏感数据模型的半参数性质迁移到隐私环境，使率双稳健性得以保留，从而实现本地隐私保护下无偏且半参数有效的推断，并将非参数和参数 nuisance 估计器转化为具有相同收敛性质的隐私版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私保护（通过局部噪声注入）会阻碍推断，而率双稳健性有助于推断。本文旨在调和二者之间的矛盾，使得在保护个体隐私的同时仍能进行高效、稳健的统计推断。

Method: 1) 定义一类率双稳健目标参数，由无限维度线性部分和低维度非线性部分索引（包含因果参数）；2) 设计合适的局部隐私机制，将敏感数据模型的半参数性质迁移到隐私环境；3) 证明率双稳健性的迁移，从而实现本地隐私下无偏且半参数有效的推断；4) 将非参数 nuisance 估计器转化为隐私版本并保持收敛性质；5) 对参数 nuisance 模型，提出隐私矩估计方法及其大样本推断理论。

Result: 隐私机制成功迁移了率双稳健性，使得在本地隐私保护下仍能获得无偏、半参数有效的目标参数推断；非参数和参数 nuisance 估计器的隐私版本继承了非隐私版本的收敛性质。

Conclusion: 隐私保护与率双稳健推断可以实现调和，为隐私保护下的因果推断等应用提供了理论基础和方法框架。

Abstract: We reconcile privacy protection and rate-double-robust inference. The privacy of individuals is protected by a local privacy mechanism: injecting noise into their sensitive data, revealing only the noisy data for inference. Hence, privacy protection hinders inference. In contrast, the inference of a target parameter is rate-double-robust when the large-sample bias of an estimator of the parameter is characterised by a trade-off between the estimation errors of two other, nuisance, parameters. Hence, rate-double-robustness facilitates inference. Our starting point of reconciliation is a class of rate-double-robust target parameters indexed linearly by an infinite-dimensional and nonlinearly by a low-dimensional regression. Among others, this includes causal parameters. To infer these targets privately, we show how suitable privacy mechanisms transfer the semiparametric properties of the sensitive-data model to the private setting. Rate-double-robustness is transferred, enabling locally-private, unbiased and semiparametrically efficient inference of our target parameters. Finally, we transform general nonparametric nuisance estimators into private ones, which inherit convergence properties of their nonprivate counterparts. For parametric nuisance models, we develop a private method-of-moments estimator and its large-sample inference theory.

</details>


### [178] [Leveraging tails for adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20480)
*Sergios Agapiou,Ismaël Castillo,Paul Egels*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了非参数贝叶斯模型中具有p-指数尾先验的后验收缩率，发现收缩率随p减小而改善，且在p→0极限下可实现近乎最优的自适应平滑性。方法应用于白噪声回归中的级数先验和随机设计回归中的浅层ReLU神经网络，证明了过参数化浅层ReLU网络能自适应任意0≤β≤2的正则性，模拟结果与理论高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非参数贝叶斯模型中不同尾厚度的先验分布（尤其是p<1的重尾先验）对后验收缩率的影响，并验证其在神经网络等现代模型中的自适应能力。

Method: 理论上推导了具有p-指数尾先验的后验收缩率，并将其应用于白噪声回归中的级数先验和随机设计回归中的浅层ReLU神经网络，同时通过模拟研究进行实证验证。

Result: 收缩率随p减小而改善；在p→0极限下，可实现对平滑性的完全自适应（仅相差对数因子）；过参数化浅层ReLU网络能自适应0≤β≤2的正则性；模拟结果与理论预测高度一致。

Conclusion: 重尾先验（p小）能显著提升非参数贝叶斯收缩率，并在极限情况下实现近乎最优的自适应平滑性，特别是在浅层ReLU网络中展现出强大适应能力。

Abstract: We consider contraction of Bayesian posterior distributions in nonparametric settings where coefficients of a function over a basis or dictionary are given priors with $p$--exponential tails, including Laplace tails $(p=1)$ and heavier tails $(p<1)$. It is shown that contraction rates improve as $p$ decreases and that full adaptation to smoothness, up to logarithmic factors, is obtained in an appropriate $p\to 0$ regime. As applications, we consider both series priors in white noise regression and shallow ReLU neural networks in random design regression. In particular, we show that overparametrised shallow ReLU networks can adapt to any regularity $0\le β\le 2$. Through a simulation study, we show strong empirical agreement with the behavior predicted by our theory.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Computational Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19361)
*Lucius E. J. Bynum,Rajesh Ranganath,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"计算可识别性"框架，区别于传统理论可识别性，通过有限计算搜索程序在指定误差容限内寻找经验估计量，从而在有限样本、模糊图标准则、混合观测-干预数据等实际场景下实现因果效应的可识别性判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统理论可识别性依赖于渐近性质和无限数据等理想化条件，无法处理有限样本、模糊图标准则等实际场景下的识别问题。作者旨在弥合理论识别与实际统计推断之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出"计算可识别性"框架：定义一个有限计算搜索程序，在给定参数先验分布和搜索过程的条件下，若能在指定误差容限内找到经验估计量，则视为可识别。该方法通过计算实验而非纯数学推导来判定可识别性。

Result: 通过多个实验证明该框架能够回答细粒度的实用识别问题，包括：小有限样本下的识别、模糊图标准则下的识别、混合观测-干预数据的识别、以及反事实数据和估计量之间的识别。

Conclusion: 计算可识别性为实际因果推断提供了一种更灵活的识别判定方法，弥补了理论可识别性在实践中的局限，代码已开源。

Abstract: Identification conditions describe the computability of a target query or parameter of interest as a function of the type and amount of information available. In causal identification, this information is often expressed in the form of a causal graph, and data are observed or collected for some subset of variables in the graph. Target queries may be for a single effect alone or for a class of effects in a given model. The derivation of an identification algorithm then defines mathematically the process by which the desired causal effect(s) can be uniquely determined, theoretically, in expectation. Identifiability in expectation, or 'theoretical identifiability,' generally assumes asymptotic properties, infinite data, or other mathematically idealized conditions. In this paper, we explore a fundamental distinction between this theoretical, idealized notion of identifiability and a proposed alternative that is computation-bound. The framework we propose - 'computational identifiability' - is to instead define a finite computational search procedure for an empirical estimator. If this process finds an estimator empirically, within a desired error tolerance, then identifiability is satisfied, conditional on the specified assumptions of the search (i.e., a prior distribution over the parameters) and conditional on the search procedure itself. Through several experiments, we demonstrate how this framework allows us to answer fine-grained, practical identification questions, such as identification with small finite samples, with ambiguous graphical criteria, with mixed observational-interventional data, and across counterfactual data and estimands. Code is available at https://github.com/lbynum/metadentify.

</details>


### [180] [When to Trust, How to Distill: Multi-Foundation Model Guidance for Lightweight, Robust Scientific Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19363)
*Rupasree Dey,Abdul Matin,Nathan Orwick,Yao Zhang,Shrideep Pallickara,Sangmi Lee Pallickara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GUARD框架，通过门控不确定性感知路由蒸馏方法，从分布偏移的时序基础模型中提取知识，训练轻量级专用预测器，在四个气候关键领域取得了显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序基础模型在物理科学领域部署面临两难：零样本预测时存在严重的分布偏移，且计算成本过高无法部署于边缘传感器网络。核心挑战是如何从分布不对齐的基础模型中提取潜在结构知识，训练轻量级专用预测器。

Method: 提出GUARD框架，将多教师蒸馏重构为实例级决策过程，包含两个自适应机制：(1) 上下文路由器，基于局部输入统计动态选择最相关的教师模型，利用不同基础模型间的互补性；(2) 不确定性门控温度机制，作为"断路器"，在教师置信度与领域现实不一致时自动衰减蒸馏强度。

Result: 在气象学、生态系统碳通量、土壤湿度和能源网格四个气候关键领域上，GUARD显著降低了RMSE；即使教师模型因分布偏移零样本精度欠佳，仍能在28.5%的最难实例上超越全局最优的基础模型。

Conclusion: 分布不对齐的教师模型仍可作为关键修正信号，该方法可实现适用于资源受限边缘部署的高精度科学预测。

Abstract: The deployment of Time-Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) in physical sciences is hindered by a critical trade-off: while these models encode rich, universal temporal dynamics, they suffer from severe distributional misalignment when applied zero-shot to specific scientific domains, and their computational cost prohibits deployment in edge-computing sensor networks. We address a fundamental challenge: How can we extract latent structural knowledge from misaligned foundation models (FM) to train lightweight, specialized forecasters? We propose Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation (Guard), a novel framework that reframes multiteacher distillation as an instance-wise decision process with two adaptive mechanisms: (1) a Contextual Router that dynamically selects the most relevant teacher based on local input statistics, exploiting complementarity across diverse foundation models; and (2) an Uncertainty-Gated Temperature mechanism that acts as a "circuit-breaker," automatically attenuating distillation strength when teacher confidence diverges from domain reality. We evaluate our proposed lightweight framework on four climate-critical domains: meteorology, ecosystem carbon flux, soil moisture, and energy grids. Our method significantly reduces RMSE relative to a fixed-weight multi-teacher distillation baseline, successfully distilling knowledge from pretrained FMs (teachers) even when they exhibit suboptimal zero-shot accuracy due to distribution shift between the original and target data domains. We demonstrate that these domain-misaligned teachers can still serve as critical correctives, outperforming the globally superior FMs on 28.5% of the hardest instances. Ultimately, this enables high-precision scientific forecasting suitable for resource-constrained edge deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/RupasreeDey/GUARD-KDD2026.

</details>


### [181] [Closing the Social-Semantic Gap: SPSD for Edge-Based Prompt Compression in Cloud LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19364)
*Abhinit Sen,Ajeet Kumar,Manaranjan Pradhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPSD（情感保持语义蒸馏）方法，在边缘设备使用4-bit量化小语言模型压缩用户提示词后再传输给云端大语言模型，在保持响应质量非劣效的前提下实现每调用平均节省99.9个输入token，估计节能70-270μWh。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理的prefill阶段日益成为云端能耗的主要来源，而用户提示中大量社交性填充语（礼貌用语、道歉性开场白、重复话、建立关系语言）对人类交流重要但对机器推理信息密度低，存在"社交-语义鸿沟"，因此需要在不损害响应质量的前提下压缩提示词以减少云端计算开销和能耗。

Method: 提出SPSD管道：在边缘设备部署4-bit量化小语言模型（Gemma-2-2B-Instruct Q4_K_M）对用户提示进行压缩蒸馏，保留情感语义后传输至云端大语言模型（Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct）。安全相关领域通过规则门控路由至原始透传模式。

Result: 在248条提示词语料上评估：每次蒸馏调用平均节省99.9个输入token，146次蒸馏调用全部为正节省；盲评中43%平局、28%蒸馏胜出、29%原始胜出，在15分制下1分非劣效边界内蒸馏路径不劣于原始路径；余弦相似度均值0.682，中位数0.712，54.1%对超过0.70参考阈值；每调用净节能估计70-270μWh。

Conclusion: SPSD证明设备端提示词蒸馏可以在实际可接受的非劣效边界内减少云端LLM输入token成本，同时保持响应质量，为降低大规模LLM推理的能源开销提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The prefill stage of Large Language Model (LLM) inference is a growing contributor to cloud-scale energy cost. Many consumer-support and conversational prompts contain social scaffolding: politeness markers, apologetic preamble, repetition, and rapport-building language that is important for human communication but carries low marginal information for machine reasoning. We call this discrepancy the Social-Semantic Gap. We present SPSD (Sentiment Preserving Semantic Distillation), an edge-based pipeline that compresses user prompts using a 4-bit quantised Small Language Model before transmission to a cloud-deployed LLM. Evaluation on a 248-prompt corpus using Gemma-2-2B-Instruct (Q4_K_M) as the SLM and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct as the cloud evaluation model yields a mean input token saving of 99.9 tokens per distilled call, with all 146 distilled calls yielding positive savings. Response quality, assessed by blind LLM-as-judge scoring across 121 pairs, is non-inferior to the raw path within a pre-specified 1-point margin on a 15-point rubric; the judge awarded 43 percent ties, 28 percent distilled wins, and 29 percent raw wins. Cosine similarity is mixed: mean 0.682, median 0.712, with 54.1 percent of pairs above the 0.70 reference threshold. Safety-critical domains are conservatively routed to passthrough via rule-based gates. Per-call net energy saving is estimated at 70-270 uWh under stated assumptions. SPSD shows that on-device prompt distillation can reduce cloud LLM input-token cost while preserving response quality within a practical non-inferiority margin.

</details>


### [182] [Performance Analysis and Optimization of 3D Generative Diffusion Models across GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19365)
*Jeeho Ryoo,Yongchan Jung,Muhammad Ali Khaliq,Weidong Zhang,Jiatong Han,Byeong Kil Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对Med-DDPM扩散模型在三维MRI合成中的GPU性能进行了全面分析，揭示了训练阶段cuDNN卷积和隐式GEMM内核主导的计算瓶颈，并提出TF32张量核心激活和3D channels-last布局两种架构感知优化，在A100上实现SM周期和动态指令减少高达100倍，张量核心利用率提升至9.98倍，IPC提升7%，且不损失合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在高质量3D MRI合成中效果显著，但每样本需数百次U-Net评估导致GPU资源需求巨大，且内核行为高度异构，现有研究缺乏对医学扩散模型在GPU架构层面的系统性性能分析。

Method: 在NVIDIA三代架构上对Med-DDPM进行内核级运行时分解、指令混合特征、内存系统利用率、线程束级活动及分析器优先级评分估计的全面性能分析，并评估TF32张量核心激活和3D channels-last布局两种架构感知优化。

Result: 训练主要由cuDNN卷积和隐式GEMM内核主导，瓶颈源于内存访问模式、张量布局转换及张量核心利用率不足；两种优化在A100上实现SM周期减少100倍、动态指令减少100倍、张量核心利用率从1.45提升至9.98倍、IPC提升7%。

Conclusion: 架构感知优化可显著提升医学扩散模型的GPU计算效率，且不牺牲合成质量，为医疗影像领域的扩散模型高效部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become essential for high-fidelity 3D MRI synthesis, yet their deployment remains constrained by substantial GPU resource demands arising from hundreds of U-Net evaluations per sample and a highly heterogeneous kernel behavior. This paper performs a comprehensive performance analysis of the state-of-the-art medical diffusion model, Med-DDPM, across three generations of NVIDIA architectures to study kernel-level runtime breakdowns, instruction-mix characteristics, memory system utilization, warp-level activities, and profiler priority-score estimates. We show that training is overwhelmingly dominated by cuDNN convolution and implicit-GEMM kernels, with inefficiencies arising from memory-access patterns, tensor-layout conversions, and limited Tensor Core utilization. Guided by these insights, we evaluate two architecture-aware optimizations TF32 Tensor Core activation and a 3D channels-last layout and demonstrate that they reduce SM cycles by up to 100x, cut dynamic instructions by 100x, raise Tensor Core utilization from 1.45 to 9.98x, and increase IPC by 7% on A100, all without degrading synthesis quality.

</details>


### [183] [Information Lattice Learning as Probabilistic Graphical Model Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19366)
*Haizi Yu,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了信息格学习（ILL）与概率图模型（PGM）之间的深层联系，证明ILL学习的概率规则可解释为基于商变量的约束因子图，其提升过程等价于最大熵重建，从而为符号-概率混合学习提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立ILL与概率图模型之间的正式联系，阐明ILL学习的概率规则在PGM框架下的自然解释，为ILL提供更坚实的理论根基并开辟新方向。

Method: 通过将ILL中的划分视为确定性商变量、规则视为商变量的边缘分布、一般提升视为满足约束的联合分布族、特殊提升（L2均匀性）视为最大熵重建，建立ILL与对数线性因子图的等价关系，并区分信息格与贝叶斯网络的本质差异。

Result: ILL学习的规则集等价于一组可解释抽象上的边际约束；在香农熵提升下，相同约束产生以学习到的抽象为索引的对数线性因子图；信息格编码的是抽象的精化与粗化关系，而非条件依赖关系。

Conclusion: ILL应被理解为面向可解释约束因子图（基于商变量）的结构学习方法，而非传统贝叶斯网络；这一视角为推理、可辨识性和符号-概率混合学习指明了新方向。

Abstract: Information lattice learning (ILL) learns interpretable rules of a signal by alternately projecting the signal onto a partition lattice that encodes a hierarchy of abstractions and lifting selected rules back to the signal domain. When the signal is a probability mass function, we show the probabilistic rules learned by ILL admit a natural probabilistic graphical model (PGM) interpretation and develop this interpretation in detail. A partition in ILL induces a deterministic quotient variable, and a rule is the marginal law of that quotient variable. A rule set is therefore a collection of marginal constraints over interpretable abstractions. General lifting is the feasible family of all joint distributions satisfying those constraints, while special lifting chooses a maximum-ignorance reconstruction, implemented in ILL by an L2 uniformity principle closely related to maximum entropy. Under a Shannon-entropy lifting, the same constraints yield a log-linear factor graph whose factors are indexed by learned abstractions. The information lattice itself, however, is not a Bayesian network: its edges encode refinement and coarsening of abstractions, not conditional dependence. Thus ILL is best viewed as structure learning for interpretable constraint-based factor graphs over quotient variables. This view clarifies how ILL relates to graphical models and maximum entropy models, while suggesting new directions for inference, identifiability, and hybrid symbolic-probabilistic learning.

</details>


### [184] [Weibull Weight-Scale Parameter Evolution under AdamW Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19367)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于双参数Weibull框架诊断Transformer权重分布，发现AdamW训练中权重尺度参数λ(t)经历增长、过冲和松弛三个阶段。通过推导AdamW更新的三力分解（对齐力、注入力、衰减力），揭示对齐力主导增长阶段（占绝对力预算88-94%），而与衰减力在饱和期达到平衡，解释了λ(t)的动态转变。提出样条位移法从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力，准确率约92-94%，并观察到λ(t)峰值与训练数据一致性相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdamW训练过程中Transformer权重分布参数λ(t)为何呈现增长、过冲和松弛的非单调动态行为，并建立可解释的力学分析框架。

Method: 基于双参数Weibull框架，从AdamW更新推导出平方权重范数的三力分解（对齐力、注入力、衰减力）；在自训练的Pythia-70M模型上使用真实优化器矩验证；提出样条位移法从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力。

Result: 对齐力贡献增长阶段88-94%的绝对力预算，对超权重移除鲁棒；样条位移法恢复对齐力准确率约92-94%，约为朴素两点基线的两倍；RMS-to-Weibull重构偏移约5-6%；λ(t)峰值与训练数据一致性相关。

Conclusion: 三力分解成功解释了λ(t)的动态行为，样条位移法使得该方法可应用于真实模型（优化器矩不可用的情况），数据一致性对权重尺度的影响留待后续研究。

Abstract: Building on a two-parameter Weibull framework for diagnosing transformer weight distributions, we study why the Weibull weight-scale parameter $λ$ grows, overshoots, and then relaxes during AdamW training. We derive a leading-order three-force decomposition of the squared weight norm from the AdamW update: an alignment force measuring the correlation between weights and the adaptive update direction, an injection force from adaptive step magnitude, and a decay force from decoupled weight decay. On self-trained Pythia-70M models with ground-truth optimizer moments, alignment dominates the rise phase, contributing 88-94% of the absolute force budget across four random seeds and remaining robust to super-weight removal. Near saturation, alignment and decay approach balance, explaining the transition from weight-scale growth to relaxation. These force dynamics directly govern the squared-norm component underlying $λ(t)$; the remaining RMS-to-Weibull reconstruction offset is measurable and decomposes into bridge and integration components, totaling approximately 5-6% in densely sampled regions. To extend the analysis to real models where optimizer moments are unavailable, we introduce a spline displacement method that recovers the alignment force from sparse checkpoints with approximately 92-94% accuracy, about twice the naive two-point baseline. We further observe that the peak value of $λ(t)$ varies with training-data coherence in our experiments, suggesting a data-dependent component of weight-scale growth that we leave to a controlled follow-up study. Code and data are available at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public.

</details>


### [185] [Zero-Inflated Gaussian Distributions Enable Parameter-Space Sparsity in Estimation-of-Distribution Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19369)
*Andreas Faust,Sven Nitzsche,Juergen Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多变量零膨胀高斯分布作为估计分布算法（EDA）的采样律，首次将EDA推广到稀疏黑箱优化问题，无需手工设计稀疏算子，能联合优化稀疏模式与活动值，在Lunar Lander基准上表现优于密集高斯EDA、手工稀疏进化算法和临时稀疏EDA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EDA在连续参数空间表现良好，但无法处理大多数系数恰好为零的稀疏参数空间。现有的稀疏黑箱优化器重新引入了手工设计的稀疏算子、双层方案、零阈值等EDA本应避免的偏差性假设。本文旨在填补这一空白，将EDA推广到稀疏场景。

Method: 提出多变量零膨胀高斯（ZIG）分布作为EDA的采样律。ZIG分布通过潜在高斯模型分别建模指示维度和取值维度，从而表示稀疏模式、活动参数间的相关性以及二者的交互作用。引入实用的基于摊销逆推的估计器来估计模型潜在参数，这些估计器能准确恢复潜在相关结构。

Result: 在Lunar Lander基准上，ZIG-EDA收敛速度更快，最终回报更高，优于密集高斯EDA、手工稀疏进化算法和临时稀疏EDA，同时找到的控制器仅需少量参数处于活动状态。估计器能准确恢复潜在相关结构，且潜在参数具有可辨识性。

Conclusion: 多变量零膨胀高斯分布有效将EDA推广到稀疏黑箱优化，实现了稀疏模式与活动值的无层次联合优化，避免了手工设计稀疏算子带来的偏差，为稀疏参数空间的进化优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Estimation-of-distribution algorithms (EDAs) are a powerful class of evolutionary methods for black-box optimization, especially when little is known about the structure of the objective. Whereas classical evolutionary algorithms rely on hand-designed mutation and crossover operators, hard to devise for unknown problem structures, and a source of bias, EDAs sidestep operator design entirely: they fit a probability distribution to the best individuals and sample the next generation from it. EDAs are well established on continuous parameter spaces, but they have not previously been generalized to sparse ones, in which most coefficients of a good solution are exactly zero. Existing sparse black-box optimizers therefore reintroduce exactly what EDAs were designed to avoid: hand-crafted sparsity operators, bi-level schemes alternating between support set and active values, zeroing thresholds, and other baked-in assumptions. We close this gap by proposing multivariate zero-inflated Gaussian (ZIG) distributions as EDA sampling laws. A latent Gaussian model with separate indicator and value dimensions represents sparsity patterns, correlations among active parameters, and the interactions between the two, so sparsity patterns and active values are optimized jointly, hierarchy-free. We show that the latent parameters of this model are identifiable from observed samples, unlike in the missing-data settings where related constructions originate, and introduce practical amortized inversion-based estimators for them. The estimators accurately recover latent correlation structures, and on the Lunar Lander benchmark the resulting ZIG-EDA converges faster and reaches higher final returns than a dense Gaussian EDA, a hand-crafted sparse evolutionary algorithm, and an ad-hoc sparse EDA, while finding controllers with only a small fraction of parameters active.

</details>


### [186] [Human-like autonomy emerges from self-play and a pinch of human data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19370)
*Daphne Cornelisse,Julian Hunt,Zixu Zhang,Waël Doulazmi,Kevin Joseph,Jaime Fernández Fisac,Eugene Vinitsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合自对弈强化学习与少量人类演示（仅30分钟）的驾驶策略训练方法，在保持自对弈高效性的同时，通过将人类数据作为正则化目标，解决了纯自对弈产生的与人类驾驶习惯不兼容的问题，仅需单GPU训练15小时即可实现与人机协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯自对弈强化学习训练的驾驶策略会学习高效但不符合人类驾驶习惯的"异类"行为，而传统解决方法（奖励工程、域随机化）脆弱且劳动密集。作者希望在不完全抛弃人类演示的前提下，用极少量人类数据来对齐策略行为。

Method: 将人类演示作为正则化目标，叠加在最小化安全到达目标的奖励之上。采用自对弈强化学习作为主体框架，仅使用30分钟（约2500倍少于模仿学习）的人类演示数据。

Result: 训练出的策略能够与人类轨迹协调交互，训练时间仅15小时（单消费级GPU），且数据效率极高。

Conclusion: 少量人类数据（如同调味料）就能有效解决自对弈策略的行为对齐问题，在保持自对弈扩展性的同时大幅降低对人类数据的依赖。

Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning has recently emerged as a way to train driving policies without any human data. It uses cheap, large-scale simulations to substitute expensive, large-scale human driving demonstrations. A key limitation of this approach is that policies trained through pure self-play can learn effective but alien driving conventions incompatible with people. Previous works attempt to mitigate such behavioral misalignments through extensive reward engineering and domain randomization, which are brittle and labor-intensive. Instead of completely discarding human demonstrations, our method treats them as a regularization objective on top of a minimal safe goal-reaching reward. Like the spice in a good stew, we find that a little human data goes a long way: our method uses only 30 minutes of human demonstrations, 2500x fewer than comparable imitation learning approaches. Resulting policies coordinate with held-out human trajectories and complete training in 15 hours on a single consumer-grade GPU. Videos and full source code are available at https://spiced-self-play.com/.

</details>


### [187] [ProMUSE: Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty-guided Staged Evidential Alzheimer Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19371)
*Long Doan,Branden Chen,Ethan Litton,Huan Huang,Jiajing Huang,Yixin Xie,Weihua Zhou,Nandakumar Narayanan,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProMUSE提出了一种渐进式多模态不确定性引导的分阶段证据网络，通过自适应判断何时需要额外影像模态（MRI/PET），在保持诊断准确性的同时减少50-90%的高成本影像数据使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病早期诊断依赖于多模态数据（临床评估、MRI、PET），但MRI/PET采集成本高且不易普及，导致全模态推断在临床实践中不切实际。

Method: 首先使用低成本的临床数据进行证据分类，通过基于Dirichlet的主观逻辑模型量化不确定性；当不确定性超过学习阈值时，逐步引入MRI或PET特征，利用Dempster-Shafer理论融合模态级置信度和不确定性，得到校准的多模态预测。

Result: 在ADNI、AIBL、OASIS数据集上的CN-AD、CN-MCI和MCI-AD任务中，ProMUSE实现了与全模态基线相当或更优的准确率，同时将MRI/PET使用量减少了50-90%。

Conclusion: ProMUSE是一种实用、不确定感知且资源高效的阿尔茨海默病筛查方案，能显著降低临床工作流中的数据采集成本。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a fatal disorder that destroys memory and cognitive skills in the elderly population. Most treatments for AD are effective in the early stage, leading to an increasing demand for early AD diagnosis. AD diagnosis increasingly relies on multimodal data such as clinical assessments, structural Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI), and Positron Emission Tomography (PET) imaging. However, MRI and PET acquisition remain costly and not universally accessible, making full-modality inference impractical in real-world clinical workflows. We propose ProMUSE, a Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty Guided Staged Evidential Network that adaptively determines when additional modalities are necessary, helping reduce the overall cost of data acquisition while maintaining accuracy. ProMUSE first performs evidential classification using low-cost clinical data and quantifies uncertainty via a Dirichlet-based subjective logic model. When uncertainty exceeds a learned threshold, ProMUSE progressively incorporates MRI or PET features, fusing modality-wise belief and uncertainty through Dempster-Shafer theory to obtain a calibrated multimodal prediction. This staged acquisition strategy enables accurate diagnosis while minimizing reliance on expensive imaging. Experiments on ADNI, AIBL, and OASIS across CN-AD, CN-MCI, and MCI-AD tasks demonstrate that ProMUSE achieves competitive or superior accuracy compared to full-modality baselines while reducing MRI/PET usage by 50-90%, yielding substantial cost savings. These results highlight ProMUSE as a practical, uncertainty-aware, and resource-efficient solution for real-world AD screening.

</details>


### [188] [cAPM: Continual AI-Assisted Pace-Mapping with Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19373)
*Dylan O'Hara,Pradeep Bajracharya,Casey Meisenzahl,Karli Gillette,Anton J. Prassl,Gernot Plank,Saman Nazarian,Roderick Tung,John L Sapp,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了cAPM，一种持续主动学习AI辅助起搏标测方法，通过任务无关的代理神经网络、主动学习策略和持续学习策略，在保持先前目标知识的同时，实现跨多次室性心动过速起搏标测任务的知识迁移，将定位精度提升至81%（5mm精度）仅需4.5个起搏位点，远优于现有方法的38%和13.7个位点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动学习AI模型在每次室性心动过速（VT）标测任务中需重新训练，无法在同一个患者或不同患者的多个VT之间迁移知识，导致标测效率低下、所需起搏位点数量过多。
