Abstract: Lifelong knowledge editing aims to efficiently and sequentially update language models over time, as new knowledge becomes available or when the model makes mistakes, while preserving acceptable performance on past knowledge. One unresolved challenge is that existing methods modify a fixed set of layers for all new knowledge samples, reducing flexibility and increasing catastrophic forgetting. Another is requiring access to previous knowledge and extensive pre-processing to obtain data statistics. To address these challenges, we introduce LOKI, a novel approach that uses dynamic layer selection based on the Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion and projects gradient updates onto the null-space of the model weights, bypassing the requirement for previous knowledge access. We show that LOKI achieves superior performance to existing approaches across a wide variety of experiments, achieving up to a 14\% improvement in average accuracy.

</details>


### [225] [Multi-Granular Attention-Driven Reinforcement Learning Framework for Web Intelligent Enhancement Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19690)
*Navin Chhibber,Deepak Singh,Anokh Kishore,Nikita Chawla,K. Anguraj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多粒度注意力增强的强化学习网络智能增强系统(MGAR-WIES)，通过语义图建模、注意力机制和自适应强化学习，在异构动态Web数据上实现个性化服务，准确率达80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习、深度学习和强化学习模型在不断演化的Web环境中存在语义理解不足、适应性和可扩展性差的问题，难以处理异构动态Web数据以提供个性化上下文感知服务。

Method: 首先收集异构Web数据并进行预处理生成统一特征表示，然后构建动态语义图并通过注意力机制增强的图嵌入捕获局部相关性和全局上下文依赖，接着采用自适应多智能体强化学习策略优化个性化Web行为，最后集成连续在线反馈实现实时更新和持续适应。

Result: 在准确率指标上达到80%，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: MGAR-WIES通过融合语义图建模、注意力机制和自适应强化学习，有效提升了Web智能增强系统在动态环境中的语义理解能力、适应性和可扩展性，为个性化Web服务提供了有效方案。

Abstract: From the past few years, web intelligent enhancement systems increasingly rely on heterogeneous and dynamic web data to deliver personalized, context-aware services. However, traditional machine learning, deep learning, and reinforcement learning models often struggle with semantic understanding, adaptability, and scalability in continuously evolving web environments. In this research, a Multi-Granular Attention-based Reinforcement Web Intelligent Enhancement System (MGAR-WIES) is proposed to address the challenges by integrating semantic graph modeling, attention mechanisms, and adaptive reinforcement learning. Initially, heterogeneous web data comprising structured, semi-structured and unstructured sources are collected and preprocessed for generating unified feature representations. These representations are transformed into a dynamic semantic graph, where entities and their relationships are modeled by using graph embeddings enhanced by attention mechanisms for capturing both local relevance and global contextual dependencies. Subsequently, an adaptive multi-agent reinforcement learning strategy leverages the attention-aware semantic states to optimize personalized web actions like content recommendation, navigation optimization, and service adaptation. Finally, the continuous online feedback is further integrated to update graph representations and learning policies in real time by ensuring sustained adaptability and performance. The proposed MGAR-WIES acheived better results in terms of accuracy (80%) when compared with existing approaches.

</details>


### [226] [Efficiently Representing Algorithms With Chain-of-Thought Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19697)
*Yanhong Li,Anej Svete,Ashish Sabharwal,William Merrill*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，链式思维（CoT）Transformer可以在仅有多对数开销的情况下高效模拟Word RAM算法（如排序、Dijkstra算法），这一效率远超模拟图灵机所需的二次开销。研究覆盖了有限精度Transformer、连续CoT以及混合架构（Transformer+线性RNN）三种设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论表明CoT Transformer可以模拟图灵机从而执行任意计算，但图灵机模型不适合讨论算法效率——Word RAM模型（随机访问内存、单位成本操作）才是算法设计和分析的标准抽象。因此核心问题是：CoT Transformer能否高效模拟Word RAM算法？

Method: 论文在三种设置下展开：1）有限精度Transformer（多对数宽度、最右唯一硬注意力）；2）连续CoT（推理形式为向量而非token）；3）混合架构（Transformer层叠加线性RNN层）。通过在Word RAM指令集上构建CoT模拟器，分析了不同指令集（平坦指令集、无乘法平坦指令集）下的模拟开销。

Result: CoT Transformer可以仅用多对数开销模拟任意Word RAM算法；平坦指令集下开销降至对数平方；无乘法平坦指令集下仅需对数开销。这与模拟图灵机所需的二次开销形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: CoT Transformer在算法效率模拟上远优于以往认为的图灵机等价性结论，其实际计算能力更接近Word RAM模型，这为推理模型的实用性提供了更强的理论支撑。

Abstract: The increasing popularity of \emph{reasoning} models -- language models that output a series of reasoning or thought tokens before producing an answer -- is justified, in part, by theoretical results showing that chain-of-thought (CoT) transformers can simulate Turing machines, and thus perform arbitrary computation. However, the Turing machine, while suitable for complexity-theoretic analysis, is not convenient, intuitive, or efficient for discussing algorithms. Algorithms are typically designed and analyzed at a higher level of abstraction, captured by the \emph{Word RAM} model with random-access memory and unit-cost operations on $\bigO(\log n)$-bit words. As a result, Word RAM algorithms can be substantially more efficient than their Turing machine counterparts, raising the question: \emph{Can CoT transformers efficiently simulate Word RAM algorithms?} For instance, can they sort $n$ items in $\bigO(n \log n)$ steps or run Dijkstra's algorithm in $\bigO(E + V \log V)$ steps? We answer affirmatively, up to poly-logarithmic overhead. We first establish this for finite-precision transformers with poly-logarithmic width and rightmost unique hard attention, then strengthen the result to two more practical settings with finite width and log-precision: \emph{continuous} CoT, where reasoning takes the form of vectors rather than tokens, and a \emph{hybrid} architecture in which transformer layers sit atop a recurrent (linear RNN) layer. In all three cases, we find that CoT \emph{can} efficiently simulate any Word RAM algorithm with only a poly-logarithmic overhead in $n$. This overhead reduces to log-square when the Word RAM has a ``flat'' instruction set, and only logarithmic for multiplication-free flat instructions -- in stark contrast to known CoT simulations of Turing machines, which require quadratic overhead over Word RAM.

</details>


### [227] [Manifold Bandits: Bayesian Curriculum Learning over the Latent Geometry of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19750)
*Darrien McKenzie,Nicklas Hansen,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将问题采样建模为流形结构化多臂老虎机问题，提出贝叶斯流形课程（BMC）框架，通过层次化任务树和贝叶斯学习引导采样，发现仅优先难度不足以提升下游性能，需考虑任务结构与类型意识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应课程学习方法通常将问题选择视为独立臂的标准老虎机问题，忽视了任务空间的结构化异质性，导致采样效率受限。

Method: 将问题采样建模为流形结构化老虎机问题，利用模型潜在表示空间中的任务关系，提出贝叶斯流形课程（BMC）框架，组织层次化任务树并应用贝叶斯学习指导采样。

Result: 不同采样策略在生产力（学习信号）、多样性（任务流形覆盖）和效用（评估相关性）之间存在非平凡权衡，仅优先难度不足以实现强下游性能。

Conclusion: 在强化学习优化LLM推理能力时，问题采样需考虑任务的结构化特征和类型意识，而非仅关注难度。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is a central approach for improving reasoning capabilities in large language models (LLMs), where training efficiency depends critically on how problems are sampled during optimization. Existing adaptive curriculum learning methods typically prioritize prompts of intermediate difficulty, treating problem selection as a standard bandit problem with independent arms and overlooking the structured, heterogeneous nature of the task space. In this work, we frame problem sampling as a manifold-structured bandit problem with endogenous non-stationarity: problems are related through the model's latent representation space, and sampling decisions can steer how learning signals evolve across that space. To operationalize this perspective, we introduce Bayesian Manifold Curriculum (BMC), a structure-aware framework that organizes problems into a hierarchical task tree and applies Bayesian learning to guide sampling. Empirically, we find that different sampling strategies induce non-trivial tradeoffs between productivity (learning signal), diversity (coverage of the task manifold), and utility (evaluation relevance). These results show that prioritizing difficulty alone is insufficient for strong downstream performance, highlighting the importance of incorporating structure and type-awareness into problem sampling.

</details>


### [228] [Learning universal approximations for partial differential equations with Physics-Informed Broad Learning System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19754)
*Zhiwen Yu,Derong Yang,Liujian Zhang,Kaixiang Yang,Peilin Zhan,Jianmin Lv,Jane You,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无反向传播的物理信息宽度学习系统（PIBLS），通过直接最小二乘优化求解偏微分方程，在保持高精度的同时比传统PINN快1-3个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值求解器虽稳健但计算成本高，而PINN虽无网格但收敛慢且优化不稳定，需要一种兼具高效与精度的替代方案。

Method: 提出Physics-Informed Broad Learning System (PIBLS)，一种无反向传播框架，将PDE求解转化为直接最小二乘优化；改进了算法以高效处理非线性PDE，并给出了通用逼近性质的严格数学证明。

Result: 在线性和非线性PDE实验中，PIBLS比传统PINN快1-3个数量级，同时显著提高了求解精度。

Conclusion: PIBLS为科学机器学习提供了一种计算高效的范式，是实时仿真和设计优化任务的实用高速替代方案。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) play a central role in modeling complex physical, biological, and engineering systems. While traditional numerical solvers are robust, they often incur prohibitive computational costs due to mesh dependencies, whereas recent Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) offer a mesh-free alternative but frequently suffer from slow convergence and optimization instability. To bridge this gap, this article proposes the Physics-Informed Broad Learning System (PIBLS), a novel backpropagation-free framework that reformulates PDE solving as a direct least-squares optimization. We improved an algorithm within this framework to handle nonlinear PDEs efficiently and provide a rigorous mathematical proof establishing the universal approximation property of PIBLS for these equations. Experiments on linear and nonlinear PDEs demonstrate that PIBLS is one to three orders of magnitude faster than conventional PINNs while achieving significantly higher solution accuracy. This framework provides a computationally efficient paradigm for scientific machine learning, offering a practical, high-speed alternative for real-time simulation and design optimization tasks.

</details>


### [229] [An Information Theoretic Framework for Graph Novelty Generation via Latent Mixture Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19770)
*Itsuki Nakagawa,Kenji Yamanishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个基于信息理论的图新颖性生成框架，通过描述长度约束在潜在空间中生成与现有模式不同但保持全局结构一致性的图数据，并理论证明了误分类概率以显式速率收敛到零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图生成方法缺乏对生成样本新颖性与可靠性的理论化、可量化控制，难以在保持全局结构一致性的同时生成真正区别于已有模式的新颖图数据。

Method: 将数据嵌入潜在空间，使用有限混合模型建模潜在分布；基于最小描述长度(MDL)原理，施加显式的新颖性条件（生成样本不被现有混合成分解释）和可靠性条件（对整体混合结构影响可控），从而生成新颖样本。

Result: 理论上证明了在适当阈值选择下，误分类非新颖或不可靠样本的概率以显式速率收敛到零；在合成和基准图数据集上的实验验证了该方法能够实现具有可量化风险的原则性新颖性生成。

Conclusion: 提出的信息理论框架能够以可量化的风险控制实现原则性的图新颖性生成，在理论保证和实验效果上均表现良好。

Abstract: We propose an information-theoretic framework for graph novelty generation, which aims to generate data that are distinct from existing patterns while preserving global structural consistency. Our approach embeds data into a latent space, models the latent distribution using finite mixture models, and generates novel samples by imposing explicit novelty and reliability conditions formulated in terms of description length. Specifically, novelty is enforced by requiring generated samples to be poorly explained by all existing mixture components, while reliability constrains their impact on the overall mixture structure under the Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that, with appropriate threshold choices, the probabilities of misclassifying non-novel or unreliable samples converge to zero with explicit rates. Experiments on synthetic and benchmark graph datasets demonstrate that the proposed method enables principled novelty generation with quantifiable risk.

</details>


### [230] [Flow Map Denoisers: Traversing the Distortion-Perception Plane for Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19802)
*Nicolas Zilberstein,Morteza Mardani,Santiago Segarra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现流映射模型（flow map models）通过学习平均场隐式定义了一个连续跨越失真-感知（DP）前沿的单参数去噪器族，其中前视参数t可作为MMSE与感知质量之间的控制旋钮。对于高斯目标证明其精确恢复最优DP前沿，在自然图像上经验性观察到类似行为，并通过即插即用求解器扩展到一般逆问题，无需配对数据或超参数调优即可匹配或超越专用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像恢复面临失真与感知质量的根本性权衡：最小化误差的方法产生模糊重建，而最大化感知质量的方法产生锐利但不忠实的图像。现有方法要么固定在DP前沿的单一操作点，要么需要配对数据监督、辅助模型或采样器超参数调优才能访问不同点。

Method: 利用流映射模型（flow matching的扩展，用于少步采样并学习平均场），证明其隐式定义了一个以t为参数的单参数去噪器族，连续跨越DP前沿。t作为前视参数控制MMSE与感知质量之间的权衡。对于高斯目标从理论上证明精确恢复最优DP前沿，并通过即插即用（PnP）求解器扩展到一般逆问题。

Result: 在CelebA (128×128)和AFHQ (256×256)数据集上，针对多种线性和非线性逆任务，单个训练好的流映射模型跨越整个DP权衡，匹配或超越两端专用基线。虽然此设置缺乏精确最优性保证，但实证结果验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 流映射模型提供了一种无需配对数据或超参数调优、通过单一模型即可连续控制失真-感知权衡的通用方法，在图像恢复任务中取得了与专用基线相当或更优的性能。

Abstract: Image restoration faces a fundamental tradeoff: methods that minimize error produce blurry reconstructions, while those that maximize perceptual quality yield sharp but less faithful images. Existing approaches either commit to a single operating point on this distortion perception (DP) frontier or require paired-data supervision, auxiliary models, or hyperparameter tuning of the sampler to access different points. We show that flow map models, a recent extension of flow matching for few-step sampling that learns an average field, implicitly define a one-parameter family of denoisers that continuously spans the DP frontier. The lookahead parameter t acts as a control knob between the MMSE and perceptual regimes. For Gaussian targets, we prove that varying t exactly recovers the optimal DP frontier; for natural images, we observe similar behavior empirically. Within a Plug-and-Play solver, the same mechanism extends to general inverse problems, where it controls a tradeoff between perceptual alignment and data consistency. Despite the lack of exact optimality guarantees in this setting, a single trained flow map spans the DP tradeoff, matching or exceeding specialized baselines at both extremes. Extensive experiments on CelebA ($128\times 128$) and AFHQ ($256\times 256$) across several linear and nonlinear inverse tasks validate our findings.

</details>


### [231] [Uncertainty-Aware Reward Modeling for Stable RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19818)
*Licheng Pan,Haocheng Yang,Haoxuan Li,Yichen Sun,Yunsheng Lu,Shijian Wang,Lei Shen,Yuan Lu,Zhixuan Chu,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RLHF中奖励模型不可靠且策略优化会放大错误信号的问题，提出UARM方法，通过分位数保形预测实现校准不确定性，并基于异方差方差分解重加权GRPO优势函数，在多个基准上显著提升奖励模型校准、减少奖励黑客并改善对齐质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF流程存在两个根本问题：(1)奖励模型作为确定性点估计，无法指示其预测何时不可靠；(2)基于组的策略优化（如GRPO）对奖励进行统一处理，会放大不可靠的奖励信号，导致严重的奖励黑客（reward hacking）问题。

Method: 提出UARM框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)基于分位数保形预测（quantile-based conformal prediction）为奖励模型配备校准的不确定性估计；(2)通过异方差方差分解（heteroscedastic variance decomposition）对GRPO的优势函数进行重加权，降低高不确定性奖励样本对策略优化的影响。

Result: 在HelpSteer、UltraFeedback和PKU-SafeRLHF三个基准上，UARM相比标准GRPO和忽略不确定性的基线方法，显著提升了奖励模型的校准质量，有效减少了奖励黑客现象，并改善了下游对齐效果。

Conclusion: 将不确定性量化引入奖励建模并据此调整策略优化权重，是缓解RLHF中奖励黑客问题、提升对齐质量的有效途径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) aligns large language models by training reward models on preference data and optimizing policies to maximize predicted rewards. However, this pipeline faces two fundamental challenges: (1) reward models cannot signal when their predictions are unreliable, since they usually act as deterministic point estimators; and (2) modern group-based policy optimization can amplify unreliable reward signals, as exemplified by GRPO's uniform treatment of rewards during advantage computation. As policies explore increasingly diverse responses, these two limitations create a critical vulnerability: unreliable reward estimates may be granted disproportionate influence, triggering severe reward hacking. We propose Uncertainty-Aware Reward Modeling (UARM), which equips reward models with calibrated uncertainty via quantile-based conformal prediction and reweights GRPO advantages through heteroscedastic variance decomposition. Experiments across HelpSteer, UltraFeedback, and PKU-SafeRLHF demonstrate that UARM significantly improves reward model calibration, reduces reward hacking, and enhances downstream alignment quality compared to standard GRPO and uncertainty-agnostic baselines.

</details>


### [232] [Optimal Deterministic Multicalibration and Omniprediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20557)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了多校准（multicalibration）中随机化是否必要的问题，提出了首个达到极小极大最优样本复杂度$\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$的确定性预测器算法，并将其推广到结果不可区分性（OI）、全能预测（omnipredictors）和泛预测（panpredictors）等场景，解决了多个开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多校准是可信机器学习的基本性质，此前所有达到最优样本复杂度的预测器都是随机化的，而确定性预测器的样本复杂度显著更差。因此，随机化是否是获得最优样本复杂度的必要条件是一个重要的开放问题。

Method: 提出了一种新的确定性多校准算法，达到$\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$的极小极大最优样本复杂度；随后将该算法推广到有限或有限覆盖的测试类上的结果不可区分性（OI），进而得到确定性全能预测器和泛预测器。

Result: 给出了首个极小极大最优的确定性多校准预测器算法；推广后得到确定性OI预测器、全能预测器和泛预测器，均达到最优样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 随机化并非多校准达到最优样本复杂度的必要条件，确定性预测器同样可以实现最优性能。这一结论解决了多个文献中的开放问题。

Abstract: A model is multicalibrated on a collection of group weights $G$ if it is calibrated -- i.e. unbiased even conditional on its prediction -- not just overall, but also after reweighting contexts by each $g \in G$. It is a useful property for many downstream applications and is a basic desideratum of trustworthy machine learning. Before this work, all predictors known to attain the minimax-optimal $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ sample complexity rate for $\varepsilon$-multicalibration were randomized, while deterministic predictors were known only with substantially worse sample complexity. Whether randomization is necessary for optimal sample complexity in multicalibration was explicitly asked by [CLNR26] and implicitly in several prior works.
  We resolve this open problem by giving a minimax-optimal multicalibration algorithm that outputs a deterministic predictor. We then generalize the algorithm to produce optimal deterministic predictors that satisfy outcome indistinguishability (OI) with respect to finite or finitely covered collections of tests. As an application, this also gives deterministic omnipredictors and panpredictors with optimal sample complexity, resolving open problems posed by [OKK25] and [BHHLZ25].

</details>


### [233] [Enhancing Graph Neural Networks Using Proximity Graphs for Dust Source Emission Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19825)
*Maryam Sanisales,Zahed Rahmati,Ali Darvishi Boloorani,Ali Vefghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将邻近图（Delaunay三角剖分、Gabriel图、k近邻图、Yao图）与图神经网络（GraphSAGE、GCN、GAT）结合用于沙尘源排放预测，通过消息传递捕获复杂的时空动态关系。实验表明，基于邻近图的GNN方法在预测性能上显著优于随机图的GNN以及LSTM模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沙尘暴对环境和健康危害严重，但传统预测方法难以捕捉其复杂的时空动态特性，亟需更有效的建模方法。

Method: 利用邻近图（Delaunay三角剖分、Gabriel图、k近邻图、Yao图）作为图神经网络（GraphSAGE、GCN、GAT）的输入结构，通过消息传递机制建模数据点间的时空关系。

Result: 基于邻近图的GNN方法在沙尘源排放预测中显著优于使用随机图的GNN，且远优于LSTM模型。

Conclusion: 邻近图与GNN的集成能够有效提升沙尘源预测的准确性和鲁棒性，为沙尘暴预警提供了有前景的技术路径。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of dust source emissions is critical for mitigating the significant environmental and health hazards posed by dust storms. Traditional forecasting methods often struggle to capture the complex spatiotemporal dynamics of these phenomena.
  In this paper, we demonstrate that proximity graphs enable Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) to effectively model the intricate spatial and temporal relationships between data points. Specifically, we use proximity graphs--such as Delaunay triangulation, Gabriel graph, k-Nearest Neighbor graph, and Yao graph--as the input for GNNs (including GraphSAGE, Graph Convolutional Networks, and Graph Attention Networks) to perform message passing.
  Our approach highlights the effectiveness of integrating proximity graphs with GNNs for robust and accurate dust source forecasting. To emphasize the importance of proximity graph representations, we compare our method against GNNs using random graphs for message passing. The results show that GNNs with proximity graphs significantly outperform those with random graphs and are also far superior to Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) model in dust source emission forecasting.

</details>


### [234] [When, Where, and How: Adaptive Binning for Tabular Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19827)
*Daehwan Kim,Haejun Chung,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应分箱（Adaptive Binning）预文本任务，通过训练自适应的逐特征粗到细课程学习，改进了医学表格数据的自监督学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学表格数据普遍存在，但深度学习应用于表格数据的研究不足，主要原因在于可靠标签通常需要昂贵的人工判定。自监督学习可以利用无标签表格数据，但现有基于分箱的预文本任务采用固定的全局分位数离散化和特征无关的监督，限制了表示学习的效果。

Method: 提出Adaptive Binning方法，核心包括：(1) 训练自适应的离散化预文本任务，将离散化与学习过程耦合；(2) 通过检测性能平台期，逐特征从粗到细逐步细化分箱；(3) 选择表征感知的分割点，同时优化值空间集中度和表征空间一致性；(4) 设计异质性感知目标函数，统一处理类别特征的分类监督和数值特征的有序监督。

Result: 在公共医学表格数据集上，采用统一评估协议，在linear probing和fine-tuning设置下均取得持续改进，无需针对数据集调整离散化超参数。

Conclusion: 自适应分箱是一种有效的医学表格数据自监督学习方法，通过课程学习和表征感知离散化，克服了固定分箱方法的局限，并提供了标准化基准协议以推动该领域可重复的研究进展。

Abstract: Medical tabular data are ubiquitous in clinical research, but deep learning for tables remains underexplored because reliable labels often require costly expert adjudication, even though structured clinical variables are routinely available in tabular form. Self-supervised learning can leverage these unlabeled tables, and recent binning-based pretexts offer a promising inductive bias, but existing objectives fix a single global quantile discretization and apply feature-agnostic supervision. We propose Adaptive Binning, a training-adaptive discretization pretext for tabular SSL that couples discretization to learning through a feature-wise coarse-to-fine curriculum. Motivated by the spectral bias of neural networks and the principles of curriculum learning, our method progressively refines discretization per feature upon plateau detection and selects representation-aware splits to jointly improve value-space concentration and representation-space coherence. A heterogeneity-aware objective unifies categorical reconstruction with ordinal supervision for numerical features, and experiments on public medical tabular datasets under unified evaluation protocols show consistent gains for linear probing and fine-tuning without dataset-specific discretization tuning. We further introduce a medical tabular SSL benchmark with standardized protocols to support reproducible progress in this underexplored domain. Our code is available at https://github.com/labhai/Adaptive-Binning.

</details>


### [235] [Neural Additive and Basis Models with Feature Selection and Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19850)
*Yasutoshi Kishimoto,Kota Yamanishi,Takuya Matsuda,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在神经加性模型（NAM）和神经基模型（NBM）中引入特征选择层，通过训练过程中更新选择权重来降低计算成本和模型规模，使得在保持可解释性的同时能够处理高维数据和特征交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAM和NBM虽然可解释性强，但考虑特征交互或处理高维数据时计算资源需求激增，导致训练变得困难。

Method: 在NAM和NBM中引入特征选择层，在训练过程中动态更新特征选择权重，实现特征选择与模型训练的联合优化。

Result: 所提模型相比原始NAM和NBM降低了计算成本和模型规模，能够在高维数据集中使用双输入神经网络捕捉特征交互，性能达到或超越当前最优的GAMs。

Conclusion: 特征选择机制有效缓解了NAM和NBM的计算瓶颈，使其在高维场景下仍能保持可解释性和良好性能。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) exhibit attractive performance in various fields but often suffer from low interpretability. The neural additive model (NAM) and its variant called the neural basis model (NBM) use neural networks (NNs) as nonlinear shape functions in generalized additive models (GAMs). Both models are highly interpretable and exhibit good performance and flexibility for NN training. NAM and NBM can provide and visualize the contribution of each feature to the prediction owing to GAM-based architectures. However, when using two-input NNs to consider feature interactions or when applying them to high-dimensional datasets, training NAM and NBM becomes intractable due to the increase in the computational resources required. This paper proposes incorporating the feature selection mechanism into NAM and NBM to resolve computational bottlenecks. We introduce the feature selection layer in both models and update the selection weights during training. Our method is simple and can reduce computational costs and model sizes compared to vanilla NAM and NBM. In addition, it enables us to use two-input NNs even in high-dimensional datasets and capture feature interactions. We demonstrate that the proposed models are computationally efficient compared to vanilla NAM and NBM, and they exhibit better or comparable performance with state-of-the-art GAMs.

</details>


### [236] [Physics-Informed Neural Network with Squeeze-Excitation-like Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19853)
*Yun-Fei Song,Long-Gang Pang,Fu-Peng Li,Jun-Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEA-PINN是一种结合Squeeze-Excitation注意力机制的物理信息神经网络架构，通过动态重校准神经元重要性实现稳定初始化，在20个基准测试中17个表现出几乎可忽略的方差和显著降低的初始损失，无需傅里叶特征嵌入或周期激活函数即可在高频问题上达到与TSA-PINN竞争的精度，且可作为轻量级插件模块提升其他PINN方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理信息神经网络（PINN）在处理高频问题及复杂非线性任务时存在收敛不稳定、初始化敏感、精度有限等挑战，尤其是标准FNN-PINN在高频场景下表现不佳，而现有改进方案如TSA-PINN虽针对性设计了可学习频率的正弦激活函数，但仍有进一步提升空间。

Method: 提出SEA-PINN架构，核心是在PINN中引入类似Squeeze-Excitation的注意力机制，对各层神经元的'重要性'进行动态重校准，从而增强网络对关键特征的表达能力；该方法无需依赖傅里叶特征嵌入或周期激活函数。

Result: 在20个基准问题中的17个上，SEA-PINN的初始化方差几乎可忽略，初始损失显著降低；在高频案例7上，相比FNN-PINN取得了83%-90%的改进，与专为高频设计的TSA-PINN精度相当；将SEA-PINN集成到TSA-PINN中进一步提升了42.49%的性能。

Conclusion: SEA-PINN作为一种轻量级即插即用模块，能够增强非线性表示能力，促进更稳健和高效的收敛，提升物理信息学习的整体可靠性，并可作为通用插件增强现有PINN架构。

Abstract: We introduce SEA-PINN, a novel architecture that incorporates a Squeeze-Excitation-like attention mechanism into physics-informed neural networks to dynamically recalibrate the importance of neurons across layers. A key feature of SEA-PINN is its highly stable initialization. On 17 out of 20 benchmark problems, SEA-PINN exhibit nearly negligible variance and significantly reduced initial loss, establishing a quasi-deterministic and favorable starting point for optimization. Notably, without employing Fourier feature embeddings or periodic activation functions, SEA-PINN attained competitive accuracy (83\% vs. 90\% improvement relative to FNN-PINN on the high-frequency case 7) as compared with TSA-PINN-a model specifically engineered for high-frequency problems via learnable frequencies in sinusoidal activations. Furthermore, integrating SEA-PINN into TSA-PINN boosted performance by 42.49\%. These results underscore SEA-PINN as a lightweight plug-in module that enhances nonlinear representation power, promotes more robust and efficient convergence, and strengthens the overall reliability of physics-informed learning.

</details>


### [237] [On the Oracle Complexity of Interpolation-Based Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19878)
*Dongmin Lee,William Lu,Anuran Makur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分段多项式插值的梯度下降法（PPI-GD），通过查询数据域内等距点的一阶预言机构造多项式插值来近似全梯度，在强凸和非凸损失函数下分析了其预言机复杂度，并在数据维度受限且损失函数足够光滑时优于多种GD变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明，ERM损失函数在训练数据上的光滑性可被利用来改进梯度下降法的预言机复杂度。本文旨在利用数据域而非参数域的光滑性，设计一种更高效的近似梯度方法。

Method: 提出PPI-GD方法：每次迭代在数据域等距点处查询一阶预言机，通过构造多项式插值来近似全梯度；将bicubic样条插值的误差分析技术推广到d变量张量积多项式插值场景。

Result: 在数据空间维度为训练样本数的多对数函数有界时，分析了PPI-GD在强凸和非凸损失函数下的预言机复杂度，发现在损失函数足够光滑的关键场景下优于多种GD变体。

Conclusion: PPI-GD通过利用数据域光滑性有效降低了梯度计算的预言机复杂度；所发展的d变量张量积多项式插值误差分析技术具有独立的理论价值。

Abstract: Recent work on first-order optimizers for empirical risk minimization (ERM) has suggested that smoothness of ERM loss functions in the training data, rather than in the optimization parameters, can be leveraged to improve the oracle complexity of gradient descent (GD) methods. In this paper, we propose an inexact gradient method, piecewise polynomial interpolation-based gradient descent (PPI-GD), which approximates the full gradient in each iteration by querying the first-order oracle at equidistant points in the data domain to construct polynomial interpolants of the resulting gradient samples over appropriately sized patches of the data domain. We analyze the oracle complexity of PPI-GD for strongly convex and non-convex loss functions when the data space dimension is bounded by a polylogarithmic function of the number of training samples, and find it to outperform several GD variants in key regimes when the loss function is sufficiently smooth. Furthermore, our analysis extends several techniques from the error analysis of bicubic spline interpolants to the setting of $d$-variate tensor product polynomial interpolants which may be of independent interest in interpolation analysis.

</details>


### [238] [Global Convergence of Gradient Descent for Score Matching in Gaussian Mixtures via Reverse Fisher Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19876)
*Alexander Tyurin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究反向Fisher散度作为评分匹配的替代目标，证明在高斯混合模型上，梯度下降能从任意初始化全局收敛，且优化景观远优于前向Fisher散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前向Fisher散度在简单高斯混合模型中存在初始化依赖的不良收敛行为，需要寻找优化性质更好的替代目标。

Method: 理论分析反向Fisher散度下梯度下降的收敛性，提出新的基于Lyapunov函数的分析方法，考虑高斯混合模型的两种设定：教师为单高斯、教师也为高斯混合模型。

Result: 教师为单高斯时，证明GD从任意初始化全局收敛；教师为高斯混合时，在随机初始化和目标均值Ω̃(1)-分离条件下，每个学生分量高概率收敛到最近教师分量，并给出总变差距离收敛条件。

Conclusion: 反向Fisher散度比前向Fisher散度具有更有利的优化景观，为生成模型和未归一化统计模型训练提供了更好的目标函数选择。

Abstract: The score matching problem is a central training objective in modern generative modeling, diffusion models, fitting unnormalized statistical models, and inverse problems. A standard approach is to minimize the forward Fisher divergence, where the expectation is taken with respect to the teacher distribution. However, recent results show that even in simple Gaussian mixture model settings, this objective can lead to undesirable and initialization-dependent convergence behavior. In this paper, we study an alternative objective: the reverse Fisher divergence, where the expectation is taken with respect to the student distribution. We analyze gradient descent (GD) for fitting Gaussian mixture models and show that this change in the objective leads to significantly better optimization properties. First, when the teacher distribution is a single Gaussian and the student is a Gaussian mixture model with fixed weights and identity covariances, we prove the global convergence of GD from arbitrary initializations. Second, we extend the analysis to the case where the teacher is also a Gaussian mixture model and prove global convergence guarantees under a global random initialization scheme and a $\widetildeΩ(1)$-separation assumption on the target means. In particular, with high probability, each student component converges near its closest teacher component, and we provide conditions under which the student distribution converges in total variation distance. Our proofs rely on a new Lyapunov-based analysis of the gradient descent dynamics, showing that the reverse Fisher divergence has a much more favorable optimization landscape than the forward Fisher divergence.

</details>


### [239] [Matching Markets meet Cumulative Prospect Theory: Towards Optimal and Adversarially Robust Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19883)
*Ananya Kunisetty,Avishek Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了双向匹配市场中基于累积前景理论（CPT）的多智能体多臂老虎机竞争问题，提出了两种改进算法：通过主动选择探索臂集消除主项中对臂数K的依赖，获得更优的遗憾界；以及在对抗性市场下（已知/未知总腐败预算）建立对数级玩家最优遗憾保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习算法在CPT权重扭曲奖励下的遗憾界对最小偏好差距Δ的依赖次优，且当臂数K远大于玩家数N时效率低下；同时，实际市场中存在奖励被对抗性腐败的情况，需要设计鲁棒算法。

Method: 利用累积前景理论（CPT）的非线性α-Hölder连续权重函数建模人类偏好；通过主动选择探索过程中的臂集来消除对K的依赖；针对对抗性市场，分别设计已知和未知总腐败预算下的鲁棒算法。

Result: 原始算法获得O(K log T (1/Δ)^{2/α})的玩家最优遗憾；改进算法消除了主项中K的依赖，在K>>N时达到最优遗憾；对抗性市场中在已知和未知腐败预算下均建立了对数级玩家最优遗憾保证。

Conclusion: CPT能有效建模人类偏好，所提算法在竞争性匹配市场中实现了最优或近最优的遗憾界，并对抗奖励腐败具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: We study a multi-agent multi-armed bandit problem in the competitive setup with two-sided matching markets under a human centric decision making model. To capture human preferences, we use cumulative prospect theory (CPT) that weighs the actions of the agent in a nonlinear fashion using a ($α$-Hölder continuous) weight function. CPT has been widely used in behavioral economics and risk sensitive machine learning to emulate human preferences. We analyze the state-of-the-art learning algorithm with CPT weight distorted rewards and obtain a player optimal regret of $\mathcal{O}(K\log T \left(\frac{1}Δ\right)^{2/α})$, where $K$ denotes the number of arms, $T$ is the learning horizon, and $Δ$ represents (suitably defined) players' minimum preference gap. Noticing the dependence on $Δ$ to be sub-optimal, we further improve this regret by judiciously selecting the active set of arms during exploration, which removes the dependence on $K$ in the dominant term and achieves an improved (optimal) regret guarantees in the setting where the number of arms $K$ is significantly larger than the number of players $N$. In addition, we consider adversarial markets where the observed rewards of the agents may be corrupted. We propose and analyze algorithms for robust markets with CPT as risk sensitive measure in both settings where the total corruption budget is known and where it is unknown, and establish logarithmic player-optimal regret guarantees in both cases.

</details>


### [240] [Towards Graph-Based Deep Learning for Map Generalization: Insights from Building Footprints Simplification and Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19956)
*Yanning Wang,Zhiyong Zhou,Zhouyu Liu,Mengni Yu,Yu Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将图深度学习应用于建筑足迹的简化（节点移动预测）和聚合（链接预测），评估了GCN、GAT和GraphSAGE三种架构，发现GraphSAGE在链接预测上表现较好，但节点移动预测仍具挑战，且聚合任务比简化更复杂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地图综合是制图学的基础任务，尤其是复杂建筑足迹的简化和聚合。现有方法难以充分捕捉空间关系，本文探索将图深度学习引入这两个任务，以推动自动化地图综合的发展。

Method: 将建筑足迹的简化重构为图上的节点移动预测任务，聚合重构为链接预测任务，统一在图学习框架中。评估了GCN、GAT和GraphSAGE三种图神经网络架构在多尺度建筑数据集上的表现。

Result: GraphSAGE在链接预测精度上表现相对较好；节点移动预测仍存在持续挑战；聚合任务比简化任务更复杂、更具挑战性；当前深度学习方法在捕捉高层空间关系上仍有不足。

Conclusion: 本研究为深度学习方法推进自动化地图综合提供了有价值的见解和方法论方向，但数据不平衡和后处理需求等问题仍待解决。

Abstract: Map generalization remains one of the fundamental tasks in cartography, especially for the simplification and aggregation of complex building footprints. This study presents the first exploratory application of graph-based deep learning to both tasks, reformulating simplification as node movement prediction and aggregation as link prediction within a unified graph learning framework. We evaluate representative graph neural network architectures (GCN, GAT, and GraphSAGE) on multi-scale building datasets, showing that GraphSAGE demonstrates relative strengths in link prediction accuracy, while also revealing persistent challenges in precise node movement prediction. Beyond quantitative performance, the results highlight that aggregation poses greater complexity and challenges than simplification, underscoring the difficulty of capturing higher-level spatial relationships in map generalization with current deep learning approaches. Although limitations such as data imbalance and the need for post-processing remain, the study provides valuable insights and methodological directions for advancing automated map generalization with deep learning approaches.

</details>


### [241] [Low-Energy Reduced RISC-V Instruction Subset Processor for Tsetlin Machine Inference at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19964)
*Chanda Gupta,Sanidhya Bhatia,Shaurya Priyadarshi,Himani Panwar,Rishad Shafik,Sudip Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向Tsetlin Machine推理的领域专用RISC-V微处理器架构，通过精简指令集和优化数据路径，在保持可编程性的同时，相比基线RV32IM核心和Binarized Neural Networks实现了高达98%的执行时间缩减和平均29.7倍的能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Tsetlin Machine加速器设计虽性能高，但通常依赖紧耦合接口、微码风格编程和外部主机处理器，灵活性和易编程性受限，缺乏可编程且高效的边缘AI推理方案。

Method: 利用RISC-V模块化结构，设计精简指令子集处理器；通过指令性能分析指导指令缩减，并针对TM推理简化数据路径和控制路径；在多个数据集上与基线RV32IM核心和Binarized Neural Networks进行对比评估。

Result: TM在CIFAR-2上准确率达88.18%（BNN仅60.0%）；执行时间最多降低98%；平均能耗降低29.7倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的领域专用RISC-V架构在保持可编程性的同时，显著提升了TM推理的性能和能效，证明了其在可编程且高效的边缘AI系统中的有效性。

Abstract: Tsetlin Machine (TM) is a logic-based machine learning approach that relies on simple bitwise operations and finite-state automata, which makes it attractive for edge AI deployments. Recent work has focused on co-processor and accelerator designs based on Tsetlin Machines (TMs). Although these designs achieve high performance, they typically depend on tightly coupled interfaces, microcode-style programming, and external host processors, limiting flexibility and ease of programming. In this work, we present a domain-specific RISC-V microprocessor architecture and design flow tailored for TM inference. Leveraging the modular structure of RISC-V, we design a reduced instruction subset processor that retains programmability while targeting improved performance and lower energy consumption for TM workloads. Instruction profiling is employed to guide instruction reduction, followed by datapath and control path simplifications tailored to TM inference. Both the baseline RV32IM core and the proposed reduced core are evaluated across multiple datasets and compared with Binarized Neural Networks (BNNs), which serve as a hardware-efficient baseline due to their reliance on bitwise operations during inference. Results show that TM achieves comparable or higher accuracy (e.g., up to 88.18% on CIFAR-2 compared to 60.0% for BNN) while reducing execution time by up to 98% across multiple datasets. Furthermore, the proposed design achieves an average $29.7\times$ reduction in energy consumption, demonstrating its effectiveness for programmable and efficient edge AI systems.

</details>


### [242] [Kolmogorov-Arnold Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19984)
*Juntian Huang,Jurgen Kurths,Ying Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kolmogorov-Arnold Reservoir Computing (KARC)，将储层计算与KAN相结合，通过显式基函数展开替代传统储层，在保持KAN表达能力的同时实现高效闭式训练，在偏微分方程等基准测试中优于现有方法，并可集成扩散模型进行文本到图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统储层计算虽轻量但难以捕获长程依赖，且存在超参数敏感性；下一代储层计算虽去除了递归但特征维度快速增长。为此，本文旨在设计一种兼具KAN表达能力和储层计算高效训练的新框架。

Method: 受Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理启发，KARC用显式基函数展开替换传统储层，本质上是KAN的轻量化设计，保留了KAN的表达潜力，同时支持高效的闭式训练。

Result: 在包括偏微分方程在内的具有挑战性的基准测试中，KARC在可比成本下优于现有储层计算方法，并能与生成式扩散模型集成用于文本到图像生成。

Conclusion: 该工作在储层计算与KAN之间建立了原则性桥梁，实现了高效且高保真的动力系统预测。

Abstract: Reservoir computing offers a lightweight framework for forecasting dynamical systems but may struggle to capture long-range dependencies due to limited representational capacity. Conventional reservoir computing recurrently uses trainable reservoirs with hyperparameter sensitivity, while the next-generation reservoir computing removes recurrence at the cost of rapidly growing feature dimensions. Here, we develop Kolmogorov-Arnold Reservoir Computing (KARC), which replaces reservoirs with explicit basis-function expansions inspired by the Kolmogorov-Arnold representation theorem. We rigorously show that KARC is a lightweight design of Kolmogorov-Arnold networks (KANs), preserving the potential expressive capacity of KANs while admitting efficient closed-form training of reservoir computing. At comparable cost, KARC outperforms existing reservoir computing methods on challenging benchmarks including partial differential equations. It can also be integrated with generative diffusion models for text-to-image generation. This work thus establishes a principled bridge between reservoir computing and KANs, enabling efficient and high-fidelity dynamical system forecasting.

</details>


### [243] [Activation- and Influence-Aware Ranks (AIR): Function-Preserving SVD Compression for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19993)
*Nico Harder,Daniel Becking,Karsten Mueller,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AIR框架，基于SVD和交替最小二乘法的LLM压缩方法，在<=60%参数保留时困惑度比SVD-LLM(W)提升>18%，匹配其质量所需校准数据减少~90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM压缩中，现有SVD方法仅考虑激活感知（activation-aware）最优，但忽略了反向信号的影响力（backward-signal influence），导致压缩质量受限。

Method: 从SVD-LLM(W)的激活感知最优出发，通过单次闭式交替最小二乘法（ALS）扫描，逐元素集成影响力指标，在单调下降保证下进行低秩近似。

Result: 在<=60%参数保留时，困惑度比SVD-LLM(W)提升>18%；匹配SVD-LLM(W)质量所需校准数据减少~90%；参数节省转化为FLOP、峰值内存和每token延迟收益。

Conclusion: AIR是层局部方法，能与端到端方法正交组合（如AIR+LoRA超越ACIP），在LLM压缩中实现更高效、更高质量的模型压缩。

Abstract: We present Activation- and Influence-Aware Ranks (AIR), an SVD-based LLM compression framework that guides each weight matrix's low-rank approximation with a backward-signal influence metric. Starting from the activation-aware optimum of SVD-LLM(W), AIR runs a single closed-form alternating least squares (ALS) sweep that integrates influence element-wise under a monotone-descent guarantee. AIR is layer-local and composes orthogonally with end-to-end methods: alone it exceeds ACIP, and AIR+LoRA outperforms it further. AIR improves perplexity over SVD-LLM(W) by >18% at <=60% parameter retention, matches its quality with ~90% less calibration data, and turns parameter savings into FLOP, peak-memory, and per-token latency gains.

</details>


### [244] [Connect the Dots: Training LLMs for Long-Lifecycle Agents with Cross-Domain Generalization Via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20002)
*Yanxi Chen,Weijie Shi,Yuexiang Xie,Boyi Hu,Yaliang Li,Bolin Ding,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoD框架，通过端到端强化学习训练LLM在长周期智能体场景中具备"串联点"元能力，使其能在执行任务序列时持续探索、学习并自我更新上下文，实验验证了该框架的有效性及跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在长期部署场景中缺乏持续学习和自我更新上下文的能力，无法在任务序列中积累经验并逐步提升性能，亟需一种框架使LLM具备这种元能力。

Method: 提出CoD框架，包含：(1)端到端RL算法与基础设施，支持交错执行任务与更新上下文的长 rollout 序列；(2)专门设计的任务与环境以激励和评估LLM的串联点元能力。实现了GRPO风格RL算法与细粒度信用分配。

Result: 端到端RL训练在CoD设定下有效，展现出训练域内、跨域以及从CoD到Ralph-loop设置上的分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: CoD框架连接了多条先前工作路线，为推进LLM和AI智能体研究开辟了新机会，并开源了实现代码。

Abstract: This work presents a general framework for training large language models (LLMs) to "Connect the Dots" (CoD), a meta-capability required by long-lifecycle agents: as an LLM-based AI agent gets deployed in an environment, it solves a long sequence of tasks while continuously exploring the environment, learning from its own experiences, and iteratively self-updating its context about the environment, thereby achieving progressively better performance on future tasks conditioned on the updated context. Major components of the CoD framework include: (1) algorithm design and infrastructure for end-to-end reinforcement learning (RL) with long rollout sequences interleaving solve-task and update-context episodes; (2) tasks and environments for incentivizing and eliciting the targeted meta-capability in LLMs during training, as well as for faithfully measuring progress during evaluation. We present proof-of-concept implementations of the CoD framework, including a GRPO-style RL algorithm with fine-grained credit assignment, as well as tasks and environments tailored to the targeted meta-capability (rather than domain-specific LLM capabilities or standard task-by-task RL). Empirical results validate the efficacy of end-to-end RL training in the CoD setting, and demonstrate the potential for out-of-distribution generalization -- within the training domains, across different domains, and from CoD to Ralph-loop settings -- of the elicited meta-capability. Our investigation of CoD connects several lines of prior works, and opens up new opportunities for advancing LLMs and AI agents. To facilitate further research and applications, we release our implementations at \url{https://github.com/agentscope-ai/Trinity-RFT/tree/research/cod/examples/research_cod}.

</details>


### [245] [StreamKL: Fast and Memory-Efficient KL Divergence for Boosting Attention Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20005)
*Guangda Liu,Yiquan Wang,Chengwei Li,Wenhao Chen,Jing Lin,Yiwu Yao,Danning Ke,Wenchao Ding,Jieru Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StreamKL是一种用于注意力KL散度计算的融合GPU原语，通过在线流式计算避免了二次复杂度的分布物化，将显存占用从O(N_QN_K)降至O(1)，在前向和反向传播中分别实现高达43倍和14倍的加速，支持单GPU上的长上下文蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的注意力蒸馏方法需要物化两个完整的注意力分布矩阵再进行KL散度计算，导致O(N_QN_K)的内存和IO开销，在长上下文场景下变得不可接受。

Method: 推导了耦合双分布KL散度的在线计算公式，设计了单遍前向核函数通过片上SRAM流式处理查询-键分块；反向传播时分块重计算注意力概率，避免存储二次中间结果；并实现了专用的GPU内核优化。

Result: 前向传播加速高达43倍，反向传播加速高达14倍；额外HBM占用从O(N_QN_K)降至O(1)，实现了单GPU上的长上下文蒸馏。

Conclusion: StreamKL通过在线流式计算和分块重计算策略，彻底解决了注意力蒸馏中KL散度计算的二次复杂度和显存瓶颈，为长上下文场景下的高效注意力蒸馏提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Attention distillation, which trains one attention distribution to match another by minimizing their Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence, is widely used in knowledge distillation, model compression, continual learning, and sparse-attention LLM training. However, existing approaches materialize both attention distributions before computing the KL reduction, incurring $O(N_QN_K)$ memory and IO costs that become prohibitive at long context lengths. We present StreamKL, the first fused GPU primitive for attention KL divergence that eliminates this quadratic materialization. StreamKL derives a novel online formulation for the coupled two-distribution KL reduction, enabling a single one-pass forward kernel that streams query-key tiles through on-chip SRAM. For the backward pass, StreamKL recomputes attention probabilities tile-by-tile, avoiding storage of quadratic intermediates. We further design and implement efficient GPU kernels with dedicated optimizations. Experiments show StreamKL delivers up to $43\times$ and $14\times$ speedups over baseline methods in the forward and backward passes, respectively. Most importantly, StreamKL reduces the extra HBM footprint of attention distillation from $O(N_QN_K)$ to $O(1)$, enabling long-context distillation on a single GPU.

</details>


### [246] [VIMPO: Value-Implicit Policy Optimization for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20008)
*Zhewei Kang,Aosong Feng,Sergey Levine,Dawn Song,Xuandong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VIMPO是一种无需评论家的策略优化方法，通过KL正则化强化学习的最优性条件推导出策略隐含的价值函数，在数学推理基准上优于GRPO，尤其在竞争性评估和噪声奖励场景下表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法面临简单性与信用分配之间的权衡：GRPO等群组相对方法避免了训练评论家，但只能为每个token分配轨迹级优势信号；而演员-评论家方法虽能提供更密集的学习信号，却需要学习价值函数，带来训练不稳定性。

Method: VIMPO从KL正则化强化学习的最优性条件出发，推导出策略隐含的价值函数。对于自回归生成，该值递归可以用策略-参考log比值表示，并以轨迹结束无未来奖励为终止条件。通过这一推导，VIMPO同时得到了无需评论家的价值损失和演员优势，将奖励整合（通过价值损失）与策略改进（通过PPO风格的演员更新）分离。

Result: 在MATH-500、AIME 2024、AIME 2025和OlympiadBench等数学RLVR基准上，VIMPO全面优于GRPO，尤其在竞争性评估中提升显著。在噪声奖励条件下，VIMPO仍保持对GRPO的持续优势。

Conclusion: VIMPO通过策略隐含的价值优化，在保持无需评论家训练的实践简单性的同时，提供了更精细的信用分配能力，有效解决了现有方法的权衡困境。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has become a central tool for improving the reasoning ability of large language models, but current methods face a trade-off between simplicity and credit assignment. Group-relative methods such as GRPO avoid training a critic, but typically assign a trajectory-level advantage to every token. Actor-critic methods provide denser learning signals, but require a learned value function with its own training instability. We introduce VIMPO, a critic-free policy optimization method that derives a policy-implied value function from the optimality conditions of KL-regularized reinforcement learning. For autoregressive generation, the resulting value recurrence can be written in terms of policy-reference log-ratios and anchored by the terminal condition that no future reward remains at the end of a trajectory. This gives a simple value loss that incorporates outcome-level verifiable rewards without training a critic. The same derivation also yields a critic-free actor advantage, allowing VIMPO to separate reward incorporation through the value loss from policy improvement through a PPO-style actor update. On mathematical RLVR benchmarks, VIMPO improves over GRPO across MATH-500, AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and OlympiadBench, with especially larger gains on competition-style evaluations. Under noisy rewards, VIMPO retains a consistent advantage over GRPO, suggesting that policy-implied value optimization can provide finer credit assignment while preserving the practical simplicity of critic-free training.

</details>


### [247] [Self-Adaptive Scale Handling for Forecasting Time Series with Scale Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20010)
*Xu Zhang,Zhengang Huang,Yunzhi Wu,Xun Lu,Erpeng Qi,Yunkai Chen,Zhongya Xue,Peng Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对时间序列预测中尺度异质性问题（不同序列数值量级差异大），提出自适应尺度处理模块AS，包含尺度校准和尺度选择两个子模块，在保持语义可区分性的同时减少逆缩放误差，在真实基金销售数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测研究主要关注同尺度数据，而真实工业场景中不同时间序列常存在数量级差异（尺度异质性）。尽管这些序列共享相似时间模式，联合建模有利于数据利用，但现有归一化方法要么压缩低尺度信号（全局归一化），要么破坏语义可区分性并放大逆缩放误差（窗口缩放），亟需一种能自适应处理尺度异质性的方法。

Method: 提出自适应尺度处理模块AS，包含两个子模块：1）尺度校准模块，通过神经网络学习校准先验均值缩放因子；2）尺度选择模块，决定是否应用校准或保留原始因子以避免过度校准。

Result: 在蚂蚁财富和支付宝的真实基金销售数据集上，AS模块能够无缝集成到主流时间序列预测模型中，并一致提升其预测性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的AS模块有效解决了尺度异质性问题，在保持语义可区分性的同时减少了逆缩放误差，具有良好的通用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Current time series forecasting (TSF) research predominantly focuses on scale-homogeneous data, where different time series share similar numerical magnitude ranges. However, in real-world industrial scenarios such as financial product sales, different time series often differ by orders of magnitude (scale heterogeneity). Since these series share similar temporal patterns, joint modeling is desirable for better data utilization, yet existing scaling methods either compress low-scale signals (global normalization) or destroy semantic discriminability and amplify inverse-scaling errors (window-based scaling). This paper proposes a self-Adaptive Scale-handling (AS) module that learns adaptive scale factors tailored to each input, preserving semantic discriminability while reducing inverse-scaling errors. AS consists of Scale Calibrating (SC), which calibrates prior mean scaling factors through neural networks, and Scaling Selection (SS), which decides whether to apply calibration or retain the original factor, avoiding over-calibration. Experiments on real-world fund sales datasets from Ant Fortune and Alipay show that AS seamlessly integrates into popular TSF models and consistently improves their performance. The code and dataset are available at the link https://github.com/Meteor-Stars/ASTSF.

</details>


### [248] [Hierarchical Control in Multi-Agent Games: LLM-based Planning and RL Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20014)
*Jannik Hösch,Alessandro Sestini,Florian Fuchs,Amir Baghi,Joakim Bergdahl,Konrad Tollmar,Jean-Philippe Barrette-LaPierre,Linus Gisslén*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种分层架构，使用预训练大语言模型作为中央策略控制器，协调多个专用RL技能策略，在2v2对抗任务中达到与手工行为树相当的性能，且被用户认为更具类人行为表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在多智能体环境中面临稀疏奖励、大状态动作空间和学习协调策略的困难，需要一种能有效结合高层推理与低层执行能力的混合架构。

Method: 构建分层系统：上层为预训练LLM作为中央策略控制器，负责战略性决策和技能选择；下层为多个专用RL策略，负责反应式低层执行。在2v2 King of the Hill环境中与行为树和端到端Flat RL进行对比。

Result: LLM+RL系统与手工行为树性能统计等价（46.4% vs 51.5%胜率，p=0.103），两者均显著优于Flat RL。用户研究中60%参与者认为LLM+RL智能体最具类人性（p=0.027），归因于行为适应性和战术多样性。

Conclusion: 预训练LLM的推理能力可以有效协调预训练的RL技能，实现有竞争力的多智能体协调，并在无需手工规则工程的情况下获得更高的感知可信度。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved strong performance in sequential decision-making, yet scaling to complex multi-agent environments remains challenging due to sparse rewards, large state-action spaces, and the difficulty of learning coordinated strategies. We propose a hierarchical architecture where a pretrained large language model (LLM) acts as a centralized strategic controller that selects among specialized RL skill policies for a team of agents, while RL policies handle reactive low-level execution. We evaluate this hybrid system in a competitive 2v2 King of the Hill environment against behavior tree (BT) and \emph{``Flat''} RL (end-to-end training without skill decomposition) baselines. The LLM+RL system achieves task performance statistically equivalent to hand-crafted BT (46.4\% vs 51.5\% win rate, $p=0.103$) while both significantly outperform Flat RL trained without skill decomposition. A user study ($n=15$) reveals that 60\% of participants perceive LLM+RL agents as the most human-like ($p=0.027$), citing behavioral adaptability and tactical variability. These results demonstrate that pretrained LLM reasoning can effectively orchestrate pretrained RL skills, achieving competitive multi-agent coordination and superior perceived believability without manual rule engineering.

</details>


### [249] [Adaptive Distance-Aware Trunk Deep Operator Learning for Long-Span Roadway Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20015)
*Bilal Ahmed,Diab W. Abueidda,Waleed El-Sekelly,Tarek Abdoun,Mostafa E. Mobasher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应主干深度算子网络(AD-DeepONet)，通过KNN动态构建荷载相关学习域、距离感知特征编码及刚度-informed Schur补全场的物理信息全场重建，实现大跨度桥梁在车辆荷载下的局部化响应快速预测，在保持FEM精度(相对误差<5%)的同时将评估速度提升约60倍(推理速度达4个数量级)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大跨度桥梁在车辆荷载下呈现高度局部化的结构响应，传统有限元分析计算成本高昂(如影响面生成、数字孪生等应用)，现有科学机器学习方法难以准确捕捉这种局部化响应特征。

Method: (1) 采用KNN策略动态构建荷载依赖的学习域，使网络聚焦于结构影响区；(2) 增强主干网络的距离感知特征，编码荷载与结构节点间的几何关系；(3) 引入刚度-informed Schur补全公式实现全场物理信息重建；(4) 使用降阶等效壳模型生成训练数据，保留全局主导行为的同时降低计算成本。

Result: (1) 在基准桥模型和真实Mussafah大桥上验证，相对误差低于5%；(2) 总响应评估时间(含全场重建)较FEM减少约60倍；(3) 排除后处理重建步骤，AD-DeepONet推理速度比FEM快4个数量级；(4) 可快速生成任意车辆荷载配置下的全场响应、影响线和影响面。

Conclusion: 提出的AD-DeepONet框架有效解决了大跨度桥梁局部化响应的高效预测问题，在保持FEM精度的同时大幅提升计算效率，具备在大规模桥梁分析和数字孪生应用中的强大潜力。

Abstract: Long-span roadway bridges exhibit highly localized structural responses under vehicular loading, making repeated FE analysis computationally expensive for applications such as influence surface generation and structural digital twins. Existing SciML approaches struggle to accurately capture these localized responses. To address this challenge, this study proposes an adaptive-trunk DeepONet for localized structural response prediction in large-scale bridge systems. The framework dynamically constructs a load-dependent learning domain using a KNN strategy, allowing the network to focus on structural influence zones. The trunk network is further enhanced using distance-aware features that encode the geometric relationship between the load and structural nodes. A physics-based full-field reconstruction is incorporated through a stiffness-informed Schur complement formulation, enabling predictions at adaptive nodes to be extended to the entire structural domain. To enable scalable training, response data are generated using a reduced-order equivalent shell model that preserves the dominant global behavior while significantly reducing computational cost. The proposed framework is validated on both a benchmark bridge model and the real-world Mussafah Bridge. Results show that the method achieves FEM-level accuracy with relative errors below 5%, while reducing the total response evaluation time (including full-field reconstruction) by approximately 60x; excluding the post-processing reconstruction step, the AD-DeepONet inference is up to four orders of magnitude faster than FEM. In addition, the framework enables rapid generation of full-field responses, influence lines, and influence surfaces under arbitrary vehicular loading configurations, demonstrating strong potential for large-scale bridge analysis and digital twin applications.

</details>


### [250] [When Calibration Fails the Vulnerable Hospital: Federated Conformal Risk Control via Risk-Curve Shrinkage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20115)
*Nafis Fuad Shahid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于收缩的联邦式保形风险控制协议，用于多中心医学图像分割。该协议在保护各机构隐私的同时，通过传输经验风险曲线而非原始数据，实现了对分割质量的分布无关保证，显著优于朴素池化方法和本地校准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦部署中标准保形风险控制方法（池化校准）虽能保证平均覆盖，但会严重损害个体机构的覆盖表现（40%机构违规，最差机构假阴性率超出目标7.8个百分点）；而朴素本地校准虽恢复覆盖但导致预测集膨胀83倍，临床不可用。现有方法无法兼顾覆盖保证与预测集效率。

Method: 提出收缩式联邦CRC协议：各机构仅传输标量经验风险曲线（G个标量）至服务器，服务器计算经收缩正则化的每机构阈值。引入单一超参数n0平滑权衡最坏情况覆盖与预测集效率，通过留一机构敏感性分析确定n0=19。采用拉格朗日直接优化覆盖率预算失败，且有限样本修正项至关重要。

Result: 在FeTS-2022真实多中心脑肿瘤数据（1251名受试者，20个机构）上，该方法实现2.7/20违规，预测集膨胀2.0倍；移除修正项后违规数增至三倍。拉格朗日直接优化会集中风险于弱势医院。

Conclusion: 收缩式联邦CRC能在保护隐私的前提下，有效平衡各机构的覆盖保证与预测集效率，是联邦医学图像分割中保形风险控制的有效方案。边际CRC保证在所述站点混合假设下通过构造得到保持。

Abstract: Conformal risk control (CRC) provides distribution-free guarantees on segmentation quality by calibrating a prediction-set threshold on held-out data. In federated deployments, the standard approach pools calibration scores across sites into a single threshold. We provide the first quantification, on real multi-institutional brain tumor data (FeTS-2022, 1,251 subjects, 20 institutions), showing that this naive pooled CRC protects the average hospital but violates coverage at 40% of individual institutions, with the worst site exceeding the target false-negative rate by 7.8 percentage points. The naive alternative, per-site local CRC, largely restores coverage but inflates prediction sets by 83x, rendering them clinically useless. We propose a shrinkage-based federated CRC protocol: each site transmits only its empirical risk curve (G scalars) to a server, which computes a shrinkage-regularized threshold per site. A single hyperparameter n0 smoothly trades worst-case coverage for prediction-set efficiency; leave-one-site-out sensitivity analysis identifies n0=19, achieving 2.7/20 violations at 2.0x stretch. We further show that direct Lagrangian optimization of coverage budgets fails, concentrating risk on vulnerable hospitals, and that the finite-sample correction term is essential: removing it triples violations. The marginal CRC guarantee is preserved by construction under the stated site-mixture assumption; per-site coverage is validated across four targets with three seeds. No patient-level images, masks, or per-volume scores leave any site.

</details>


### [251] [Multi-Modal Contrastive Learning for Implicit Earth Embeddings via Location Tying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20167)
*Jonathan Hecht,Lukas Arzoumanidis,Ziyue Li,Youness Dehbi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种多模态对比学习框架（MELT和SALT），用于空间预测任务中的自监督预训练。两种方法在四个下游任务中与最强的双模态基线SATCLIP性能相当，但增加模态数量并不能持续提升性能，表明位置编码器本身是主要瓶颈。MELT训练更稳定，为未来扩展提供了更好基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间预测任务常缺乏高质量标注数据，现有对比学习方法通常只对齐地理坐标与单一额外模态，缺乏支持多模态对比学习的框架。

Method: 提出MELT（通过位置绑定进行多模态嵌入）和SALT（序列交替位置训练）两种多模态对比学习架构，利用未配对的地理空间数据将对比学习从双模态扩展到多模态。

Result: 两种方法在四个下游任务中与最强的双模态基线SATCLIP性能匹配；增加模态数量并未持续提升性能；对比目标在训练早期即达到峰值；MELT比SALT训练更稳定。

Conclusion: 当前位置编码器是性能提升的主要瓶颈，而非模态多样性或预训练数据量；MELT为未来的多模态扩展提供了更稳定的基础。

Abstract: Spatial prediction tasks are often limited by a lack of high-quality labelled ground-truth observations. To overcome this challenge, self-supervised pre-training is a possible solution, with contrastive learning dominant for location encoders. Those approaches usually align geographic coordinates with just one additional modality. We propose two multimodal contrastive learning architectures: Multimodal Embedding via Location Tying (MELT) and Sequential Alternating Location Training (SALT). These architectures expand this framework beyond two modalities by utilising unpaired geospatial data. Both methods are technically viable and match the performance of the strongest two-modality baseline (SATCLIP) across four downstream tasks. However, increasing the number of modalities does not consistently improve performance, suggesting that the chosen location encoder is the main limitation - the contrastive objective reaches its peak early, regardless of modality diversity or pre-training volume. MELT provides more stable training than SALT and presents a stronger foundation for future scaling.

</details>


### [252] [Predicting gestational age at birth in the context of preterm birth from multi-modal fetal MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20172)
*Diego Fajardo-Rojas,Megan Hall,Daniel Cromb,Mary A. Rutherford,Lisa Story,Emma C. Robinson,Jana Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发并评估了一套基于机器学习的方法流程，利用多模态胎儿MRI数据预测出生时的胎龄（GA），并进一步将预测结果分类为足月产和早产。该流程在333例对照和93例早产数据上取得了R²=0.13、MAE=2.74周、准确率0.77、灵敏度0.59、特异性0.82的表现。关键特征包括宫颈长度和胎盘T2*值统计量。该工作首次将早产预测从分类问题拓展到回归问题，提供了概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早产与高死亡率和终身发病风险相关，但其复杂的多因素病因导致预测困难，从而难以实现最优护理。现有文献仅将早产作为分类问题处理，缺乏对出生时胎龄进行连续预测的研究。

Method: 构建了一套包含数据插补、特征选择和回归模型的机器学习流程，利用多模态形态学和功能胎儿MRI数据，预测出生时胎龄（GA）。通过分层10折交叉验证评估性能，并进行了消融实验验证流程设计。将GA预测结果分类为足月产和早产，报告准确率、灵敏度和特异性。

Result: 流程在GA预测上取得R²=0.13、MAE=2.74周；分类任务上准确率0.77、灵敏度0.59、特异性0.82。主要特征为宫颈长度和胎盘T2*值统计量。

Conclusion: 快速、运动鲁棒的多模态胎儿MRI技术与机器学习相结合，能够预测出生时的胎龄，为早产预测提供了从分类到回归的概念验证。未来需扩大队列规模以实现在早产群体内的更精细分层。

Abstract: Preterm birth is associated with significant mortality and a risk for lifelong morbidity. The complex multifactorial aetiology hampers accurate prediction and thus optimal care. A pipeline consisting of bespoke machine learning methods for data imputation, feature selection, and regression models to predict gestational age (GA) at birth was developed and evaluated from comprehensive multi-modal morphological and functional fetal MRI data from 333 control cases and 93 preterm birth cases. The GA at birth predictions were classified into term and preterm categories and their accuracy, sensitivity, and specificity were reported. An ablation study was performed to further validate the design of the pipeline. Performance was evaluated using stratified 10-fold cross-validation. The pipeline achieves an R2 score of 0.13 and a mean absolute error of 2.74 weeks. It also achieves a 0.77 accuracy, 0.59 sensitivity, and 0.82 specificity across folds. The predominant features selected by the pipeline include cervical length and statistics derived from placental T2* values. The confluence of fast, motion-robust and multi-modal fetal MRI techniques and machine learning prediction allowed the prediction of the gestation at birth. This information is essential for any pregnancy. To the best of our knowledge, preterm birth had only been addressed as a classification problem in the literature. Therefore, this work provides a proof of concept. Future work will increase the cohort size to allow for finer stratification within the preterm birth cohort. Our code is available at https://github.com/dfajardorojas/ml-for-preterm-birth-.

</details>


### [253] [Recurrent neural networks approximate continuous functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20325)
*Valentin Abadie,Clemens Hutter,Helmut Bölcskei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于[-1,1]上的任意连续函数，存在一个固定权重和固定隐藏维度的单ReLU循环神经网络，通过延长其运行时间即可实现任意精度的均匀逼近。研究引入"带神经单元的图灵机"(TMNU)作为中间模型，并给出了极小极大下界证明运行时间是不可或缺的资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典逼近定理在目标精度提高时需要重新选择神经网络。本文研究相反的可能性：能否一次性固定网络架构，仅通过延长运行时间来提高逼近精度？

Method: 引入带神经单元的图灵机(TMNU)作为中间模型，该模型既保留实现多项式逼近方案所需的算法自由度，又足够刚性以被具有显式隐藏维度和权重界限的RNN模拟。通过TMNU将多项式逼近方案转化为固定网络的时间演化过程。

Result: 证明了对于[-1,1]上任意连续函数，存在一个固定权重和固定隐藏维度的单ReLU循环神经网络，通过延长运行时间即可实现任意精度的均匀逼近。收敛速率反映了底层多项式逼近的速率。此外给出了极小极大下界，证明运行时间在此固定网络逼近范式下是不可或缺的资源。

Conclusion: 固定网络可以通过时间维度的延长而非空间维度的扩展来实现任意精度逼近，运行时间在此范式中是不可回避的资源消耗。

Abstract: Classical approximation theorems ask for a new neural network whenever the target accuracy is improved. This paper studies the opposite possibility: can the network be chosen once and for all, and can accuracy be bought only by letting it run longer? We prove that this is possible for every continuous function on [-1,1]. More precisely, each such function is uniformly approximated by the time evolution of a single ReLU recurrent neural network with fixed weights and fixed hidden dimension. The mechanism behind the construction is a new intermediate model, the Turing machine with neural units (TMNU). This model retains the algorithmic freedom needed to implement polynomial approximation schemes, while remaining rigid enough to be simulated by RNNs with explicit bounds on hidden dimension and weight magnitude. The resulting convergence rates reflect the underlying polynomial approximation rates. We complement the construction with minimax lower bounds showing that runtime is not merely a proof artifact, but an unavoidable resource in this fixed-network approximation paradigm.

</details>


### [254] [Quantum-classical physics-informed Kolmogorov-Arnold networks for PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20326)
*Xiang Rao,Yuxuan Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个量子-经典物理信息KAN网络(QCPIKAN)，基于切比雪夫多项式KAN层和参数化量子电路，通过嵌入物理约束求解偏微分方程，理论证明其可加速高频误差指数收敛并抑制数值色散，在多孔介质渗流场景中优于现有量子-经典PINN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子-经典物理信息神经网络在求解偏微分方程时存在高频误差收敛慢和数值色散等问题，需要一种更高效且物理一致的混合框架。

Method: 构建基于切比雪夫多项式KAN层与参数化量子电路的混合网络架构，将物理约束嵌入训练损失函数，并从逼近论角度证明该设计加速高频误差指数收敛并抑制数值色散。

Result: 在单相流、组分输运和两相流三种典型多孔介质渗流场景中，QCPIKAN在全局预测精度、局部误差控制、动态演化追踪和驱替前缘定位方面均优于现有量子-经典物理信息神经网络。

Conclusion: QCPIKAN为求解复杂偏微分方程提供了一种鲁棒且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: We develop QCPIKAN, the first quantum-classical physics-informed Kolmogorov-Arnold network designed to solve partial differential equations (PDEs). Built upon Chebyshev-polynomial KAN layers and parameterized quantum circuits, this hybrid framework embeds physical constraints into the training loss to enforce physical consistency. Our theoretical investigations grounded in approximation theory prove that this design accelerates high-frequency error convergence to an exponential rate and effectively mitigates numerical dispersion. We validate the framework across three typical seepage scenarios in porous media, including single-phase flow, component transport and two-phase flow. Compared with existing quantum-classical physics-informed neural networks, QCPIKAN achieves superior performance in global prediction accuracy, local error control, dynamic evolution tracking and displacement front localization. This work provides a robust and efficient alternative for solving complex PDEs.

</details>


### [255] [Constrained hybrid modelling to predict microbial dynamics and organic matter turnover in soil systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20329)
*Paul Collart,Juergen Gall,Andrea Schnepf,Holger Pagel,Lars Doorenbos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个混合建模框架，利用宏基因组推断的功能性状数据，通过神经网络预测基于过程的土壤有机质周转模型的生物动力学参数，并结合生态理论约束实现准确预测，即使在小数据集下也能有效学习不可观测组分的动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土壤微生物控制有机质循环，对气候变化减缓至关重要，但现有过程模型难以从基因组数据中获取微生物动力学参数，且基因组与微生物驱动过程之间的复杂关系尚未解决。

Method: 构建混合建模框架：使用神经网络将宏基因组推断的功能性状数据映射为过程模型的生物动力学参数值，并整合生态理论和文献约束以确保模型行为符合现实，即使针对未观测状态变量也保持合理性。

Result: 在合成基因组性状数据集和真实数据上的评估表明，该方法优于多个基线模型，能有效学习过程模型中不可观测组分的动态，且在小训练数据集下仍表现良好。

Conclusion: 该混合建模框架成功解决了从宏基因组数据推断过程模型参数的关键难题，为土壤碳循环建模提供了可行的基因组数据整合方案。

Abstract: Soil microorganisms control organic matter cycling and largely determine how soil systems can cope with and mitigate climate change and environmental threats. Representing microbial dynamics in process-based soil models is therefore critical to predict carbon cycling in soils, albeit highly challenging to inform from data. One promising approach to improve their parametrisation is the integration of genomic data, yet modelling the complex and unknown relationship between genomes and the processes the microbes are driving is an unsolved problem. In this work, we present the first hybrid modeling framework for deriving biokinetic parameter values of a process-based soil organic matter turnover model from metagenome-inferred functional traits based on DNA sequencing data. Our model predicts biokinetic parameters of the process-based model from genomic trait data with a neural network and integrates constraints from ecological theory and literature to ensure realistic behavior, even of non-observed state variables. We evaluate our method on synthetic genomic trait datasets of varying complexity and on real data, showing that our approach improves performance over multiple baselines and learns the dynamics of unmeasurable components of the process-based model effectively, even for small training datasets.

</details>


### [256] [Critical Percolation as a Synthetic Data Model for Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20347)
*Aryeh Brill,Tom Ingebretsen Carlson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于临界渗流簇的分层合成数据集，作为神经网络可解释性研究的理论化测试平台，该数据集具有稀疏性、自相似性、幂律分布和解析可解性，能真实模拟自然数据的分层多尺度结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用于评估可解释性方法的合成数据集缺乏自然数据的分层多尺度结构，限制了其作为真实Toy Model的价值。作者旨在弥合这一差距，构建一个既有理论可解性又具现实结构特征的合成数据基准。

Method: 在嵌入高维空间的临界平均场渗流簇上定义分层函数；利用渗流簇、随机树和加性聚结之间的映射，提出近线性时间算法联合采样随机树及其分层潜在分解；潜在变量模拟分类学层级生成目标值。

Result: 该数据模型具有解析可解性，已知临界指数无需超参数调优；支持任意规模的数据生成；探针实验表明模型的真实潜在变量可从神经网络激活中线性解码。

Conclusion: 稀疏性、自相似性、幂律统计和解析可解性使临界渗流成为可解释性研究的原理性测试平台。

Abstract: Neural networks learn features that reflect the hierarchical, multi-scale structure of natural data. Synthetic datasets used to evaluate interpretability methods typically lack this structure, limiting their value as realistic toy models. To close this gap, we introduce a family of synthetic datasets consisting of hierarchical functions defined on critical mean-field percolation clusters embedded in a high-dimensional data space. The percolation data consists of sparse, low-dimensional fractal clusters with a power-law size distribution. Latent variables modeling a taxonomic hierarchy generate each data point's target value. The data model is analytically tractable with known critical exponents that fix its properties without requiring hyperparameter tuning. We leverage a mapping between percolation clusters, random trees, and additive coalescence to propose an almost linear-time algorithm to jointly sample a random tree and its hierarchical latent decomposition, enabling data generation at arbitrary scale. Using probing experiments, we find that the model's ground-truth latent variables can be linearly decoded from neural network activations. Together, sparsity, self-similarity, power-law statistics, and analytical tractability make critical percolation a principled testbed for interpretability research.

</details>


### [257] [On the Variance of Temporal Difference Learning and its Reduction Using Control Variates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20357)
*Hsiao-Ru Pan,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了表格形式下阶段设置中时序差分(TD)学习的方差，揭示了TD通过有效聚合更多独立轨迹来降低方差的机制。证明TD方差渐近上界不超过蒙特卡洛(MC)估计器，且固定样本量下短视距更新方差更小。进一步表明直接优势估计(DAE)可视为回归调整控制变量，在大样本极限下比TD具有更紧的方差界。最后通过精心设计的环境进行数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解时序差分学习中方差降低的机制，比较不同估计器（TD、MC、DAE）的方差特性，为实际算法设计提供理论指导。

Method: 在表格表示的阶段设置下，从理论上分析TD学习的方差，将其解释为聚合多个独立轨迹的机制；通过渐近分析比较TD与MC的方差上界；将DAE重新解释为回归调整控制变量方法。

Result: (1) TD方差渐近上界不超过MC；(2) 固定样本量下，短视距更新方差更小；(3) DAE作为回归调整控制变量，在大样本极限下比TD具有更紧的方差界。

Conclusion: TD学习通过聚合轨迹降低方差，短视距更新更优；DAE在大样本下方差更紧；这些理论见解有助于指导强化学习中估计器设计与选择。

Abstract: We analyze the variance of temporal difference (TD) learning using the phased setting with tabular representation, and show that one of the mechanisms behind its ability to reduce variance is by effectively aggregating over a larger number of independent trajectories. Based on this insight, we demonstrate that (1) the variance of TD is asymptotically bounded from above by Monte Carlo (MC) estimators, and (2) shorter horizon updates incurs less variance for a fixed number of samples. Beyond TD, we show that Direct Advantage Estimation (DAE), a method for estimating the advantage function, can be seen as a type of regression-adjusted control variate, which achieves a tighter bound on the variance compared to TD in the large-sample limit. Finally, we numerically illustrate the behaviors of these estimators with carefully designed environments.

</details>


### [258] [Train, Retrieve, or Both? A Four-Arm Head-to-Head for Correct Statutory Citation on the Ontario Residential Tenancies Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20359)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了安大略省《住宅租赁法》（RTA）的法定条文引用任务，比较了四种方法：基础零样本、LoRA微调、仅检索（RAG）以及微调+检索混合。结果表明，检索至关重要，混合方法在精确匹配（0.481）和零幻觉方面表现最佳，且廉价的小型检索模型即可媲美更大、更专业的检索系统，增加数据也无帮助。所有结果均为初步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帮助自我代理的租户、房东及帮助台人员准确找到适用的法律条文及正确引用，解决法律文本中“法定条文引用”这一实际需求。

Method: 以Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct为基础，设计四组对比实验：基础零样本、LoRA微调、仅RAG检索、微调+RAG混合。使用精确匹配（节+小节）评分，在小型人工验证待定的真实评估集上测试。

Result: 基础模型无法引用RTA；仅微调会错误回忆条文；检索将幻觉降至零；混合方法得分最高（0.481精确匹配，零幻觉）。廉价bge-small混合模型性能优于使用更大嵌入器+交叉编码器重排序的管道。增加训练数据无提升。

Conclusion: 强法定条文引用性能不需要专业化检索模型或更多数据；混合方法达到了超越基线的标准但未达到0.70的期望目标。所有结果均为初步。

Abstract: Self-represented tenants, landlords, and help-desk staff need to be pointed at the provision of law that actually governs a question, with a correct statutory citation. We study this task on the Ontario Residential Tenancies Act, 2006 (RTA) and its core regulation, asking the operator's question empirically: is fine-tuning enough, or is hybrid retrieval needed? We run a four-arm head-to-head on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (base zero-shot, LoRA SFT-only, RAG-only, and an SFT+RAG hybrid), scored on citation exact-match (section+subsection) over a small, human-verification-pending real eval set. The base model cannot cite the RTA and SFT-only mis-recalls sections; retrieval is essential and drives hallucination to zero by construction; and the SFT+RAG hybrid scores highest at 0.481 exact-match with zero hallucinated citations. Its edge comes from SFT making provision selection more robust to the higher-recall candidate sets that hurt zero-shot RAG. Notably, this cheap bge-small hybrid matches or beats a pipeline built on bigger, specialized retrieval models (a larger embedder and a cross-encoder reranker), and a larger/improved training set does not help either: strong statutory-citation performance here does not require specialized retrieval models or more data. The artifact zeroes hallucination and clears the lift-over-base bar but does not reach the aspirational 0.70 exact-match target. All results are on a small, human-verification-pending real eval set and are reported as preliminary.

</details>


### [259] [Judging to Improve: A De-biased VLM-as-3D-Judge Protocol for Single-Image 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20364)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究将VLM评判器从排序任务扩展到优化任务，通过硬化处理（双模型解耦、位置偏差校正、三种失效模式修复）使其可用于训练。使用轻量级PEFT适配TRELLIS生成器生成家具类别3D网格，发现条件修复在严重退化下达到基线水平（50%胜率），但所有方法均未超过65%胜率目标，表明仅靠轻量级公开数据适配难以超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一种无需人工标注、低成本的方法，利用VLM评判器的偏好来专业化优化强开放3D生成器（TRELLIS），使其专注于特定资产类别（家具）的生成质量。

Method: 1. 将VLM评判器从排序任务扩展到优化循环，进行\"优化级硬化\"：训练评判器（Qwen2.5-VL-7B）与评估评判器（InternVL3-8B）解耦以避免循环性；位置偏差校正；修复三种失效模式（图像过载、几何隐藏splat渲染、无参考评判奖励干净但错误输出）。2. 使用轻量级参数高效微调（PEFT）在公开数据和模型上适配TRELLIS生成器。3. 通过质量对比构造偏好信号（因基线样本本身无学习偏好，顺序翻转率0.94）。4. 探索六种适配方法、两种输入模式和严重度扫描。

Result: 最有效的适配方法（严重降解下的条件修复）达到与基线持平（50%胜率），但没有任何方法达到>=65%胜率目标。胜率在n=8对象时具有方向性。清洁输入使评判器饱和，流DIT微调通过采样器被冲刷，条件修复是影响几何的关键环节。

Conclusion: 在强公开数据基线上，仅靠轻量级PEFT适配无法超越基线。要超越基线需要超越轻量级适配的方法。但本研究的评判器协议是可重复使用的，且匹配基线本身已具有信息价值。

Abstract: A companion study established a de-biased, cross-model VLM-as-3D-judge that reliably ranks single-image-to-3D mesh quality where cheap geometry and CLIP proxies fall short. This paper asks: can that judge's preferences specialize a strong open generator, TRELLIS, on one asset class (furniture), cheaply and without human labels? Taking the judge from ranking to optimization is where the work lives. Pushing a VLM judge into the training and evaluation loop exposes failure modes ranking never triggered, so our contribution is an optimization-grade hardening of the judge: a training judge (Qwen2.5-VL-7B) held distinct from an evaluation judge (InternVL3-8B) to break circularity; position-bias correction; and fixes for three failure modes (image overload, geometry-hiding splat renders, and reference-free judging that rewards clean-but-wrong outputs), with calibration evidence (clear-gap win-rate 0.83-1.0; base-vs-base ~0.5). Using this protocol as an independent evaluator, and working only from public models and data with lightweight parameter-efficient adaptation, we find our methods match the strong base rather than exceed it. Independent base samples carry essentially no learnable preference (0.94 order-flip rate), so signal must be engineered by quality-contrastive construction. Across six adaptation methods, two input regimes, and a severity sweep, the most targeted - conditioner repair under severe degradation - reaches parity (0.50) with the base, while no method clears the >=65% win-rate target. The result is mechanistic: clean inputs saturate the judge, flow-DIT fine-tuning washes out through the sampler, and conditioning repair is the locus that moves geometry. Win-rates are directional at n=8 objects. Matching a strong public-data base with cheap adaptation is itself informative: exceeding it needs more than lightweight PEFT on public data, and the judge protocol is reusable.

</details>


### [260] [CRAX: Fast Safe Reinforcement Learning Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20376)
*Tristan Tomilin,Mourad Boustani,Mickey Beurskens,Thiago D. Simão*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAX是一个基于JAX加速的安全RL基准测试平台，利用MJX物理引擎实现高达~100倍的速度提升，包含6个环境套件和3个智能体任务，评估6种安全RL方法后发现没有单一方法在所有任务中占优，并展示了课程学习和安全迁移的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全RL基准测试在高保真3D物理环境下计算速度慢，限制了大规模实验和快速原型开发，亟需一个加速的基准测试平台。

Method: 基于MuJoCo XLA (MJX)物理引擎构建CRAX基准，利用JAX的向量化操作和硬件加速能力，设计了6个环境套件和3个智能体特定任务，每个任务包含3个难度级别。

Result: 与基于CPU的安全基准相比实现高达~100倍加速；评估6种流行安全RL方法后发现无单一方法在所有任务中占优；课程学习和安全迁移在困难设置中优于直接训练。

Conclusion: CRAX为安全RL研究提供了高效的实验平台，揭示了不同方法在性能与安全之间的权衡，并验证了课程学习和安全迁移策略的有效性。

Abstract: Safety is a core concern for deploying reinforcement learning (RL) agents in real-world domains such as robotics and autonomous driving. While benchmarks have been central to progress in RL, existing safety benchmarks with high-fidelity 3D physics remain computationally slow, limiting large-scale experimentation and rapid prototyping. To address this gap, we propose CRAX (Constrained RL Accelerated with JAX). Built on top of the MuJoCo XLA (MJX) physics engine with realistic 3D dynamics, CRAX leverages vectorized operations and hardware acceleration, yielding up to ~100x speedups over comparable CPU-based safety benchmarks. The benchmark features six environment suites and three agent-specific tasks, each spanning three difficulty levels. Evaluating six popular safe RL methods shows that no single approach dominates across all tasks, and reveals the trade-offs between performance and safety. We find that curriculum learning across difficulty levels and safety transfer can improve performance over direct training in harder settings.

</details>


### [261] [Towards Modality-imbalanced Federated Graph Learning: A Data Synthesis-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20382)
*Zhengyu Wu,Hongchao Qin,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedMGS方法，通过隐式图感知的潜在语义表示合成范式，解决多模态联邦图学习中客户端级和节点级两种粒度的模态不平衡问题，在四个任务上取得高达17.41%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态联邦图学习（MM-FGL）在实际部署中面临两种粒度上的模态不平衡挑战：客户端级（某些客户端缺失完整模态）和节点级（单个节点缺失视觉或文本属性）。现有研究主要针对非图场景或中心化场景，难以直接适配。

Method: 提出FedMGS框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 可用性感知图编码器，防止缺失模态污染局部结构传播；(2) 原型引导的潜在语义合成器，建立跨客户端的语义锚点以恢复缺失模态；(3) 可靠性校准的语义融合机制，在预测读出前调控恢复的潜在表示的影响。

Result: 在四个任务上，FedMGS一致优于竞争基线，最高提升达17.41%，且具有最佳效率-性能平衡。

Conclusion: 将模态不平衡的MM-FGL形式化为隐式图感知的潜在语义表示合成问题，通过在表示空间中直接恢复缺失模态语义，最大化与原始数据语义分布的对齐，缓解缺失模态引起的高方差。

Abstract: MultiModal Federated Graph Learning (MM-FGL) offers a natural collaborative training paradigm, but its practical deployment is challenged by two granularities of modality imbalance. Client-level imbalance occurs when certain clients lack entire modalities, while node-level imbalance occurs when individual nodes exhibit missing visual or textual attributes. While several relevant studies exist, our investigation reveals that they predominantly target graph-agnostic or centralized scenarios, rendering them difficult to adapt directly. To address these challenges, we formalize modality-imbalanced MM-FGL as an implicit graph-aware latent semantic representation synthesis problem. This paradigm recovers missing modal semantics directly within the representation space, thereby maximizing alignment with the original data's semantic distribution and mitigating the high variance induced by missing modalities. To this end, we propose FedMGS (Federated Modality-aware Graph Synthesis), which integrates three core components. The availability-aware graph encoder prevents missing modalities from contaminating local structural propagation. The prototype-guided latent semantic synthesizer establishes cross-client semantic anchors for unavailable modalities. The reliability-calibrated semantic fusion mechanism regulates the impact of recovered latent representations prior to predictive readout.
